# AI Agent Instructions (AGENTS.md)

> **Documentation Map (Version 10.0.0 Architecture & Governance):** این فایل مرجع یگانه قواعد معماری و حاکمیت پروژه است. قوانین بنیادین: `ARCHITECTURE_RULES.md` • نقشه راه فعال: `V10_MASTER_ROADMAP.md` • نقشه راه آرشیو v9: `V9_MASTER_ROADMAP.md` • نقشه راه آرشیو v8: `V8_MASTER_ROADMAP.md` • نقشه راه آرشیو v7: `V7_MASTER_ROADMAP.md` • نقشه راه آرشیو v6: `V6_MASTER_ROADMAP.md` • نقشه راه آرشیو v5: `V5_MASTER_ROADMAP.md` • نقشه راه آرشیو v4: `V4_MASTER_ROADMAP.md` • نقشه راه آرشیو v3: `V3_MASTER_ROADMAP.md` • دفتر بدهی: `TECH_DEBT.md` • دفتر مشاهده‌های سری ۹: `docs/audit/V9_OBSERVATIONS.md` • چنج‌لاگ فعال: `src/data/changelogs/10.ts` (سری‌های ۷، ۸ و ۹ بسته و منجمد: `7.ts`، `8.ts`، `9.ts`) • راه‌اندازی ویندوز: `docs/LOCAL_DEV_WINDOWS.md` • ابزار MCP: `docs/LOCAL_MCP_TOOLING.md`.

هر ایجنت هوش مصنوعی برای حفظ پایداری سیستم ملزم به رعایت دقیق این قواعد است:

## 1. Drizzle ORM & PostgreSQL Rules
1. **NO RAW SQL FOR MUTATIONS:** Never use `tx.execute(sql\`UPDATE ...\`)` or raw SQL with bindings for inserts/updates (prevents `operator is not unique: unknown * unknown` in node-postgres).
2. **Update Pattern (Read-Calculate-Update):** Always fetch row via `tx.select()`, compute mutations in TypeScript, and save via `tx.update().set()`.
3. **Row Locking:** For data updated within the same transaction, use `.for('update')` (NOT `.forUpdate()`).
4. **Explicit Text Casting for WHERE Queries:** For code columns (e.g. `project_code`), cast with `sql\`${table.projectCode} = ${String(code)}::text\``.
5. **Drizzle CamelCase Mapping & Standard Serialization (TD-170):** Express API endpoints for production projects and stages use standardized `camelCase` keys matching the Drizzle schema and typed contracts via `formatProject` and `formatStage` (with backward compatibility aliases for safe consumption).
6. **Atomic Sequence Numbering:** Sequential numbers (vouchers, treasury transactions, document references, project codes — `assignProjectCode` in `src/services/projects/projectCode.ts`, v8.0.80, TD-350) MUST use PostgreSQL Sequences (`SELECT nextval('...')`) or atomic counter tables (`document_ref_counters` / `item_code_counters`, row lock or UPSERT), NEVER `COUNT(*)` or `MAX() + 1`. A sales invoice or sales return number comes only from the server series (v9.0.327, TD-783, product-owner decision ت۹ of package 8): `POST /documents` and `PUT /documents/:id` refuse another number with 422 `DOCUMENT_REF_SERVER_SERIES` (`assertManualRefAllowed` in `src/services/documents/documentRecordRule.ts`, rule shared in `src/lib/documents/documentRefRules.ts`) and the forms show the number read-only and send `auto`; any other document may take a manual number, but one already held in its type and fiscal year is 409 `DOCUMENT_REF_TAKEN` (`assertRefNumberFree` in `src/services/documents/documentRefNumber.service.ts`, never silently replaced), a manual number never moves the series counter, and the automatic number skips numbers already taken (`firstFreeSerial`). The forms send `auto` for the untouched suggested number (`refNumberToSend`), and the audit row and the response carry the stored number.
7. **Module Resolution (`/src/db/drizzle.js`):** Subdirectory services MUST import Drizzle instance explicitly from `../../db/drizzle.js` (or `.ts`) to ensure clean production bundling.
8. **Money Columns are Decimal on the Server (v7.0.67, audit P2-6 / TD-210, product-owner decision):** Money columns are declared with `moneyNumeric` (`src/db/schema/moneyColumn.ts`; done for every money column — accounting, treasury, document lines/VAT/exchange rate, WAC, Kardex, item prices, payroll and piecework (v7.0.68)). Report totals are summed in SQL (`SUM(...)::text` → `fin()`) or accumulated with `FinancialDecimal`, never with JS `+=` on numbers (v7.0.71, TD-210 resolved).. Their value is `Money` (`src/lib/money.ts`, a `FinancialDecimal`): do arithmetic and comparisons with its methods or `fin()`, write with `money(x)` / `moneyOr(x, fallback)`, never through `Number()` or JS operators. API JSON stays numeric (`Money.toJSON`); DTOs typed `number` convert explicitly with `.toNumber()`.
9. **Flag and Date CHECK Constraints (v7.0.73 / v7.0.75, audit P3-14 / P3-15, product-owner decision — no type change):** Integer `is_*` / `has_*` flags carry `chk_<table>_<column>_flag CHECK (col IN (0,1))`; text date columns carry `chk_<table>_<column>_datefmt CHECK (erp_date_text_ok(col, 'iso' | 'any'))` (migrations 0028 and 0030; `*_iso` columns are `iso`). A new flag or text date column gets the same constraint in its migration (regression tests `reg_flag_check_constraints_p3_14` / `reg_date_format_check_constraints_p3_15` fail otherwise). Constraints are added `NOT VALID` and validated only when existing data is clean — never rewrite old rows silently to satisfy them; a repair needs a product-owner decision and records every old value (v7.0.82, TD-231: `legacy_date_repairs`, shown by the financial health check).
10. **Text Date Columns Store Gregorian ISO, Users See Jalali (v7.0.131–v7.0.136, TD-232, product-owner decision):** Every business text date column stores `YYYY-MM-DD` Gregorian (constraint kind `iso`); server timestamps kept as text (`treasury_transactions.reconciled_at`, `project_stages.completed_at`, `users.last_failed_login_at`) use kind `isots` (Gregorian date-time, never Jalali). `journal_vouchers.date` is `iso` too since v7.0.137 (TD-248, migration 0044; vouchers in a closed fiscal year were left as they were and recorded as refused). Write paths normalize input with `requireStorageDate` / `optionalStorageDate` (`src/lib/storageDate.ts`, invalid date → 422); a document's date (`documents.date`, a Tehran wall-clock timestamp) goes through `requireDocumentTimestamp` / `resolveDocumentTimestamp` in the same file (a non-existent day or non-date → 422, empty → business now), its numbering year comes from that stored date, and a draft or proforma moved to another fiscal year takes that year's next number (v8.0.50, TD-313, product-owner decision); query-string date filters use `storageDateParam` (`src/middleware/validate.ts`). The single converter is `src/utils/calendarDate.ts` (`toStorageDate`, `isoToJalaliDate`; SQL twin `erp_text_date_to_iso`). The browser shows dates with `formatPersianDate`, takes today from `getTodayJalaliDate` / `getTodayIsoDate` (display time zone, never `new Date().toISOString()`, v8.0.49, TD-312; the dashboard banner's date, clock and greeting come from `displayClockNow`, v9.0.293, TD-683), fills pickers with `isoToJalaliDate`, uses `JalaliDateInput` (`src/components/common/JalaliDateInput.tsx`) instead of `<input type="date">`, and compares dates as ISO. A "month" in payroll, work reports, treasury trends and the stock movement chart (`monthlyTrends` of `/dashboard-bi-stats`, `getMonthlyMovementTrends`, v8.0.54, TD-316) is the Jalali month. A new text date column gets kind `iso` from day one. Server timestamps (UTC, no zone) go to the browser with a `Z` (`serverTimestampToUtcIso` in `src/lib/serverTimestamp.ts`), and a day filter over them is the business time zone's day turned into a UTC range with `zonedDayRangeUtc` (activity log, v8.0.53, TD-315). The events routes (`utcTimestampResponses` in `src/middleware/utcTimestampResponses.ts` with `EVENT_TIMESTAMP_KEYS`), the notification list and the WooCommerce order log send their server timestamps the same way (`withUtcTimestampKeys` in `src/lib/serverTimestamp.ts`, also used by the workflow routes), leaving stored event payloads, metadata and rule configuration as stored (`EVENT_OPAQUE_KEYS`), and the notification bell prints its relative time from UTC with Persian digits (`notificationRelativeTime`; v9.0.436, TD-725).

## 2. API Response Handling & Array Safety Guard
- **Safe Extraction:** APIs return paginated `{ data: [...], total, page, limit }` or arrays `[...]`. Always extract safely:
  ```ts
  const rawData = Array.isArray(res?.data) ? res.data : (Array.isArray(res) ? res : []);
  ```
- **Guard Array Operations:** Always guard before calling array methods:
  ```ts
  const safeItems = Array.isArray(items) ? items : [];
  const filtered = safeItems.filter(...);
  ```

## 3. Inventory & Stock Management
- **Single Source of Truth for Stock (v7.0.45 / v7.0.48, audit P2-1 / TD-214, product-owner decisions):** Per-warehouse stock lives ONLY in `item_warehouse_stocks` (one row per item × warehouse, CHECK non-negative); a missing row means zero. The `items.stocks` JSONB column was dropped (migration 0021; old values kept in `items_stocks_archive`). `items.current_stock` is maintained ONLY by the database (`trg_iws_refresh_item_current_stock` on `item_warehouse_stocks` and `trg_items_current_stock_from_table` on `items`): application code must never write it — a direct write is replaced by `SUM(item_warehouse_stocks)`. API responses that need a per-warehouse map build `stocks` / `stock_<code>` from `ItemWarehouseStockService.getStocksForItems` (canonical warehouse codes via join); availability / count / transfer checks read the table.
- **Weighted Average Cost (WAC):** Updated ONLY on stock 'in' — including the return of a voided outflow, which comes back at that outflow's own Kardex cost (v8.0.11, TD-254, `applyStockReversal`; a cost of 0 leaves WAC unchanged). If current stock <= 0, new WAC equals the new unit price. An incoming movement at price 0 leaves WAC unchanged and its Kardex row records the current WAC as its cost (v8.0.12, TD-256); a production receipt voucher takes such an item's cost from the document's own Kardex `in` rows (`kardexInCostByItem`). A receipt or purchase line with no net price (free goods: price 0 or a full line discount) is posted at that Kardex cost — debit inventory, credit «درآمد کالای اهدایی» (5204, mapping `donatedGoodsIncomeAccountCode`; v8.0.17, TD-268, product-owner decision); the supplier is credited only with the priced lines. An item without a weighted average cost never enters stock at zero cost: a final receipt, purchase or production receipt (create and finalize, so procurement delivery and project delivery too) refuses a line whose net IRR unit cost is zero for such an item with 422 `INCOMING_LINE_WITHOUT_COST` naming the items, before any movement (`assertIncomingLinesHaveCost` in `src/services/documents/incomingLineCost.ts`; v9.0.453, TD-906 / TD-916, product-owner decision ت۲ الف of phase 5); a zero line of an item that has a cost stays the donated-goods case. WAC and Kardex amounts are in IRR: a foreign-currency document's unit price is converted with the document's own exchange rate (`stockUnitPriceInIrr` in `src/services/documents/documentExchangeRate.ts`) before `applyStockMovement` (v7.0.69, TD-227, product-owner decision).
- **Purchase Line Discount in Cost (v8.0.9, TD-250):** An incoming line (receipt, purchase, production receipt — not a sales return) enters stock at its net unit price, (quantity × price − line discount) ÷ quantity floored at 0 (`netLineUnitPrice` in `src/services/documents/purchaseLineCost.ts`), the same net the purchase voucher posts; a foreign-currency line is converted on the net price without intermediate rounding.
- **No Outflow Without Cost (v7.0.46, audit P2-4, product-owner decision):** An `out` movement records the item's WAC as its cost. If WAC <= 0 the movement is rejected (`ValidationError`) — sales invoices, remittances/waste, project material allocation, WooCommerce orders — except stock-count and Excel adjustments (`documentType = 'audit'`), which are recorded at cost 0. The document price (sale price) or a price-list price is never used as cost. The financial health check values stock at WAC only too: an item with stock and no WAC counts 0 and is listed (v8.0.116, TD-401). A final sales invoice whose gross is zero (free sample, gift) still gets its voucher: cost of sales at the Kardex cost of its `out` rows against inventory, with no revenue or customer row (`syncSalesInvoiceVoucher` writes no zero row; v9.0.270, TD-772, product-owner decision ت۳ of package 8).
- **Sales Return Cost (v7.0.81, TD-230, product-owner decision):** A sales return (`return`, stock in) enters stock at the cost of the original sales invoice's Kardex `out` rows (`documents.return_of_document_id`, `resolveSalesReturnUnitCosts` in `src/services/documents/salesReturnCost.ts`), or at the item's current WAC when no original invoice is linked — never at the sale price. Its voucher reverses cost of sales with the return's own Kardex `in` cost. A return with an original invoice is capped per item at the invoice's active Kardex `out` quantity minus the active Kardex `in` quantity of that invoice's earlier returns (v8.0.8, TD-253, `assertReturnWithinSold`: items then the invoice are row-locked; checked on final create and on finalize; voided or draft returns do not count). An invoice with a sales return that is not voided, in any status, is not voided: `deleteDocument` refuses with 409 `DOCUMENT_HAS_ACTIVE_RETURNS` naming the returns, after the document lock and before any write (`assertVoidHasNoReturns` in `src/services/documents/voidDependents.ts`; v9.0.271, TD-773, product-owner decision ت۴ of package 8). Nor is a document with a live treasury receipt or payment (completed, not a reversal): 409 `DOCUMENT_HAS_TREASURY_ROWS` naming them (`assertVoidHasNoTreasuryRows`, same file); the treasury user moves such a row on account or to another active document of the same party and currency with `PUT /accounting/treasury/:id/document` (`relinkTreasuryDocument` in `src/services/accounting/treasury/treasuryDocumentRelink.ts`, `accounting.treasury`, the TD-501 link rules, bank → document → row locks, audited with `tx`; the voucher is unchanged; the treasury table offers «علی‌الحساب» and «انتقال به سند دیگر», whose targets come from `GET /accounting/treasury/:id/document-options` (`listTreasuryRelinkOptions`, active documents of the same side, currency and party; v10.0.123, TD-1122)) (v9.0.272, TD-779, same decision). A return with an original invoice takes that invoice's currency, exchange rate and net unit price per item — (quantity × price − line discount, floored at 0) summed over the item's lines ÷ their quantity, rounded to four decimals (`invoiceReturnTerms` in `src/lib/documents/returnUnitPrice.ts`, shared with the stock page) — with no line discount; a return that completes an item's invoiced quantity together with the earlier final returns puts the exact remaining net of the invoice on one unit of that item's last line (`completeReturnLines` in `src/services/documents/salesReturnCompletion.ts`, the completion price accepted by the price check; v10.0.232, TD-933, product-owner decision ب); another currency, rate or price, a line discount or an item not on the invoice is 422 (`RETURN_CURRENCY_MISMATCH` / `RETURN_EXCHANGE_RATE_MISMATCH` / `RETURN_PRICE_MISMATCH`), and a field not sent is filled from the invoice (`enforceReturnInvoiceTerms` in `src/services/documents/salesReturnPrice.ts`, on create, on draft edit and on finalize, where a stored draft that differs is 422); the stock page fills and locks them. The stock page finds the original invoice with `GET /documents/by-ref/:ref?type=invoice` and an optional `fiscalYear` (v9.0.326, TD-782): one query for an active final document of that type and number (`findFinalDocumentIdByRef` in `src/services/documents/documentRefLookup.ts`), the type is required (400 without it), none is 404 `DOCUMENT_REF_NOT_FOUND`, and a number with final documents in several fiscal years is 409 `DOCUMENT_REF_AMBIGUOUS` listing them, so the page asks for the year, and `GET /documents/:id` reads only a positive whole id (a number there is 400, v10.0.125, OBS-R1-93); the page shows each lookup error's own message (`returnInvoiceLookupError` in `src/lib/documents/returnInvoiceLookup.ts`). A return without an original invoice keeps the user's price (v9.0.273, TD-788, decision ت۱۰ of package 8).
- **Stock Count and Excel Adjustments Post a Voucher (v8.0.3, TD-255 / TD-262 / TD-263, product-owner decision):** A stock count (`audit` document) is recorded only as `final` — a draft is refused and `finalizeDocument` refuses an audit. Its shortages and surpluses are valued at the item's WAC (an item without WAC at 0, never a price-list or sale price), and `syncStockAdjustmentVoucher` (`src/services/accounting/stockAdjustmentVoucher.ts`) issues, in the same transaction, a draft voucher at the Kardex cost against «کسری و اضافات انبار» (7012, mapping `inventoryCountDifferenceAccountCode`), linked by `source_document_id`. Excel stock adjustments of existing items get one such voucher per import; the new Excel items with stock of one import share one draft opening voucher with one debit row per item at its opening Kardex value (`ItemOpeningService.issueImportOpeningVoucher`; v9.0.206, TD-663, product-owner decision ت۱۰), and the items of every opening voucher are recorded in `item_opening_voucher_items` (migration 0074, legacy vouchers backfilled from `reference_id`), the one lookup for an item's opening voucher (`itemIdsWithOpeningVoucher`). The item list builds the reservation report only for the items of its own page (`getReservedStocksMap({ itemIds })`) and an Excel import reads and locks the items its file names once, in id order (`ImportItemIndex` in `src/services/items/itemImportIndex.ts`). The item opening voucher (`ItemOpeningService.issueItemOpeningVoucher`, Dr inventory / Cr opening capital) is worth the item's opening Kardex rows — quantity × cost recorded at creation (`openingKardexValue` in `src/services/inventory/itemOpeningValue.ts`) — with or without the item workflow, never current stock × current WAC, and rewrites no Kardex row; an issued opening voucher that differs is only listed by the financial health check (`item_opening_voucher_value`) (v9.0.93, TD-481, product-owner decision ت۴). A new item's opening voucher (`POST /items`, when no item workflow starts) and its audit row are written in the item's own create transaction, so a voucher failure refuses the item and its opening stock (v9.0.169, TD-652, product-owner decision ت۶). Two active items never share a code (key `upper(btrim(code))`, letter case and surrounding spaces ignored) or a name (key `lower(btrim(name))`): `assertItemCodeAvailable` / `assertItemNameAvailable` (`src/services/items/itemIdentity.ts`) check the item form and the Excel import, and the partial unique indexes `uq_items_code_active` / `uq_items_name_active` (migration 0070, created only on data without duplicates; the financial health check lists duplicates as `item_identity_uniqueness`) turn a concurrent duplicate into the same Persian 409; a new Excel item without a name is a row error (v9.0.170, TD-653, product-owner decision ت۴). An item's code (the WooCommerce SKU) changes only through `POST /items/:id/change-code` (`changeItemCode` in `src/services/items/itemCodeChange.ts`, permission `products.change_code`, requires `products.edit`, granted to no role): a required reason, the item's version, the code pattern of its type and a free code, under the item row lock, with an `item_code_history` row (migration 0111) and a `CHANGE_CODE` audit row in its transaction; `PUT /items/:id` refuses another code with 422 `ITEM_CODE_CHANGE_SEPARATE`, and the item list offers «تغییر کد» with the earlier codes (`ItemCodeChangeModal`); the site's SKU follows the new code only while «تغییر خودکار کد در سایت» is on (v10.2.15, D-04 part C), else it is changed by hand (v10.1.9, D-15, TD-1264). `PUT /items/:id` requires the item's `version` (400 without it) and writes with the version in its WHERE (409 `OCC_CONFLICT` when stale or changed meanwhile); a field missing from the body keeps its current value, the Excel import advances the version too, and the edit and its audit row (before / after / changes) run in one transaction (v9.0.171, TD-654, product-owner decision ت۷). Saving an item (form, code change, Excel import) moves its code series counter in `item_code_counters` to at least the code's serial under the counter row lock, in the same transaction (`advanceItemCodeCounter` in `src/services/items/itemCodeCounter.ts`; a series without a counter row is left alone), so `/items/next-code` never suggests a saved code (v9.0.172, TD-656, product-owner decision ت۴). The item form's numbers (reorder point, cost, opening stock per warehouse, weight) go through `decimalInput` and are non-negative (`src/routes/items.schemas.ts`): text is 400, Persian digits are read, and `weight: null` clears the weight (v9.0.173, TD-657 item part, product-owner decision ت۹). Counts before v8.0.3 get no voucher. The stock-count sheet (`GET /documents/audit-items`) resolves its location by warehouse code or name like a document (`createWarehouseResolver`, empty = default warehouse, unknown = 422) and the page selects warehouses by code; each posted line carries the book stock the sheet showed (`system_stock`), and a count whose shown book stock differs from the stock under the item locks is refused with 409 `AUDIT_BOOK_STOCK_CHANGED` listing the items, nothing moves and the sheet reloads (`assertBookStocksUnchanged` in `src/services/inventory/stockCountSheet.ts`; v9.0.55, TD-480, product-owner decision ت۷). Every count line carries its count and each (item, warehouse) has one line: a line without `physical_stock` (missing or empty) is 422 `AUDIT_COUNT_MISSING` and a repeated (item, warehouse) 422 `AUDIT_DUPLICATE_LINE`, before any movement (`assertStockCountLines`, same file); the document view (`GET /documents/:id`) keys each line's variance by item and warehouse over the document's active, non-reversal Kardex rows (`stockCountVariances`) (v9.0.324, TD-777). The Excel import never changes the WAC of an item that has stock (v8.0.5, TD-264, product-owner decision): such a row is not applied and is listed in the import errors; a value within 1 rial of the current WAC (`EXCEL_WAC_TOLERANCE`) counts as unchanged and the current WAC is kept; an item without stock takes the file's WAC.
- **Item Excel Import and Price Lists (v9.0.152, TD-647, product-owner decision ت۱):** Only the configured price lists (`pricing_strategies`) are prices: the unified Excel import, the pricing page quick import (`buildQuickPriceUpdates` in `src/lib/items/quickPriceImport.ts`) and `POST /items/:id/prices` / `batch-update` read price columns through `extractRowPriceColumns` / `priceListMatcher` (`src/lib/items/excelPriceColumns.ts`); another «قیمت …» column is a row error and is ignored, and setting a price for an unconfigured title is 422 `PRICE_LIST_NOT_CONFIGURED` (deleting one is allowed). The cost column is «میانگین موزون بها» (`ITEM_WAC_COLUMN`; old WAC headers are still read as cost, never as a price). `filterActivePrices` returns configured price lists only. Migration 0068 soft-deleted the active rows titled «میانگین خرید (WAC)», «موجودی کل» and «میانگین بهای خرید» and recorded them in `item_price_title_cleanup`; other unknown titles are only listed by the financial health check (`item_price_unknown_title`). An import never rewrites a price that is already the only active price of its list with the same amount and currency, and such a price is not counted in `pricesCount`; the price history shows when each row was created (`priceHistoryRegisteredAt`, `created_at` with a `Z`) (v9.0.153, TD-662). Each price list has its own currency column «ارز - قیمت <title>» in the item export, the pricing page export and the import template, and the unified and quick imports read it first (`priceCurrencyColumn` / `priceExportCells` in `src/lib/items/excelPriceColumns.ts`); a legacy row-wide «واحد ارز» is read only for a list without its own column (v9.0.207, TD-841). The import template comes from the server (`GET /items/excel-template`, `buildItemExcelTemplate` in `src/services/items/itemExcelTemplate.ts`): one sample row with the columns the import reads, 10 in each active warehouse and «موجودی کل» their sum, and every configured price list with its currency column; it reads no item and writes no export audit row (v9.0.208, TD-842). The items and pricing pages show each button and price field by the permission of the server route it calls (`products.create` / `products.edit` / `products.delete`, `woocommerce.manage`, `products.edit_price`), never by a role code; the average cost is always shown in IRR, and the pricing page's margin badge and markup buttons compare only rial prices with it (`priceMarginPercent` / `markupPrice` in `src/lib/items/priceMargin.ts`) (v9.0.209, TD-840). Each part of the import needs its own permission, computed in the route with `can()` and passed to `importItemsFromExcel` (`src/services/items/itemExcelImport.ts`) as `ItemImportPermissions` (`src/lib/items/itemImportPermissions.ts`), never read from the body: a new item with its opening stock `products.create`, fields of an existing item `products.edit`, prices `products.edit_price`, a stock increase of an existing item `warehouse.in` and a decrease `warehouse.out`; a part the user may not change is a row error and is skipped only when it would change something, and the Excel window says so before upload (v9.0.154, TD-648, product-owner decision ت۲). For an existing item a missing column or blank cell means unchanged and the type comes from the item unless the row has a type column; the page filter only sets a new item's type, and the code format is checked only for a new item, in the browser preview with the server's patterns (`codeFormatError` in `src/lib/items/itemCodeFormat.ts`) (v9.0.155, TD-650, product-owner decision ت۳). The type cell is read by `parseItemTypeCell` (`src/lib/items/itemExcelCells.ts`, shared by the server and the preview): «ماده اولیه» / «مواد اولیه» / `raw_material` and «محصول نهایی» / «محصول» / `product`; any other word is a row error naming the column, and the preview resolves a new item's type with the server's rule (`newItemTypeOf`) (v10.0.1, TD-1010). Cost, reorder point, weight and stock cells are read by `parseNumberCell` (same file): an Excel number as is, text with Persian or Arabic digits, «٫» and thousands separators through `normalizeDecimalString`, and any other text is a row error naming the column, also shown by the preview (v10.0.2, TD-1011). A row that raises the stock of an item left without a weighted average cost (a new item with an empty or zero cost cell, or an existing item without cost) is a row error naming the cost column and writes nothing (`stockWithoutCostError` in `src/services/items/itemExcelRow.ts`; the preview marks such a new item with `newItemStockWithoutCost`), while a count decrease is still recorded at cost 0 (v10.0.13, TD-1012, product-owner decision of D-01). A row finds its item by code only (`ImportItemIndex.findByCode`: the exact code, else the single item whose code differs only in letter case; several such items are a row error; else the live item that held it as an earlier code, v10.1.9), a name held by another active item is a «نام تکراری» row error for new and existing items alike, and the import never changes an item's code (v9.0.156, TD-651, product-owner decisions ت۳ / ت۵). Stock columns are planned before anything is written (`planStockChanges` in `src/services/items/itemExcelRow.ts`): a warehouse column sets only that warehouse and a filled «موجودی کل» must equal the sum of all warehouses; «موجودی کل» alone is accepted only for a new item (default warehouse) or an item whose whole stock is in the default warehouse, an equal total is unchanged, otherwise the row is refused («موجودی هر انبار لازم است»); a negative quantity or a change in an inactive warehouse is a row error, and a refused row writes nothing (v9.0.157, TD-649, product-owner decision ت۳). Every item the import creates or changes gets one «کالا» audit row (CREATE / UPDATE) with before / after / changes of its fields, `موجودی <warehouse code>` and `قیمت <title>` (`itemAuditSnapshot` / `logItemImportChange` in `src/services/items/itemExcelAudit.ts`), plus one IMPORT summary row, all written with the import's `tx` (v9.0.158, TD-655). The pricing page and `POST /items/:id/prices` write prices only through `ItemPricingService.applyPriceWrites` inside the caller's transaction: it locks the items with `lockStockItems` before reading their active prices, so each price list of an item keeps one active price, an unchanged price is not rewritten, and the audit row is written with the same `tx`; older duplicates are only listed by the financial health check (`item_price_duplicate_active`) (v9.0.175, TD-660). A price is a decimal above zero (Persian digits accepted) in a currency of the §6 list (`parsePriceAmount` / `priceCurrencyOf` in `src/lib/items/priceInput.ts`, shared by the routes, the Excel import, the quick import and the pricing page): `POST /items/:id/prices` and `batch-update` refuse anything else with 400, `batch-update` removes a price only with `remove: true` (the pricing page sends it for an empty field and never sends an invalid amount), and an invalid price or currency refuses the whole Excel row; older rows are only listed by the financial health check (`item_price_invalid_amount_currency`) (v9.0.176, TD-657, product-owner decision ت۹).
- **Stock Movement Date and Kardex Replay (v8.0.4, TD-257 / TD-258, product-owner decision):** The date of a stock movement (document create, finalize, warehouse transfer) may not be earlier — by day — than the item's last ledger movement (rows of voided documents and their reversals do not count, so void + reissue with the original date stays possible). The only exception is the permission `warehouse.backdate` (granted to no role by default), computed in the route with `userHasRoleOrPermission` and passed as `allowBackdate` (never read from the request body); a workflow transition whose domain action moves stock (document approval, requisition receipt; route, inbox or procurement) reads it in `executeTransition` from the signer's own role permissions, for a deputy the delegator's role, the system admin always, unless the caller passed `allowBackdate: true` (`STOCK_BACKDATE_PERMISSION` in `src/lib/permissions/documentPermissions.ts`; v9.0.456, TD-928, product-owner decision ت۳ of phase 5); a backdated `out` is then accepted only when the warehouse's balance in (day, id) order stays non-negative from that date on (`assertStockMovementDate` in `src/services/inventory/stockMovementDate.ts`, called by `applyStockMovement` and the transfer service). No stock movement may be dated after the business today, for any user (422 `STOCK_MOVEMENT_FUTURE_DATE`, same function; v9.0.79, TD-483, product-owner decision); future-dated Kardex rows recorded earlier are never rewritten and are listed by the financial health check (`stock_future_movements`, `src/services/inventory/futureStockMovements.ts`), and a transfer date goes through `requireStorageDate`. The Kardex rebuild replays WAC with `replayKardexWac` (`src/services/inventory/kardexReplay.ts`): registration (id) order and exactly the live engine's formulas (voided rows in place, reversal via the `applyStockReversal` formula, transfers quantity-only), starting from WAC 0 — the item's current WAC is kept only when the replay reaches no positive WAC (v8.0.13, TD-269); the I13 invariant uses the same function. The rebuild («بازسازی موجودی از کاردکس») only rebuilds quantities and reports a WAC that differs from the replay (`replayWac`, `wacDifferences`); setting the WAC to the replay is the separate «اصلاح بهای میانگین» (`POST /inventory/correct-wac`, permission `inventory.wac_correct`, granted to no role by default), allowed only when the stock equals the Kardex balance, which issues in the same transaction a draft voucher for stock × (replay − old WAC) against 7012 (`correctItemWacFromLedger`, `src/services/accounting/wacCorrectionVoucher.ts`; v9.0.90, TD-487, product-owner decision ت۳). The initial Kardex backfill («ثبت موجودی اولیه کاردکس», `KardexBackfillService`) only adds rows for items with stock and no incoming Kardex row and never reprices an existing row (v9.0.92, TD-488). Voiding a document whose incoming rows were consumed by later-dated outflows is refused (v8.0.6, TD-265, product-owner decision): `assertVoidKeepsStockHistory` (`src/services/inventory/voidStockHistory.ts`, called first in `deleteDocument`) rejects a void that would make a warehouse's (day, id) balance negative and names the consuming documents; voiding an outflow is never refused. Voiding a receipt that empties the item's stock or leaves a negative remaining value keeps the WAC, and the void posts in its own transaction a draft rial voucher for the value left in the inventory accounts against «کسری و اضافات انبار» (7012), reference `VOID-RESIDUE-<number>` (`postVoidResidueVoucher` in `src/services/accounting/voidResidueVoucher.ts`; v10.0.84, TD-1147, product-owner decision ت۱۵).
- **Project Reservation Deduction (v7.0.102, TD-233, product-owner decision «کسر در سرور»):** A final `out` document with `projectId` deducts the project's reservation inside its own create/finalize transaction (`releaseReservationsForDocument` → `ItemStockReservationService.releaseProjectReservations`, project row lock + OCC); a failed deduction rejects the document, drafts deduct nothing. Each line is deducted across all matching reservation rows, from the quantity field the reservation report reads (`reservationQtyField`). The browser never writes `inventory_control` for this. Voiding/deleting such a document restores the same quantities to the same project in the void transaction (v7.0.105, TD-237, product-owner decision «برگردد»): each deduction is recorded in `project_reservation_releases` and `deleteDocument` locks the project before the document and calls `restoreReservationsForDocument`; deductions before v7.0.105 have no record and restore nothing. A material allocation (both paths) deducts the project's reservation the same way in its own transaction and records each deduction with its allocation id (`releaseReservationsForAllocation`; `project_reservation_releases.bom_allocation_id`, migration 0095, one source per row by `chk_project_reservation_releases_source`); releasing the allocation locks the project before the allocation row and restores exactly those quantities (`restoreReservationsForAllocation`); allocations before v9.0.452 restore nothing (v9.0.452, TD-918). Only a remittance or waste of the project consumes its reservation and passes the sellable gate with the project's own reservation free (`consumesProjectReservation` in `src/lib/transactions/projectIssueTypes.ts`); a sales invoice with a `projectId` neither deducts it nor sells it (v10.0.207, TD-947). Finalizing a project again reserves its need minus what its live final remittances and waste and its open or consumed allocations already issued (`projectIssuedQuantities` in `src/services/projects/projectIssuedQuantities.ts`, v10.0.206, TD-945), and the procurement order of a project requisition carries `documents.project_id` (v10.0.208, TD-946); all three are product-owner decision ت۱۰ of phase 5. The reservation itself (`inventory_control.reservedItems`) is written only by the server (v8.0.66, TD-306, product-owner decision): project create/update ignore `reservedItems` / `isReserved` / `finalizedAt` from the body; finalizing (`isFinalized` false → true) builds the reservation from the inventory control sections and item stock (`buildProjectReservation` in `src/lib/projects/projectReservation.ts`, via `resolveServerInventoryControl`); a section in the preset shape (materials in `items`) is turned into project rows before it is saved or reserved, in the project inventory tab and on the server: per product for a code-by-code check, one global row otherwise, each material required 1 with its stock status (`projectSectionsFromPreset` in `src/lib/projects/inventoryControlSections.ts`; v9.0.413, TD-748), saving a finalized project keeps its current (possibly deducted) reservation, and unfinalizing clears it; the project row is locked during the update. A material row of the inventory control sections, the purchase list or the reservation finds its warehouse item only through `findProjectItemMatch` (`src/lib/projects/projectItemMatch.ts`, shared by the server and the project screens): the warehouse item id, then the code key `upper(btrim)`, then the exact name key `lower(btrim)`; never a substring, never an empty code or name (v9.0.363, TD-749 / TD-768). A project's progress matrix (item × stage) has one rule, `computeProgressMatrix` / `matrixProducts` in `src/lib/projects/progressMatrix.ts` (the project's `products`, else its main item; optional stages from `selected_optional_stages`), loaded by `loadProjectProgressMatrix`: the matrix view, the ticks, the completion check (422 `PROJECT_MATRIX_INCOMPLETE`, inside the project save or delivery transaction) and the status sync all read it (v9.0.364, TD-739). A cell also carries the done work of its workshop schedule rows (`work`: each row's live linked work log of the project up to the row's quantity, rows of one unit added and several units averaged by share; `workProgressOf` in `src/lib/workshop/scheduleWorkProgress.ts`, which the workshop panel reads too; a single-item project's `prod-main` rows reach its main item), and a product's and a stage's percent are the average share of their cells, a ticked cell whole, any work at least 1, 100 only when every cell is ticked (`cellsPercent`); completion still reads the ticks only, and a cell of several units carries no quantities. Writing, editing the quantity of, deleting or moving a schedule-linked work log syncs the stage percents in the log's transaction (`syncScheduleProgress`, registered by the composition root), but never decides a project's course: a completed, cancelled or paused project is left alone, a completed stage keeps its percent, status and time, a blocked stage stays blocked (a later tick of another cell keeps it blocked too; only a tick of its own cells unblocks it), and only a waiting stage or a planned project starts, so a stage started by work stays started at 0% when that log is deleted, until the next tick save (`workSyncHoldsProject` / `workSyncStageStatus` / `workSyncProjectStatus` in `src/lib/projects/projectStatus.ts`); the workshop panel reads below 100 until the stage is complete, and the desktop matrix takes no tick while a save runs (v10.2.11, TD-1340, product-owner decision ت۴۲ الف). Reading a project never writes it: the stage and project status sync (`syncProjectFromMatrix` in `src/services/projects/projectStatusSync.ts`) runs only inside the matrix tick, stage add / edit / delete and project edit transactions, after the project row lock (`lockLiveProject`); it never changes a `paused` or `cancelled` project (`matrixProjectStatus` in `src/lib/projects/projectStatus.ts`) and writes a «پروژه تولید» audit row with the status before and after (v9.0.365, TD-738, product-owner decision ت۱ of package 11). In a project with a progress matrix (`hasMatrixProducts`) a stage's status and percent come only from the matrix: a different manual value is 422 `STAGE_STATUS_FROM_MATRIX`, and the stage form asks for them only in a project without products (`StageStatusField`; v9.0.366, TD-758). A new stage takes one more than every number the project has used, its deleted stages and progress rows included (`nextStageOrder`, under the project row lock); deleting a stage soft-deletes its progress rows and a tick needs a live stage; migration 0084 adds the partial unique index `uq_project_stages_order_active` (project, number) and the foreign key `fk_project_stages_project`, each only on clean data, and the financial health check lists what is left (`project_stage_integrity`) (v9.0.367, TD-737 / TD-753). A stage edit reads its number (a whole number 1 to `MAX_STAGE_ORDER`) and percent (a whole number 0 to 100) with `decimalInput`; a number another live stage holds, or one a deleted stage left progress rows on, is 409 `STAGE_ORDER_TAKEN`, and a renumbered stage moves its live progress rows (`moveStageOrder`; v9.0.368, TD-755). A project status, a project priority and a stage status (initial stages included) come only from `PROJECT_STATUSES`, `PROJECT_PRIORITIES` and `STAGE_STATUSES` (`src/lib/projects/projectStatus.ts`, shared with the forms; anything else is 400), and legacy free-text values are only listed by the financial health check (`project_status_values`) (v9.0.380, TD-754). The delivery quantity and unit price and the project quantity go through `decimalInput`; a delivery line without an item, with a quantity not above zero or a negative price refuses the whole delivery with 422 `PROJECT_DELIVERY_LINE_INVALID` naming the row (`deliveryLine` in `src/services/projects.service.ts`), never a silent skip, and a project quantity not above zero is 422 `PROJECT_QUANTITY_INVALID` (v9.0.381, TD-741). A stage completion time and a matrix tick time are the server UTC time (`systemNowUtcIso`) on every path (`stageCompletedAt` in `src/services/projects/projectStatusSync.ts`): a completed stage keeps its time and a stage leaving «completed» has none (v9.0.382, TD-756). Every project audit row is written in `src/services/projects/projectAudit.ts` with the write's `tx`: a project edit with before and after of the changed fields, and each stage add, edit and delete under the retained entity «مرحله پروژه تولید» with the project id and the stage before and after (v9.0.383, TD-757). A project edit never changes its stages: the edit form shows them read-only with a button to the stages section, sends no `initial_stages`, and `PUT /projects/:id` refuses them with 422 `PROJECT_STAGES_READ_ONLY`; stages change only through the stage routes (v9.0.384, TD-740, product-owner decision ت۲). `PUT /projects/:id` requires the project's `version` (400 without it) and refuses a stale one under the project row lock with 409 `OCC_CONFLICT` and «پروژه را کاربر دیگری تغییر داده؛ دوباره باز کنید.» (`assertProjectVersion` in `src/services/projects/projectVersion.ts`); every save, a status change by the progress matrix and the completion at delivery raise the version, and the edit form and the project tabs send the version they were built from and remember each save's answer (`useProjectVersion`) (v9.0.385, TD-742, product-owner decision ت۳). A production project is a page, `/projects/<id>/<tab>` (`ProjectDetailPage`; five tabs in `src/lib/projects/projectWorkspaceTabs.ts`, the earlier window's tab names still read and replaced): its step bar reads the record and `GET /projects/:id/steps` (`loadProjectStepCounts` in `src/services/projects/projectSteps.ts`: requisitions, allocations, live final remittances and waste with a live line (`projectIssueLineConditions`, the rows the issued quantities read; v10.2.3), deliveries; counts only, never an amount), its specs are edited on the page (`?edit=1` only opens the editor and is dropped from the address at once, so the editor adds no history entry), a stage's personnel come from the personnel list, and a tab with an unsaved change asks before another tab, the list, an in-app link, the browser's back button (an extra history entry of the same address), the global search, a notification or closing the browser tab (`useUnsavedChangesGuard` / `confirmLeaveIfDirty` in `src/hooks/useUnsavedChangesGuard.ts`; code that navigates elsewhere awaits `confirmLeaveIfDirty()` and navigates with `leaveNavigateOptions()`) (v10.2.1, 10.2 package 2). Narrower than 768 px (`usePhoneWidth`) the page has a phone layout: a short header (`ProjectPhoneHeader`), the summary of the project instead of the first tab (`ProjectPhoneSummary`: progress, customer, delivery date with `projectDueNote`, current step and the open steps of `pendingProjectSteps`, stage statuses) and one card per product with a 44 px button per stage instead of the progress matrix (`ProjectPhoneProgress`); the desktop table and the phone list read, tick and save through one hook, `useProductProgressTicks` with the rules of `src/lib/projects/progressTicks.ts`, so both send the same `PUT /projects/:id/product-progress`; «نمای کامل مشخصات و مراحل» shows the desktop layout, a layout holding an unsaved change or a running tick save is kept when the screen width changes, and «نمای کامل» asks only before it would drop unsaved ticks, and the phone bottom bar has «پروژه‌ها» for `projects.view`, with at most `MOBILE_NAV_MAX_ITEMS` (5) page buttons beside «فهرست» in `MOBILE_NAV_ITEMS` order (v10.2.9, 10.2 package 10, TD-1310).
- **Stock Reservations (package 7):** Only a sales proforma reserves stock: type `invoice` or `proforma` in status `proforma` (`RESERVING_DOCUMENT_TYPES` / `isReservingDocument` in `src/lib/documents/reservingDocuments.ts`); a purchase proforma (a `receipt` in `proforma` status), a receipt, purchase, remittance and any draft reserve nothing, and a sales proforma does not expire (v9.0.370, TD-818, product-owner decision ت۱ of package 7). A project reserves only the stored `inventory_control.reservedItems` of a finalized project (`isFinalized === true`; `reservingProjectRows` in `src/lib/projects/projectReservationState.ts`, shared with the stock document page): no reader rebuilds a reservation from the sections, the purchase list or the manual items, so a draft or unfrozen project reserves nothing and a consumed reservation stays consumed; legacy projects that disagree (finalized before the server wrote reservations, or not finalized with a stored reservation) are only listed by the financial health check (`project_reservation_integrity`, `src/services/projects/projectReservationHealth.ts`) (v9.0.371, TD-817, decision ت۲). Finalizing converts each control row to the item unit before summing per matched item (`planProjectReservation` in `src/lib/projects/projectReservation.ts`): a row whose unit (trimmed, case-insensitive; empty is «عدد», as the form shows) differs from the item's is divided by its own `conversionRate` when the rate is positive and `convertedUnit` is empty or the item unit — the rounded `convertedQty` is never read or stored — and any other row in another unit refuses the finalize with 422 `PROJECT_RESERVATION_UNIT_MISMATCH`; the conversion form keeps a directly entered rate unrounded (`directConversionRate`), and legacy unconverted reservations are listed by the same health check (v9.0.372, TD-820, decision ت۴). Finalizing reserves per item min(need, total stock − the reservations of others) (first come, first served): under the transaction advisory lock `PROJECT_RESERVATION_FINALIZE` (91011, taken after the project row lock) the server reads the others' reservations of the project's items only (a read error refuses the finalize) and writes each item reserved below its need to `inventory_control.reservationShortages` (ignored in the body, cleared on unfreeze), which the response carries and the purchase tab shows (`ReservationShortageList`); items reserved above their stock are only listed by the financial health check (`stock_reservation_exceeds_stock`), since a stock count or a voided receipt may still lower stock below a reservation (v9.0.373, TD-819, decision ت۳). The sales invoice item picker of an open proforma being edited asks `GET /items/options?reserved=1&excludeDocumentId=<id>`, so «رزرو» leaves out that proforma's own reservation like the sellable gate (`itemPickWithReservedUrl`, `othersReservations`; v10.1.9, TD-1209). The reservation summaries, `getReservedStocksMap` and the item list key a reservation by item id, never by the upper-cased code, and a project reservation row finds its item by id before code and name (v9.0.374, TD-822). Every reservation reader fails closed (`getReservedStockDetails` takes only `throwOnError: true`): the item list, the online shop sync and the reservation report pass a read error on and never show zero reservations, and the report page shows an error panel; a stored project row is read as text (`rowText`), and a row that reaches no live item reserves nothing and is listed by the financial health check (`reservation_row_unmatched`, `unmatchedProjectReservationRows`) (v9.0.394, TD-821). The reservation report reads only what it is asked for: the sales proforma lines in one joined query (scoped to the asked items), only active finalized projects with stored rows, and only the items those rows name by id, code key or name key, never every item; a scoped read keeps only the project rows that may reach its items, and the online shop sync is scoped to the items it pushes (v9.0.395, TD-831). Every reservation in the report is valued at the item's cost in IRR, the reservation quantity × the item's weighted average cost (`unitCost` / `totalCost`, summed as `totalReservedCost`), for a sales proforma in any currency and for a project alike; the proforma's sale price is never reported (contract `src/lib/inventory/reservedItemsReport.ts`, shared with the report page) (v9.0.399, TD-823, product-owner decision ت۶). `GET /inventory/reserved-items` sends the report as its reader may see it (`reservedItemsReportForAccess`, same file, with `access`): cost and value only to item cost readers (`ITEM_COST_READ_PERMISSIONS`: `products.view`, `products.edit_price`, every `warehouse.*` and `accounting.*` key, P05 decision ت۱۰-۲) and a proforma's buyer only to `documents.view`; other readers get quantities and sources, and the page hides the cost column (v9.0.400, TD-829). The reorder alert page filters by stock status «همه», «بی موجودی» or «زیر نقطه سفارش» (stock above zero, up to the reorder point; `stockStatusOf` / `STOCK_STATUS_FILTER_LABELS` in `src/lib/reorderAlerts/reorderItems.ts`) (v9.0.401, TD-827, product-owner decision ت۸). The reserved items report's «خروجی اکسل» writes an xlsx file «اقلام-رزروشده-<Jalali date>.xlsx» with the `xlsx` library, its cost columns labelled in rial and only for cost readers (`reservedItemSummaryRows` / `reservationEntryRows` / `reservedItemsExportFileName` in `src/lib/inventory/reservedItemsExport.ts`); a proforma's link opens the documents list searched by its number, which reads `?search=` from the address (`invoiceListSearchFromAddress`), and a project's link its inventory control page (`reservationSourcePath`) (v9.0.402, TD-828, product-owner decision ت۸). The reorder alert's purchase form records a final receipt with a line at price zero only after «کالای اهدایی» is ticked, and never for an item without a weighted average cost, which would enter stock without cost and block every outflow (`reorderReceiptPriceError` in `src/lib/reorderAlerts/reorderPurchaseReceipt.ts`); its destination warehouse comes from the active warehouses and its date from `JalaliDateInput` in ISO (`ReorderOrderFields`) (v9.0.403, TD-830). The stock-issue reorder monitor (`reorderAlertForIssue` in `src/services/events/domainEventHandlers.ts`) compares the same free stock and carries it as `freeStock` (v10.0.43, TD-937). The reorder alert (`GET /items/reorder-alerts`, `listReorderAlerts` in `src/services/items/reorderAlerts.service.ts`) compares free stock — stock minus the reservations of `getReservedStocksMap`, whose read error refuses the request — with the reorder point; the deficit and «بی موجودی» come from free stock, and the quantity on open purchase documents (receipt or purchase in draft or proforma, `inTransitQuantities`) is only shown as «در راه» (v9.0.404, TD-843, product-owner decision ت۷).
- **Raw Material Request Queue (package 7):** The review window of «مواد اولیه در انتظار تأیید» sends one form for «ذخیره فقط تغییرات» and «تأیید و افزودن به انبار» (`PendingMaterialForm` in `src/lib/pendingMaterials/pendingMaterialForm.ts`), whose keys are those of the route schemas (`src/routes/pendingMaterials.schemas.ts`); the approve and edit bodies are `strict`, so any other key is 400 instead of being dropped (v9.0.396, TD-824). Sending a request needs `pending_materials.create` (requires `pending_materials.view`; migration 0087 gave both to every role holding `products.create`); its numbers go through non-negative `decimalInput`, an inline image becomes a file through `uploadBase64ToStorage` and a request without a code stays without one (no `TEMP-` code). Approving, rejecting, editing and deleting a request each run in one transaction under its row lock `FOR UPDATE` and only from `pending` (409 `PENDING_MATERIAL_NOT_PENDING`); the approval needs a code (422 `PENDING_MATERIAL_CODE_REQUIRED`) in the raw-material pattern with its category's prefix (422 `PENDING_MATERIAL_CODE_FORMAT`) and a raw-material category (422 `PENDING_MATERIAL_CATEGORY_INVALID`; `assertApprovableMaterial`, rule `pendingMaterialCodeError` in `src/lib/pendingMaterials/materialRequestRules.ts`, which also holds the one unit list `MATERIAL_UNITS` of the request form and the approval window; v10.0.143, TD-1202), creates the item through `ItemCatalogService.createItem` (TD-653 code and name rules, code counter) and keeps its id on the request (`pending_materials.item_id`), and every write has its audit row with `tx` (`PendingMaterialsService` in `src/services/pendingMaterials.service.ts`). The page shows approve and reject by `pending_materials.approve` and delete by `pending_materials.delete`, on pending requests only (v9.0.397, TD-825). Project control's «درخواست ماده اولیه جدید» (`AddMaterialModal`, shown by `pending_materials.create`) sends a request to this queue (`pendingMaterialRequestOf` in `src/lib/pendingMaterials/customMaterialRequest.ts`), never an item and never opening stock; the request's project must be live (422 `PENDING_MATERIAL_PROJECT_INVALID`) and its title is read from the project. A request starts the active `pending_material` workflow in its create transaction (`maybeStartWorkflow({ tx })`); the domain action `registerPendingMaterialWorkflowAction` (`src/services/inventory/pendingMaterialWorkflowAction.ts`) approves the request on a step `approved` or the action `APPROVE_PENDING_MATERIAL` and rejects it on a step `rejected`, inside the transition's `tx` and with `pending_materials.approve` from the signer, and a direct approve, reject or delete closes the open instance in the same transaction (`terminateOpenWorkflows`) (v9.0.398, TD-826, product-owner decision ت۵).
- **Project BOM Allocation From a Receipt (v8.0.32, TD-287, product-owner decision):** Materials enter stock only through a purchase receipt and are then allocated from stock with `allocateMaterialsForProject` (the allocation's source movement is its own `out` row); the unused allocation-from-receipt path was removed (v10.1.63, OBS-R2-42, decision ت۴۰). `releaseAllocation` brings the materials back at the Kardex unit cost of the allocation's own `out` movement, never the current WAC; an allocation whose source movement is an `in` (a receipt allocation before v8.0.32) is released without a stock movement (v8.0.33, TD-288). Every allocation issues a draft voucher Dr work in progress (1402, detailed project) / Cr raw materials or finished goods (by item type) at the Kardex cost of its out movement, linked by `journal_vouchers.source_bom_allocation_id` (migration 0049, `src/services/accounting/bomAllocationVoucher.ts`); releasing the allocation voids it through `voidSourceVoucher`; consumption posts nothing, the materials stay in 1402 (v8.0.34, TD-286, product-owner decision). Delivering a project's finished goods to stock («ورود به انبار», `ProjectService.addProjectToInventory`) issues, in the same transaction, a final `production_receipt` document linked to the project, whose voucher debits finished goods and credits work in progress (detailed project), at the entered unit price, else the item's current WAC; it never moves stock directly (v8.0.35, TD-285, product-owner decision). Its production receipt's CREATE audit row (`createdAudit` of `createDocumentWithDetails`) and the project's delivery row, and the rows of a material allocation, its consumption and its release (`src/services/inventory/bomAllocationAudit.ts`), are written with their own transaction (v10.1.12, TD-943). That delivery is the only way a production receipt naming a project is recorded (v9.0.325, TD-780, product-owner decision ت۷ of package 8): `POST /documents` refuses one with a `projectId` with 422 `PRODUCTION_RECEIPT_PROJECT_ONLY` (`assertNotProjectDelivery` in `src/services/documents/documentRecordRule.ts`). A finished product made outside any project enters stock from the stock page as a production receipt without a project (v10.1.6, N-06, TD-1262, product-owner decision ت۲۶): final only (a draft, and finalizing any draft production receipt, is 422 `PRODUCTION_RECEIPT_FINAL_ONLY`, `assertProductionReceiptNotFinalized`), rial only (422 `PRODUCTION_RECEIPT_RIAL_ONLY`) and finished products only (422 `PRODUCTION_RECEIPT_PRODUCTS_ONLY`), checked after the item locks by `assertStandaloneProductionReceipt` (`src/services/documents/standaloneProductionReceipt.ts`); it has no party, its unit cost is the entered material cost, else the item's current weighted average cost (TD-256 / TD-906), it is recorded only with `warehouse.production_receipt` (`PRODUCTION_RECEIPT_PERMISSION`, requires `warehouse.in`, granted to no role by default), and its draft voucher debits finished goods and credits «مصرف مواد بی پروژه» (mapping `unassignedConsumptionAccountCode`, default 6003) instead of work in progress; the stock page offers it to holders of that key, rial, without a party and picking finished products only. Delivery is capped by the project's planned quantity (v8.0.72, TD-327, product-owner decision «با دلیل»): under the project lock the sum of the project's final `production_receipt` lines per item (`deliveredProjectQuantities` in `src/services/projects/projectDeliveryCap.ts`) plus this delivery may exceed the planned quantity (`products`, else the main item and quantity) only with `overDeliveryReason` (else 422 `OVER_DELIVERY_REASON_REQUIRED`, the «ورود به انبار» tab asks for the reason and resends); the reason goes into the receipt notes and the activity log; a cancelled project or an item outside the project's products is refused. A project with an open (`allocated`) BOM allocation is not deleted: `deleteProject` locks the project row and refuses with `PROJECT_HAS_OPEN_ALLOCATIONS` until the allocations are released (v8.0.121, TD-412, product-owner decision); nor is one with a live document or a work-in-progress (1402) balance in rials over vouchers of any status (409 `PROJECT_HAS_LEDGER_ITEMS`, `assertProjectDeletable` in `src/services/projects/projectDeleteGuard.ts`, `projectWipBalances` in `src/services/accounting/projectWipBalance.ts`), and the financial health check lists the 1402 balance of each completed, cancelled or deleted project (`project_wip_closed_balance`); piecework labour stays a period expense, so the delivery price is the materials cost only and the «ورود به انبار» tab says so (v10.0.172–v10.0.174, TD-922 / TD-919 / TD-920, product-owner decision ت۴ ب of phase 5); cancelling such a project is refused the same way (`assertNoOpenAllocations` in `src/services/projects/projectOpenAllocations.ts`), and materials are never allocated to a cancelled or completed project (422 `PROJECT_CLOSED_FOR_ALLOCATION` under the project lock, `isProjectOpenForAllocation` in `src/lib/projects/projectStatus.ts`; the allocation form offers only open projects), while releasing its earlier allocations stays possible (v9.0.410, TD-759, product-owner decision ت۹). A cancelled project takes no new document either: a final document with its `projectId`, the finalize of a draft one and a new or moved work log on it are 422 `PROJECT_CANCELLED` (`assertProjectNotCancelled` in `src/services/accounting/projectCancelledGuard.ts`), while voiding earlier ones stays possible (v10.0.184, TD-921, product-owner decision ت۱۰ of phase 5). Both allocation paths pass the remittance's sellable gate after the item locks and before any movement (`assertOutflowWithinSellable` with the project's id, `assertAllocationWithinSellable`): the reservations of another project or a sales proforma are never allocated, the project's own reservation is free, and a shortage is 400 `INSUFFICIENT_STOCK` with nothing moved (v9.0.451, TD-905). Allocation moves stock out and asks the remittance's `warehouse.out` only (`BOM_ALLOCATE_PERMISSIONS`); releasing an allocation and the project delivery bring stock in and ask the receipt's `warehouse.in` as a second guard besides their own keys (`PROJECT_STOCK_IN_PERMISSIONS` in `src/lib/permissions/projectPermissions.ts`), and their buttons follow both guards (`canReleaseAllocation`, `canDeliver` of `useProjectPermissions`); no migration grants the warehouse keys (v9.0.454, TD-923, product-owner decision ت۳ الف of phase 5). `GET /inventory/allocations` searches (Arabic letters folded), filters and counts in SQL; with `page` it answers one page `{ allocations, total, page, limit, statusCounts }` (counts of every status under the other filters), without it every filtered allocation for the Excel export (`listBomAllocations` / `bomAllocationStatusCounts` in `src/services/inventory/bomAllocationList.ts`), and the finalize reads only the stock items its need rows and preset materials name by code or name key, trimmed as JavaScript trims and case-mapped under the «C» collation, plus every item whose code holds a non-ASCII character or whose name a character outside ASCII and the Arabic script, so it matches as the whole list did (`readStockItemsForRefs`); a NUL in a search or filter is dropped (`normalizeSearchText`, `withoutNul` in `src/lib/search/searchText.ts`) (v10.2.17, TD-1322).
- **Project Free Stock, Delivery Cost and Issued Materials (v10.2.3, TD-1290, package 3 of 10.2):** The project materials tab, the purchase list and «سفارش خرید بخش» judge each material row with the finalize's own rule: free for the project = total stock − the reservations of sales proformas and other finalized projects (never below 0), and the need less what the project already received (`projectIssuedQuantities`), read from `GET /projects/:id/material-availability?itemIds=` (quantities only, `READ_PERMISSIONS.projectRecord`; `projectMaterialAvailability` in `src/services/projects/serverInventoryControl.ts`) and turned into a row state by `buildMaterialAvailabilityView` (`src/lib/projects/materialAvailability.ts`, which runs `planProjectReservation` on the unsaved sections): «موجود», «کسری», «تبدیل واحد لازم» and «بی کالای انبار» are automatic and a row keeps only «در حال سفارش» by hand (`procurementStatus`); without those figures no row is «موجود», the readiness is unknown and the purchase list keeps the earlier total-stock rule. The «ورود به انبار» tab reads `GET /projects/:id/delivery-cost` (the project's 1402 balance from `projectWipBalances`, every voucher status, and each planned product's delivered quantity; the balance and the average cost only for item cost readers, `ITEM_COST_READ_PERMISSIONS`) and, for a single-product project, prefills the unit cost with balance ÷ max(remaining plan, this delivery) at four decimals (`suggestedDeliveryUnitCost` in `src/lib/projects/deliveryCostSuggestion.ts`); the user may change or clear it, and a cleared field still posts no price (the current average cost, TD-977). Before posting it shows the balance, what this delivery credits and what remains, and warns for several products or a balance left at plan completion; right before each post it reads the balance again (after a failed read too; it skips the read only when a successful read said the cost is hidden), refuses a suggestion that changed meanwhile and asks a confirmation when the delivery would leave the 1402 balance below zero once rounded to the rial (`checkDeliveryPrices`, `wipOverdrawn`); the over-delivery resend is priced and checked for the quantities it sends, and changing a quantity closes its reason prompt; the server's posting rule and every existing voucher and balance are unchanged. «سفارش خرید بخش» groups a section's rows by warehouse item when the finalize turns them into its unit, else by item and row unit, never adding quantities of different units, orders min(the project's shortage of the item, the section's need) in the item unit and marks «در حال سفارش» only the rows it orders (`buildSectionPurchaseList` in `src/components/project/projectInventoryUtils.ts`), and a finalized project whose stored shortage is now covered says a re-finalize is needed to reserve it (`storedShortagesNowFree`). The materials tab lists «مواد گرفته‌شده از انبار» from `GET /projects/:id/issued-materials` (open or consumed allocations and live final remittances and waste of the project, the newest 500, the Kardex cost only for item cost readers; `projectIssuedMaterials` in `src/services/projects/projectIssuedMaterials.ts`).
- **Centralized Stock Movements:** All stock operations, warehouse locations, and WAC calculations MUST route through `DocumentService.applyStockMovement` in `src/services/document.service.ts` (item creation and opening stock included). Warehouse-to-warehouse transfers that must not change WAC move both rows through `ItemWarehouseStockService.applyMovement` and then rebuild the cache. Every transfer is a final `transfer` document (own number series through `DocumentRefNumberService.assignDocumentRefNumber`, one line at the source warehouse, both Kardex rows linked by `document_id`; source and destination are read from those rows by `transferLocationsByDocument`) and is voided only through `deleteDocument`, whose reversal of transfer rows moves quantity only (`applyStockReversal` with `quantityOnly`, the same rule in `replayKardexWac`) (v9.0.80, TD-489, product-owner decision); transfers recorded before v9.0.80 stay without a document.
- **Procurement Delivery Is Stock-In Only (v8.0.10, TD-267):** `ProcurementService.deliverOrderToWarehouse` and the linked-document finalize of a requisition accept only incoming documents (`receipt`, `purchase`); a purchase proforma is a `receipt` with status `proforma` (never type `proforma`, which finalizes into a sales invoice); `finalizeDocument` treats `purchase` as stock-in like `createDocument`. Delivering an order finalizes it and updates its requisition in one transaction: the requisition row is locked first and the document status re-read under that lock (a repeated delivery adds nothing); each item's received quantity is the sum of all active document lines, filled across the requisition rows of that item in order (`applyDeliveredLines` in `src/services/procurement/requisitionReceipt.ts`; v8.0.36, TD-290). Delivery and «دریافت کالا» finalize an order on the delivery day (`atFinalizeDay`, `finalizeDayTarget` in `src/services/documents/proformaInvoice.ts`): the document, its Kardex rows and voucher take that date, its number stays unless the fiscal year changes, and the order date stays in its notes (v10.0.236, TD-914, product-owner decision ت۹ الف). `POST /procurement/requisitions/:id/convert-to-orders` validates exactly the body the split-order form (`SplitOrderModal`) sends: the requisition row id is optional, and each package keeps its target warehouse, type and status (draft or proforma only; stock enters only through delivery) (v8.0.37, TD-291); an order group has no currency and its lines no discount: the group and its lines are `strict`, so either key is 400, never dropped into an IRR order without discount (v10.0.235, TD-941, product-owner decision ت۹ ب). Ordering more than a requisition's remaining quantity is allowed only with a reason (v8.0.38, TD-289, product-owner decision): `convertToPurchaseOrders` refuses it without `overOrderReason` (422, `OVER_ORDER_REASON_REQUIRED`, the message lists the items and excess; `findOverOrders` in `src/services/procurement/requisitionOrder.ts`), the split-order form warns and requires the reason (`OverOrderWarning`), and the reason is recorded on the requisition row (`overOrders`), in the requisition and order notes and in the activity log; the conversion runs in one transaction under the requisition row lock. Voiding a linked order locks its requisition before the items and rebuilds the ordered and received quantities of that order's items from the live documents (`rebuildOrderRows` in `src/services/procurement/requisitionReceipt.ts`, wired through `registerLinkedOrderHooks` in the composition root, since the documents package does not import procurement); a received requisition left with an open row takes new orders without a new approval (v10.0.237, TD-912, product-owner decision ت۹ الف). Editing a linked order's lines (`PUT /documents/:id`) follows the same over-order rule: an edit that raises an item's excess needs `overOrderReason` (else 422 `OVER_ORDER_REASON_REQUIRED`), recorded on the row, in the requisition and order notes and in the activity log, and the requisition's ordered quantities follow the new lines (v10.0.238, TD-913, same decision). A requisition workflow action (`executeWorkflowAction`) runs in one transaction under the requisition row lock too; «دریافت کالا» (`receive_items`, `receiveRequisitionItems` in `src/services/procurement/requisitionReceiveAction.ts`) finalizes the requisition's open orders and refuses a catalog row that was never ordered (409 `REQUISITION_ROWS_NOT_ORDERED`, «ابتدا سفارش خرید با تأمین‌کننده و قیمت صادر کنید»; v9.0.350, TD-699, product-owner decision ت۴ of package 10; before, such rows entered stock with one final receipt from «تامین‌کننده تدارکات» at the estimate), a finalize error refuses the whole action, and the received quantity is rebuilt from the requisition's final documents; a requisition then left with a row neither received nor closed (`isSettledRequisitionRow`) is refused with 409 `REQUISITION_ROWS_NOT_SETTLED` naming the rows and the whole transition into `received` rolls back, from every path (v9.0.457, TD-911); a received or rejected requisition is never received or converted to orders again (409) (v8.0.71, TD-326). The project page's purchase window records no document itself: it records the project's purchase requisition, and its direct mode, shown only to holders of `procurement.create` / `projects.edit`, `procurement.order` and `procurement.approve` / `procurement.manage`, converts that requisition at once into one draft order with the server number, a supplier picked by id and a listed warehouse (`src/lib/projects/projectPurchaseOrder.ts`; v9.0.416, TD-745, product-owner decision ت۸ of package 11). A new requisition starts its workflow in its create transaction and a start error refuses it (v10.0.45, TD-940). A requisition is converted to orders and delivered only from its approved workflow step: a holder of `procurement.approve` (only that key since v10.0.48, TD-1126) first runs the approve transition in their own name (`ensureRequisitionApproved` in `src/services/procurement/requisitionApproval.ts`), anyone else gets 409 `REQUISITION_NOT_APPROVED`; its step moves only through `executeTransition`, and no production file outside `src/services/workflow/` writes the workflow tables (Vitest `workflowTableWrites.test.ts`) (v9.0.315 / v9.0.317, TD-689 / TD-692, product-owner decision ت۱ of package 10). The default purchase requisition workflow guards every action (`PURCHASE_REQUISITION_STEP_GUARDS` in `src/services/workflow/purchaseWorkflowGuards.ts`): approve, reject and cancel `procurement.approve`, with «آغازکننده تأیید نکند» on approve, so the requisition's creator never approves it (403 `WF_INITIATOR_EXCLUDED`; the system admin is exempt), receive `warehouse.in` and reopen `procurement.create`; an install still holding the old unguarded seed is upgraded at boot (`upgradeLegacySeedGuards`), and an edited definition with an action without role or permission is only listed by the financial health check (`workflow_unguarded_purchase_action`, `src/services/accounting/unguardedPurchaseActionHealth.ts`) (v10.0.235, OBS-R2-36, payroll duties plan decision ت۱۱); the procurement page shows approve, reject and cancel by `procurement.approve` only and reopen by its route keys plus `procurement.create` (`canReopen` of `useProcurementAccess`; v10.0.48, TD-1126). A requisition is received only when every row is received or closed (`isSettledRequisitionRow`), and delivery and its receive transition run in one transaction with the user's role and permissions (v9.0.316, TD-690). Goods enter stock only with the permission that records the receipt final (`warehouse.in`, `PROCUREMENT_RECEIVE_PERMISSION` in `src/lib/permissions/procurementPermissions.ts`), on every path: the requisition's domain action asks it as `requiredPermissions` of a transition into the `received` step (`receivePermissionsOfStep`; transition route, inbox task, «دریافت کالا» and delivery's receive transition, from the signer or the delegator's role, 403 `WF_ENTITY_PERMISSION_REQUIRED`), and order delivery asks it on top of its route keys with `can()` before its transaction (`assertMayReceiveIntoStock`, 403 `PROCUREMENT_RECEIVE_PERMISSION_REQUIRED`); the procurement page shows the delivery and receive buttons only with it (`useProcurementAccess`) (v9.0.455, TD-904, product-owner decision ت۳ of phase 5). A requisition with a live order is neither deleted nor edited; deleting one terminates its workflow, and editing is allowed only before approval, keeping the stored row ids, quantities and links (v9.0.318 / v9.0.319, TD-695 / TD-696). A procurement order is a live document whose `documents.procurement_requisition_id` is set (migration 0082, written by convert-to-orders; legacy orders were linked only from an unambiguous `[تدارکات: درخواست …]` tag, the rest is listed by the financial health check as `procurement_order_link_unresolved`): the order list, the desk summary and delivery read only such documents (another document is 422 `PROCUREMENT_ORDER_NOT_LINKED`), and the list filters, counts and pages in SQL (`listProcurementOrders` in `src/services/procurement/procurementOrderList.ts`) (v9.0.347, TD-691 / TD-698, product-owner decision ت۲). Consolidation takes only unapproved requisitions without orders (`canConsolidateRequisition` in `src/lib/procurement/requisitionFields.ts`), locks them in id order and refuses a missing one; in one transaction it creates the consolidated requisition and marks each source `consolidated` with `purchase_requisitions.consolidated_into_id` (migration 0083) and a workflow terminated through `terminateOpenWorkflows`, and a consolidated requisition takes no action (409 `REQUISITION_CONSOLIDATED`); open sources of older consolidations are only listed by the financial health check (`procurement_consolidation_open_sources`) (v9.0.349, TD-694, product-owner decision ت۳).
- **Default Warehouse (TD-203, v7.0.36):** The default warehouse is the ACTIVE warehouse with the LOWEST id. Any query that picks a default/first warehouse MUST `orderBy(asc(warehouses.id))` (or use `getDefaultWarehouseCode` in `src/services/inventory/warehouseResolver.ts`); PostgreSQL row order without `ORDER BY` changes after updates. The Kardex location codes `''` and `default` always mean the default warehouse (`LEDGER_RESERVED_WAREHOUSE_CODES` in the same file), so a new warehouse with such a code is refused with 422 `WAREHOUSE_CODE_RESERVED`, a void's reversal of a row without a location writes the default warehouse's code, and a legacy warehouse with such a code is left unchanged and listed by the financial health check (`warehouse_reserved_code`, v9.0.110, TD-482).
- **Warehouse Deactivation (v9.0.112, TD-490, product-owner decision ت۵):** Deactivating or reactivating a warehouse runs in one transaction with its audit row, under the transaction advisory lock `WAREHOUSE_ACTIVE_SET` (91010) and the warehouse row `FOR UPDATE`; every stock movement locks its active warehouse row `FOR KEY SHARE` in `ItemWarehouseStockService.applyMovement` (an inactive warehouse is 422 `WAREHOUSE_INACTIVE`), so the «no stock» check sees in-flight movements. The last active warehouse is never deactivated (422 `WAREHOUSE_LAST_ACTIVE`); only the system admin reactivates one (`POST /warehouses/:id/reactivate`, «انبارهای غیرفعال» in «مدیریت انبارها»), and creating an inactive warehouse's code again is 409 pointing to reactivation.
- **Document Statuses:** Supported document statuses in API and services: `'draft'`, `'proforma'` (پیش‌فاکتور), and `'final'`. A document in `proforma` status, of type `proforma` or `invoice` (v10.0.107, TD-910, product-owner decision ت۷ الف), finalizes into an `invoice` dated the finalization moment (business now) that takes the invoice series' next number of that date's fiscal year (Kardex rows, the voucher and events carry both); the proforma number and Jalali date are kept in its notes (`proformaInvoiceTarget` in `src/services/documents/proformaInvoice.ts`; v8.0.51 / v8.0.119, TD-317 / TD-410, product-owner decisions). A sales lead is «won» only with a live proforma (TD-309): voiding the document a lead points to (`crm_leads.proforma_id`: its proforma, or the invoice finalized from it) releases the lead in the void transaction, its row locked before the items, and moves a `won` lead back to `proposal` / `active` with a note (`releaseLeadOfVoidedDocument` in `src/services/crm/leadProforma.ts`; v9.0.12, TD-423); a refused void leaves the lead unchanged. Only a sales document (`invoice` / `proforma` type) in `draft` or `proforma` status enters the approval workflow, inside its create transaction (`needsApprovalWorkflow` in `src/services/documents/documentApprovalScope.ts`, `maybeStartWorkflow({ tx })`; a start error rejects the document); the browser never starts it, and finalizing outside the workflow closes the document's open instance in the same transaction (`terminateOpenWorkflows` in `src/services/workflow/workflowTermination.ts`; v9.0.39, TD-446, product-owner decision ت۴). The reject step of a document's workflow (`rejected`) turns a sales proforma back into a draft in the transition's transaction, so it stops reserving, with an audit row (`returnRejectedProformaToDraft` in `src/services/documents/documentWorkflowAction.ts`; v10.0.85, TD-1137, product-owner decision ت۱۲). A document whose workflow is running and past its initial step is not edited: `updateDocument` refuses it under the document row lock with 409 `DOCUMENT_IN_REVIEW` naming the step (`assertDocumentNotInReview` in `src/services/documents/documentReviewLock.ts`); a saved sales proforma above the sellable stock is kept and `POST` / `PUT /documents` answer one warning per short item in `stockWarnings` (`proformaStockWarnings` in `src/services/documents/documentSellableGate.ts`, the outflow gate's rule), which the invoice form shows (v10.0.86, TD-1138, product-owner decisions ت۱۳ / ت۱۴). Voiding a document, deleting or voiding a draft journal voucher, and deleting an item, bank account or purchase requisition terminate the entity's running workflow in the same transaction, after the entity row lock: instance `TERMINATED`, pending tasks `canceled`, one `terminate` history row (v9.0.40, TD-447, product-owner decision ت۵). A proforma with `crmLeadId` is created in one transaction with its lead (v9.0.13, TD-424): `lockLeadForNewProforma` locks the lead row before the document and refuses a second proforma under that lock, the document is created with `externalTx`, and `markLeadProforma` writes the link and the note in the same transaction (`src/services/crm/leadProforma.ts`).
