Linking or unlinking a document and a lead (`crmLeadId` on `POST` / `PUT /documents`) needs `crm.manage` (`CRM_LEAD_LINK_PERMISSION`, else 403 `CRM_LEAD_LINK_FORBIDDEN` before any write), and a proforma is never linked to a closed lead, won or lost by stage or status (`isClosedLead`, 422 `CRM_LEAD_CLOSED` under the lead lock), so a closed lead never goes back to `proposal` (v10.0.176, TD-932, CRM plan decision ت۱۴). `PUT /documents/:id` links or unlinks a lead the same way inside the edit transaction (v9.0.323, TD-776): `lockLeadsForDocumentLink` locks the leads linked to the document and the new one, in id order, before the document row; a missing lead is 422 `CRM_LEAD_NOT_FOUND` and, for a proforma, a lead with another proforma 422 `CRM_LEAD_HAS_PROFORMA`, both before any write; `applyDocumentLeadLink` writes `crm_lead_id`, marks the new lead and releases an unlinked one with a note. No route writes `documents.crm_lead_id` itself: `POST /documents` passes the checked id to the document insert. `PUT /documents/:id` requires the document's `version` (400 without it) and refuses a stale one with 409 `OCC_CONFLICT`; the invoice form sends the version of the proforma it loaded (v10.0.108, TD-972).
- **Stock Direction From the Document Type (v9.0.238, TD-770, product-owner decision ت۲ of package 8):** A document's stock direction comes only from its type (`DOCUMENT_STOCK_DIRECTIONS` / `documentStockDirection` in `src/lib/documents/documentDirection.ts`, shared by the server and the stock document page): receipt, purchase, production receipt and sales return move stock in; invoice, proforma, remittance and waste move it out; a stock count moves each line by its count. Create and finalize use it (`stockDirectionOf` in `src/services/documents/documentRecordRule.ts`), and every outgoing document recorded or finalized as final passes one sellable gate, `assertOutflowWithinSellable` (`src/services/documents/documentSellableGate.ts`, v9.0.239, TD-775): the quantities of each item and warehouse are summed and checked once against min(warehouse stock, total stock − other reservations), the reservation report is read for the document's items only and a report error refuses the document; the stock document page caps an exit with the same rule for its source warehouse (`itemReservationSummary` in `src/lib/documents/stockReservations.ts`, v9.0.242, TD-799); an `inOut` against the type is 422 `DOCUMENT_DIRECTION_MISMATCH` (`assertRecordableDocument`, checked by `POST /documents` before the permission and by `createDocumentWithDetails` for every caller), a transfer is never recorded there and a document of any other type or status is never recorded, with or without lines (`DOCUMENT_TYPE_NOT_RECORDABLE` / `DOCUMENT_STATUS_INVALID`; transfers only through `transfers.routes.ts`; the route accepts only recordable types). The database holds the same rules (v9.0.338, TD-786, migration 0081): `chk_documents_type` and `chk_documents_status` on a live document, and a live line's quantity, unit price and discount are never negative (`chk_document_items_*`; a stock count line may hold a counted zero, the services refuse a zero quantity on any other line); each constraint was added `NOT VALID` and validated only on clean data, a soft-deleted row is exempt, and the financial health check lists what is left (`document_integrity_constraints`, `src/services/documents/documentConstraintHealth.ts`).
- **Soft Delete & Reversal (DB-009):** Never HARD DELETE stock transactions. Set `is_deleted = 1`, issue an atomic reversal transaction with `reversal_of_id`, and recalibrate balances via `DocumentService.applyStockMovement`. No business row is deleted physically; the exceptions are listed in `scripts/ratchets/HARD_DELETES.md` (§21).
- **Negative Stock Policy (DB-006, TD-180):** Negative stock is **always forbidden** (product-owner decision, v7.0.22). Reductions (`out`) must fail with `InsufficientStockError` before committing; the database constraint `chk_iws_current_stock_non_negative` on `item_warehouse_stocks` is the final safeguard. The legacy `warning` / `allowed` options are removed and must not be reintroduced without dropping that constraint in a new migration.

## 4. WooCommerce Integration & Webhooks
- **Public Route Exemption:** Webhook endpoints (`/api/woocommerce/webhook/order`) must be exempted from `authenticateToken` in `src/middleware/auth.ts` for ping/payloads (GET, POST, HEAD).
- **Preview Domain Resolution:** Replace `ais-dev-` with `ais-pre-` in `window.location.origin` when displaying webhook delivery URLs in AI Studio preview environments.
- **SKU Matching & Orders:** Match line items to ERP items via `items.code` (SKU); a SKU that is no live item's current code finds the item that held it as an earlier code in `item_code_history` (latest change wins, a current code always wins) and the order log names that old code (`resolveItemsByCode` in `src/services/items/itemCodeChange.ts`, `oldCodeOrderNote`; v10.1.9, D-15, TD-1264). Route orders through `DocumentService.createDocument` (`docType: 'invoice'`, `status: 'final'`, `inOut: 'out'`).
- **Order Status Policy (TD-190, v7.0.30):** All order processing lives in `WooOrderSyncService` (`src/services/woocommerce/wooOrderSync.service.ts`). Only `processing` / `completed` issue a final invoice; other statuses are logged as `deferred`. `cancelled` / `failed` void the invoice through `DocumentService.deleteDocument` (DB-009); `refunded` is only flagged `needs_review`, and so is an order moved to the trash or deleted in WooCommerce (status `trash`, or webhook topic `order.deleted`, whose body is only `{ id }` and never overwrites the stored order payload; v8.0.126, TD-407). An order with any unmatched SKU is rejected as a whole (no partial invoices). Each order is serialized by a row lock on its `woocommerce_order_logs` row; failures are persisted outside the rolled-back transaction. The webhook always answers HTTP 200 (WooCommerce never re-delivers and disables a webhook after 5 non-2xx responses); retries happen via manual order sync.
- **Rial Units of WooCommerce Orders (v8.0.39, TD-292):** An order's currency maps to IRR through `rialUnitMultiplier` (`src/lib/woocommerce/wcCurrency.ts`, shared with the price push): IRR ×1, IRT / TOMAN ×10, IRHR (thousand rial) ×1,000, IRHT (thousand toman) ×10,000; the invoice is in IRR. Any other currency stays a foreign-currency document and needs an exchange rate (TD-198).
- **Namesake WooCommerce Buyers (v9.0.332, TD-703, product-owner decision ت۱):** `resolveWooOrderCustomer` (`src/services/woocommerce/wooOrderCustomer.ts`) finds the order's customer by phone key, else (no phone) by the name key `lower(btrim(name))`, and the invoice takes the stored customer name; a new buyer whose name is another customer's (another phone, or that customer has none) gets a new customer «name (phone)» (`namesakeCustomerName`, audited), noted in the order log; a taken distinct name fails the order (422 `WOO_NAMESAKE_CUSTOMER_TAKEN`), never posts to the other customer.
- **Customer Match by Phone Key (v8.0.40, TD-296):** A WooCommerce order finds its customer by `phoneMatchKey` (`src/lib/search/phoneMatchKey.ts`, re-exported by `src/services/woocommerce/phoneMatchKey.ts`: Persian/Arabic digits to Latin, non-digits removed, the last ten digits; `phoneMatchKeySql` is the same key on the column), so +98…, 0098…, 0912… and spaced or Persian-digit forms of one number match one customer; without a phone the name is matched. The sales lead link, the customer form's duplicate check (on edit only when the number changes) and the customer Excel import match phones with the same key (`findActiveCustomerByPhone` / `assertCustomerPhoneAvailable` in `src/services/customers/customerIdentity.ts`; v9.0.7, TD-419, product-owner decision).
- **Exact Line Totals (v8.0.41, TD-297):** An order line keeps its exact total on the invoice: when the total is not divisible by its whole quantity in the currency's smallest unit (rial without decimals, cents otherwise), `exactLineSplit` (`src/services/woocommerce/exactLineTotal.ts`) makes two lines, quantity − 1 at the rounded-down price and one unit at the remainder (1000 for 3 = 2 × 333 + 1 × 334). Never use a line discount for rounding (it would post to sales discounts).
- **Negative Fees Are Line Discounts (v8.0.42, TD-295, product-owner decision):** Positive `fee_lines` and shipping are the invoice's service charge; the sum of negative `fee_lines` (an order discount) is spread over the invoice lines in proportion to their amounts as line discounts (`allocateFeeDiscount` in `src/services/woocommerce/feeDiscount.ts`, shares floored in the currency's smallest unit, remainder to the larger lines, exact sum), like a WooCommerce coupon; a discount larger than the lines rejects the order.
- **Changes to an Invoiced Order Need Review (v8.0.43, TD-294, product-owner decision):** A webhook or manual sync of an already invoiced order is compared with the payload the invoice was issued from (`describeOrderChange` in `src/services/woocommerce/orderChange.ts`: line SKU / quantity / total, the order total and the sum of refunds). A difference sets the log to `needs_review` with the difference in its message and leaves the invoice untouched; an unchanged order (e.g. processing → completed) stays `processed`. Voiding and reissuing, or a sales return, is the accountant's decision.
- **Connection Test With Stored Keys (v9.0.333, TD-723):** the WooCommerce tab sends no empty or masked («********») key to `/woocommerce/test-connection` (`wcTestConnectionBody` in `src/lib/woocommerce/wcConnectionTest.ts`); the route takes a missing key from the stored settings (`resolveWcTestCredentials`, `readWcConnectionSettings` in `src/services/woocommerce/wcConnectionSettings.ts`) and then only at the stored store address (else 422 `WC_TEST_STORED_KEYS_OTHER_URL`; incomplete settings 422 `WC_TEST_SETTINGS_INCOMPLETE`), so stored keys never go to another site.
- **API Keys Only in the Header (v8.0.127, TD-408):** Requests to the WooCommerce REST API send the consumer key and secret only in the `Authorization: Basic` header over HTTPS; only a plain-HTTP store URL (where WooCommerce refuses Basic auth) gets them in the query string (`wcAuthQueryParams` in `src/services/woocommerce/wcRequestAuth.ts`). Every request to the shop, not only the connection test, passes `assertSafeExternalUrl` first and follows a redirect only by hand: at most `WC_MAX_REDIRECTS` (3) hops, only on the same site (`shopRedirectTarget`: the same host ignoring letter case and «www.», the same scheme or http → https, the same port; the query is built again for each hop) and each hop through the guard before anything is sent to it; any other redirect is 422 `WC_REDIRECT_REFUSED` / `WC_REDIRECT_LIMIT` asking for the final shop address (`followShopRedirects` in `src/lib/woocommerce/wcRedirect.ts`, called by `makeWcRequest` in `src/services/woocommerce/wcClient.ts` since v10.2.6; v10.1.59, OBS-R2-82, decision ت۴۰).
- **Bulk Stock Sync Result (v9.0.334, TD-724):** the WooCommerce tab shows the bulk stock sync through `stockSyncOutcome` (`src/lib/woocommerce/stockSyncOutcome.ts`): any failed item (`failedCount` / `errors[]`) is an error message with the failed, updated and not-in-shop counts and the first errors, never the server's success text; the order log and invoice tables show a load error (`WcListEmptyRow`) instead of «no orders yet».
- **Online Shop Warehouse (v8.0.44, TD-293, product-owner decision):** The setting `wc_shop_warehouse` (an active warehouse code; empty = the default warehouse; an inactive or unknown code is refused on save) is the online shop's warehouse. WooCommerce invoices draw from it (`resolveShopWarehouseCode`) and the stock sync (`/woocommerce/sync-item`, `/woocommerce/sync-all-stocks`) pushes its sellable stock (`shopSellableStocks` in `src/services/woocommerce/shopWarehouse.ts`, the same `computeSellable` rule the invoice checks: min of that warehouse's stock and total stock minus reservations), never the total of all warehouses.
- **Shop Product Links and Batched Stock Push (v10.2.6, D-04 part A, OBS-R2-83, 10.2 package 6):** An item reaches the shop only through its live row in `wc_product_links` (migration 0128: one live link per item and per shop product or variation), made by «بازخوانی محصولات سایت» (`POST /woocommerce/refresh-products`, `woocommerce.manage`; `refreshWcCatalog` in `src/services/woocommerce/wcCatalog.service.ts`): it reads every catalog page in id order (100 per page, at most `WC_MAX_CATALOG_PAGES`, each product once; a refresh that reaches the cap says so and leaves untouched only the links of products above the largest id it read, and any other link whose product the shop no longer lists is closed; an empty catalog while items are linked is a failed run that changes no link (`EMPTY_CATALOG_MESSAGE`), and a reading that showed a product twice (an order with ties, such as date or menu order, which may also leave one out) or whose new ids did not come in ascending order and that was cut at the cap keeps every link it did not read and says so (`catalogUnordered`, `catalogOrderNotice`), while any other reading closes the links of products it did not list whatever its order) and the variations of variable products (at most `WC_VARIATION_READ_CONCURRENCY` at a time; a variation holding its parent's SKU is the parent), the whole read within `WC_CATALOG_DEADLINE_MS` (50 s, else a failed run; a failed read stops the other readers and no request outlives the refresh); an external or grouped product and a variation whose stock is the parent's (`manage_stock: parent`) get no link and are listed with their reason, never changed on the site; it links each product to the item its SKU finds with the order rule (`resolveItemsByCode`: the current code, else an earlier code); a SKU held by several shop products, a product without SKU, an unknown SKU and a second product of one item (the current code wins) get no link and are only listed, and a refresh creates or changes nothing on the site. The stock push (`/woocommerce/sync-all-stocks`, `/woocommerce/sync-item`; `src/services/woocommerce/wcStockPush.service.ts`) sends each link's whole, non-negative sellable stock (`siteStockQuantity`) in `products/batch` and `products/<id>/variations/batch` requests of at most `WC_BATCH_SIZE` (100) with `manage_stock: true`, says how many products had stock tracking turned on that way (`trackingEnabledCount`, `trackingEnabledNote`) and records each row's error on its link, all within `WC_PUSH_DEADLINE_MS` (50 s for the whole exchange: a refresh before it gets that less `WC_PUSH_MIN_BUDGET_MS` (5 s), a push left less than that is not started and is a failed run (`refreshBudgetMs` / `pushBudgetMs` in `src/lib/woocommerce/wcCatalog.ts`), and past the deadline nothing more is sent and the unsent products record why); an item without a link is 422 `WC_ITEM_NOT_LINKED` after one refresh. A catalog refresh and a stock push run under the session advisory lock `WOOCOMMERCE_SYNC` (91110; a second one at the same time is 409 `WC_SYNC_BUSY`); a push never sends a deleted item. Every exchange with the shop goes through the one client `makeWcRequest` in `src/services/woocommerce/wcClient.ts` (each connection's address is checked again when it opens (`guardedDnsLookup` in `src/lib/ssrfGuard.ts`, with its own HTTP and HTTPS agents, never through an environment proxy), an answer is at most `WC_MAX_RESPONSE_BYTES` (10 MB); a failing shop is 422 `WC_SHOP_REQUEST_FAILED` with a Persian message, never a 500) and a refresh or push writes a `wc_sync_runs` row (a failed one too) and one «فروشگاه اینترنتی» audit row of counts with its `tx` (no price, name or amount). The WooCommerce tab shows the links, the last refresh and push and the products and items left out (`GET /woocommerce/catalog`, `WcCatalogPanel`), and the synced-orders list reads the invoices the order log names (`erp_document_id`), never a search of the document notes.
- **Automatic Stock Push (v10.2.12, D-04 part B, 10.2 package 6):** «ارسال خودکار موجودی» (setting `wc_auto_push`, off by default; `POST /woocommerce/auto-push` with the target state `{ enabled }`, `woocommerce.manage`, audited with `tx`; turning it on needs a complete connection) runs once a minute (`WcShopSyncWorker` in `src/services/woocommerce/wcSyncWorker.service.ts`, started by `server.ts`, which then runs the part C runs, each step on its own; `runAutoPushOnce` in `src/services/woocommerce/wcAutoPush.service.ts`), after the stock writes commit and never inside them. It skips its turn while the `WOOCOMMERCE_SYNC` lock is taken and reads nothing from the shop while no link's sellable stock moved since it last brought that link in line (held in memory, single process; after a restart or a changed shop stock the link is compared with its shop stock) and no order webhook named its product (an `order.deleted` webhook through the payload its order log keeps; `markOrderLinesDue` in `src/services/woocommerce/wcAutoPushDue.ts`, called by `WooOrderSyncService.handleOrder` after the order's transaction); once an hour, and first after a start, every tracked link is compared (`WC_AUTO_FULL_PASS_MS`), and a stock changed by hand in the shop is put right only by the nightly catalog refresh of part C. It then reads the shop's open orders and sends, in batches of 100 and without `manage_stock`, only links whose shop stock tracking is already on (`site_manage_stock = 1`) and whose number differs, at most `WC_AUTO_PUSH_MAX_PRODUCTS` (500) a run, the rest on the next run; untracked links and links stopped after `WC_AUTO_MAX_LINK_FAILURES` (5) failures in a row are listed in the tab (`getAutoPushView`, `WcAutoPushPanel`) and a manual push that succeeds puts a stopped link back. A failure of the shop (no answer, timeout, redirect, 5xx, 401, 403, 408, 429; `isShopLevelFailure` in `wcStockPush.service.ts`) never counts against a link and makes the next run wait 1, 2, 4 … 30 minutes (`autoPushBackoffMinutes` in `src/lib/woocommerce/wcAutoPush.ts`); any other 4xx of a batch (a product or a parent removed in the shop) counts against each of its products and the run is `partial`, never a wait. Its runs carry `trigger: 'auto'`, write no audit row except the first failure of a streak, and are removed after `WC_AUTO_RUN_RETENTION_DAYS` (30) by the daily operational purge (each registered purge runs on its own, so one failure skips no other) (`purgeOldAutoRuns` in `src/services/woocommerce/wcSyncRunPurge.ts`, registered with `OperationalPurgeService.register`); «آخرین ارسال موجودی» shows manual pushes only. Every push, automatic or manual, sends the sellable stock less what the shop's open orders hold (`siteStocks` / `openOrderQuantities` in `src/services/woocommerce/wcOpenOrders.ts`, plan question ق۶): on-hold and processing orders without a live invoice in the order log, and completed orders whose order log is `failed` without a live invoice completed in the last `WC_FAILED_ORDER_HOLD_DAYS` (30) days, counted from the order's `date_completed_gmt`, else from when its order log was made, never from the log's last change (`failedOrderHeldSince`; an older one was most likely entered by hand, and the card says to put «سفارش ووکامرس #<number>» in that invoice's notes before «صدور فاکتور» of «دریافت دستی سفارش با شماره سفارش», which then links it instead of issuing a second invoice: `findActiveInvoice` reads the tag's Persian and Arabic-Indic digits and Arabic kaf as typed, a no-break space as a space, drops the zero-width non-joiner and the LRM, RLM and ALM marks and needs the whole number after #; a completion dated in the future is treated as missing, so the hold counts from the order log's creation, and `updated_at` only pre-filters the logs read); a pending order holds no shop stock and is not read; read with the stock in one repeatable-read snapshot, matched through the links (a variation line without its own link counts for its parent product's link). Orders are read newest first, each reading after the first starting on the last order of the one before (`offset`), and within `orderReadBudgetMs` of the push's deadline, and orders that cannot be read in full (an order listed without its id included) stop the push with a failed run and nothing sent, naming only the button that was pressed: a reading whose first order is not the last of the one before, a repeated order, a shop count (`X-WP-Total`) that changes between the first and the last page or differs from the orders read, page 1 read again at the end of a longer list showing other orders, or a first page counting more than `WC_MAX_ORDER_PAGES` (25) pages of 100, which fails after that one request; the bulk push card says so (`BULK_PUSH_OPEN_ORDERS_NOTE`).
- **Price Push, SKU Rename and Nightly Re-read (v10.2.15, D-04 part C, 10.2 package 6):** Two more automatic runs of the minute worker, each behind its own setting, off by default and written only through its route (`woocommerce.manage`, plus `products.edit_price` to choose a price list and `products.change_code` to turn the SKU rename on, checked with `can` in the route and asked by the tab too, which names the missing key; turning either off needs only `woocommerce.manage`; strict body, idempotent; the setting row locked, its «فروشگاه اینترنتی» audit row written with `tx`, turning a send on needs a complete connection; `src/services/woocommerce/wcFieldPush.service.ts`), under the `WOOCOMMERCE_SYNC` lock (taken: the run skips its turn). «ارسال خودکار قیمت» (`wc_price_list`, `POST /woocommerce/price-list` with `{ priceList }`: a configured price list, else 422 `WC_PRICE_LIST_NOT_CONFIGURED`; '' turns it off) sends that list's active price to each linked product's `regular_price` only, never the sale price (plan ق۲ / ق۳): the shop currency comes from `GET data/currencies/current` and its price decimals from `GET settings/general/woocommerce_price_num_decimals` (`readSiteMoney`, kept `WC_SITE_CURRENCY_TTL_MS` for display, both read again fresh before any run that has something to send and at night, the run planned again when either changed; `src/services/woocommerce/wcSiteCurrency.ts`; a shop that answers either one unreadably fails the run), a rial price goes to a rial-unit shop divided by its `rialUnitMultiplier` and any other price only to a shop of its own currency, never rounded: the price must be exact at the shop's decimals, at most `WC_SITE_PRICE_PLACES` (4) (`sitePricePlaces`), in plain digits, else it is not sent (`inexact_unit`; `sitePriceOf` in `src/lib/woocommerce/wcShopSync.ts`), and a link is sent only when its stored shop price (`wc_product_links.site_regular_price`, the value last sent or read) differs; a variable parent (`variable` or any `variable-*` type, `isVariableParentType`), a link whose type was never read (`site_type` '', migration 0131), a price in another currency, a zero price and an item without a price are not sent and are listed in the tab, also after a restart (the shop currency and decimals of the last price run); the price run row lists what it changed (`changed`: product, variation, from, to; at most `WC_PRICE_CHANGES_KEPT` (200) with `changedTotal`), and the tab says a regular price changed by hand in the shop is overwritten while the send is on. «تغییر خودکار کد در سایت» (`wc_sku_rename` '1', `POST /woocommerce/sku-rename` with `{ enabled }`) gives a link whose shop SKU is not its item's current code (`renamedLinks`) that code (card ت۲۹); turning it on asks with the number of links it would change. Both send in batches of 100 (`sendLinkFields` in `src/services/woocommerce/wcBatchUpdate.ts`), at most `WC_AUTO_PUSH_MAX_PRODUCTS` a run, write a `wc_sync_runs` row of kind `price_push` / `sku_push` with `trigger: 'auto'` (purged after 30 days like the stock runs, and so are the nightly refresh rows), stop a product after `WC_AUTO_MAX_LINK_FAILURES` refusals in a row (`price_failure_count` / `sku_failure_count`; a shop-level failure never counts), wait 1, 2, 4 … 30 minutes after failed runs of their own kind (`autoRunBackoff` in `src/services/woocommerce/wcRunBackoff.ts`) and audit only the first failure of a streak. While any automatic send is on, once a business day from `WC_NIGHTLY_HOUR` (3) in the display time zone, the catalog is read again like «بازخوانی محصولات سایت» but with no audit row (`runNightlyOnce` in `src/services/woocommerce/wcNightly.service.ts`; a refresh run row with `trigger: 'nightly'`, its `businessDay` and the `siteCurrency` / `siteDecimals` its prices were compared in, which label the tab's price differences, at most `WC_NIGHTLY_MAX_ATTEMPTS` failed readings `WC_NIGHTLY_RETRY_MINUTES` apart): it lists each stock or regular price that differs from what the program sent or sends (`catalogDifferences` in `src/services/woocommerce/wcCatalogDrift.ts`), sends nothing itself (the changed shop stock makes the stock push compare that link again, the price push resends the price) and lets stopped price and SKU links try again; the tab shows all of it (`shopSync` of `GET /woocommerce/catalog`, `WcShopSyncPanel`).

## 5. Security, Authentication & Audit Logging
- **HttpOnly Cookies:** JWT tokens are issued and stored exclusively via secure HttpOnly cookies (`auth_token`, `secure: true`, `sameSite: 'none'`, `path: '/'`). Browser JS MUST NOT store or read raw tokens in `localStorage`. All client fetch calls MUST specify `credentials: 'include'`.
- **No Token in Response Bodies (TD-185, v7.0.27):** Login/setup responses MUST NOT include the JWT by default. The only exception is the opt-in compatibility flag `EXPOSE_TOKEN_IN_BODY=true` for preview environments whose browsers block the iframe cookie (BUG-08); production deployments must leave it unset. In production the CSP lets only the app itself frame it and be connected to, plus the origins in `FRAME_ANCESTORS` / `EXTERNAL_API_ORIGINS` (`buildCspDirectives` in `src/lib/cspDirectives.ts`; v9.0.145, TD-597, product-owner decision t3).
- **Login Lockout per Username + IP (v7.0.70, TD-187, product-owner decision):** The progressive login lock (5 failures → 1, 2, 4 … at most 30 minutes) applies to the (username + client IP) pair, held in memory (single instance); the whole account (`users.locked_until`) is locked only after `ACCOUNT_LOCKOUT_THRESHOLD` = 50 failures from any address within 24 hours (`src/services/auth/loginSecurity.service.ts`). Unknown usernames behave identically; no CAPTCHA. Both 429 lock answers (this lock and the per-address login limiter, with the minutes left of its window) carry `locked: true` and `remainingMinutes` and the Persian-digit messages of `src/lib/auth/loginLockout.ts`; the login page counts down only from those two fields, never from the message text, and a 429 of the general request limiter is an error, not a lock (v9.0.220, TD-539). A wrong current password in the profile password change (`PUT /users/profile`) counts per user with the same progressive lock; while locked, even the right password is 429 `PASSWORD_CHANGE_LOCKED` with `locked` and `remainingMinutes` in `details`, and a right password ends the streak (`passwordChangeLockMinutes` / `recordPasswordChangeFailure` in the same service; v10.0.191, TD-961).
- **Session Verification & Logout:** Use `/api/auth/me` to verify session on load, and `/api/auth/logout` to clear the cookie. Login, logout and setup are exempt from CSRF, so they accept only the application's own origin (v9.0.77, TD-528, `sessionEndpointOriginGuard` in `src/middleware/sessionOrigin.ts`): the browser origin (`Origin`, else `Referer`) must be the request's own host, in `ALLOWED_ORIGINS` (never `*`) or `APP_URL`, plus localhost and Google preview hosts outside production; `Origin: null` is refused and a request with neither header (a non-browser client) passes. Logout with a valid session cookie requires that session's `x-csrf-token` (403 otherwise); an invalid or expired cookie is only cleared. Every login is one row of `user_sessions` (migration 0129: a random `sid` carried in the token, device user-agent, IP, sign-in and last-use time; `src/services/auth/sessionStore.ts`), and `authenticateToken` refuses a token whose session is closed or missing (`checkSession`, cached 30 s per sid and dropped from the cache when the session closes, so a close takes effect on that device's next request); a token from before it is adopted once under a key of its CSRF value, else its issue time (`legacySessionId`). Logout closes only this device's session, in one upsert so an old token's row adopted meanwhile is closed too, with the user's stored full name in its audit row, and its buttons say «خروج»; the profile lists the user's devices with this one marked and a name read from the user-agent without a library (`deviceLabelOf` in `src/lib/auth/deviceLabel.ts`), closes one other device (`DELETE /auth/sessions/:id`, this one is 422 `SESSION_IS_CURRENT`) or all the others (`POST /auth/sessions/close-others`, which reissues this device's cookie), and the users page closes one or all devices of another user with `users.manage` (`/users/:id/sessions`, the TD-520 grant rule, an admin account only by the system admin), each close audited with `tx` (`src/services/auth/userSessions.service.ts`, `UserSessionsPanel`, which warns an admin closing all devices of their own account there that this device signs out too); a token version raise (password reset, role change) still ends every session (v10.2.7, D-07, TD-1295; it replaces the v10.1.60 «خروج از همهٔ دستگاه‌ها» logout of OBS-R1-27). Every password is at least `MIN_PASSWORD_LENGTH` = 8 characters (`src/lib/auth/passwordPolicy.ts`, shared by the routes and the forms; v9.0.217, TD-532). A password an administrator sets for another user (create, reset, restore) is temporary (`users.must_reset_password = 1`), one set for one's own account is not: until the user changes it from the profile, `authenticateToken` refuses every request except `/auth/me`, `/users/profile`, `/users/my-permissions`, `/csrf` and logout with 403 `PASSWORD_RESET_REQUIRED` (`src/lib/auth/passwordReset.ts`), and the browser shows only the password form, closed only by «خروج» (v9.0.219, TD-523, product-owner decision ت۵).
- **Route Middleware Order:** Administrative/utility/seed routes MUST be registered AFTER `router.use(authenticateToken)` and protected via `requireSystemAdmin` (system maintenance) or `requirePermission` (everything else).
- **Role vs Permission Namespaces (v7.0.51, audit P2-10):** Permission keys always contain a dot (`customers.manage`); role codes never do (`ROLE_CODE_PATTERN = /^[a-z0-9_-]+$/`, enforced on role creation). `authorizePermission` / `userHasRoleOrPermission` match the role's permissions only against permission keys; `admin` always passes.
- **No Role Codes in Route Guards (v9.0.107, TD-516, product-owner decision ت۱ and the approved system-admin list):** `authorize` is gone and no route guard takes a role code. A route asks catalog permission keys (`authorizePermission` / `requirePermission`), or is one of the system maintenance routes in `SYSTEM_ADMIN_ONLY_ROUTES` (`src/tests/security/routeAccessPolicy.ts`: backup, clear data, activity-log purge, attachment cleanup and migration, default categories and chart of accounts, system health, environment, date calendar report, event reconciliation, conditional migration constraints) guarded by `requireSystemAdmin`, and `/metrics` is for the system admin too; the policy test checks the list both ways. Defining warehouses needs `warehouse.manage`, running the fiscal-year close `accounting.fiscal_close`, deleting a material request `pending_materials.delete`, and the activity log `audit_logs.view`. A legacy `*` in a role's permissions grants nothing. Migration 0062 gave each seed role (`is_system = 1`) the guard permission it had passed only by its code, expanded a non-admin `*` into every catalog key, and logged each change in `activity_logs`; custom roles were left as they were. Only the system admin role is a system role (v9.0.135, TD-885, permission model decision ت۹): migration 0067 set `is_system` to 1 on the admin row and 0 on every other role, logged in `activity_logs`, and `DELETE /roles/:id` refuses only the system admin role (a role with active users or needed by a workflow step is still refused; deleted users do not count, v9.0.179, TD-535). The system admin role's permissions are computed, never stored or edited (v9.0.136, TD-886, permission model rule 3): `GET /roles` lists it with every catalog key, `PUT /roles/:id` refuses its permissions (409 `SYSTEM_ADMIN_ROLE_FIXED`) and lets only the system admin change its name and description, and the role form shows its ticks all set and locked.
- **Shared Permission Catalog With Dependencies (v9.0.86, TD-880, permission model §4.2):** The permission catalog lives only in `src/lib/permissions/permissionCatalog.ts`, shared by the server and the browser (`GET /api/permissions` serves it). A key that is useless without another names it in `requires`: every action key of a group requires that group's view key, and `accounting.treasury_no_voucher` requires `accounting.treasury`. `POST /roles` / `PUT /roles/:id` store `withRequiredPermissions(requested)` and record the added keys in the audit row (`addedByRequirement`); the role form ticks a key's requirements, unticks its dependents (`withoutPermission`) and shows what saving an older role will add. A new catalog key that needs another key declares it there.
- **One Permission Check and Ratchets (v9.0.87, TD-881, permission model §4.5):** `can(user, ...permissionKeys)` in `src/middleware/authorize.ts` is the one permission check: the system admin (`SYSTEM_ADMIN_ROLE`, the only role code that code may name) passes, any other user passes only when the role holds one of the keys, and a dot-less argument throws. `requirePermission(...keys)` is the route guard built on it and refuses, when the router is built, a key outside the catalog or a role code; `authorizePermission` is the same guard under its old name. New checks use `can` / `requirePermission`, never a role code. `npm run ratchet:permissions` (`scripts/permission-ratchet.ts`, baseline `permission-ratchet-baseline.json`, run by Vitest `permissionRatchet.test.ts`) fails when a production file gains a role-code literal, a permission-shaped literal is not a catalog key, or a catalog key is checked by no server code; a lower count fails until the baseline is lowered with `-- --update`. Never raise the baseline. Buttons follow the same rule: an action is shown by the permission its API asks (`useHasPermission`), never by a role code or `*` — the customers page by `customers.manage` (its delete button by `customers.delete`, the key `DELETE /customers/:id` asks; v10.1.60, OBS-R1-41, decision ت۴۰), the documents list's row and details buttons by `accounting.treasury` (settlement), `documents.edit` (notes), `documents.delete` (void) and the workflow widget's keys (`INVOICE_ROW_ACTION_PERMISSIONS` in `src/lib/invoices/invoiceRowAccess.ts`, `WORKFLOW_WIDGET_PERMISSIONS`, `useHasAnyPermission`; v9.0.340, TD-795), a sales file's delete by `crm.delete` (the sales file lists render it only with a delete handler), the stock document form's item definition by `products.create` / `products.edit` (v9.0.148, TD-893), and the stock document page's directions, document types and submit button by the permission recording that type final asks (`stockPageAccess` in `src/lib/documents/stockDocumentAccess.ts`, built on `documentTypeRecordPermission`; a type the user cannot record is not offered and the button says which permission is missing; v9.0.241, TD-791), and the project screens (list, detail, inventory control, delivery, progress matrix, workshop schedule, material allocations) by the keys of `src/lib/permissions/projectPermissions.ts`, which their routes read too (`useProjectPermissions`; v9.0.437, TD-752). The events page opens with `events.view` (its route and API) and every change button of its sub-tabs (rules, outbox, dead letters, timeline replay, webhooks, event simulation) shows only for `events.manage`, the key each change route of `/events` asks (v9.0.435, TD-722). The top bar and the profile show the role's stored name, which `/users/my-permissions` returns to every user, the system admin included; no label is built from a role code (v9.0.149, TD-894). Code names the system admin only through `SYSTEM_ADMIN_ROLE` (server) or `userPermissions.isAdmin` (browser), and an unknown actor is recorded as «سیستم», never as `admin` (v9.0.177, TD-896).
- **Document Permissions by Type and Status (v9.0.125, TD-541 / TD-771, product-owner decision ت۱ of package 8):** One table says which permission records a document (`documentRecordPermission` in `src/lib/permissions/documentPermissions.ts`, shared with the invoice form): a sales document (`invoice`, `proforma`, `return`) as draft or proforma needs `documents.create`, final or finalized `documents.finalize`; a warehouse document `warehouse.in` / `warehouse.out` by the stock direction of its type (`stockDirectionOf` in `src/services/documents/documentRecordRule.ts`, the function the services move stock with; v9.0.238, TD-770); a stock count `audit.apply`; a production receipt `warehouse.production_receipt` (v10.1.6, N-06). `POST /documents`, `PUT /documents/:id/finalize` and the workflow approval step (the document action's `requiredPermissions` reads the document) check it with `can`, never a role code. A draft stays a draft; an `invoice` proforma recorded by a user without `documents.finalize` is stored as type `proforma`, as before. Migration 0063 gave `documents.finalize` to the seed manager, warehouse keeper, accountant and CFO and to roles expanded from `*`, logged in `activity_logs`. Deleting a party needs `customers.delete` (requires `customers.view`) and the catalog has no `audit.create`, which opened nothing: migration 0123 gave `customers.delete` to every role holding `customers.manage` and removed `audit.create` from every role, each change logged in `activity_logs` (v10.1.60, OBS-R1-41 / OBS-PR-13, decision ت۴۰).
- **Personnel and Payslip Bank Details by Permission (v9.0.126, TD-882, permission model §4.2):** Card, Sheba and account number and the Nobitex username are unmasked only for the system admin, the record's own linked user, and holders of `personnel.view_sensitive` or `personnel.manage` (personnel dossier) or `payroll.view_sensitive` or `personnel.manage` (payslips) — `canAccessSensitivePersonnelData` / `canAccessSensitivePayrollData` in `src/lib/piiMasker.ts`, through `can`; no role code and no `*` opens them. Migration 0064 gave both keys to the seed manager and the other key to roles holding one of them, logged in `activity_logs`.
- **Notification Recipients by Permission (v9.0.127, TD-883, permission model §4.2):** An event rule's in-app notification goes to one user (`targetUserId`), to the holders of a catalog permission (`targetPermission`: the system admin and every non-deleted user whose role holds the key, `permissionHolderUserIds` in `src/services/notifications/notificationRecipients.ts`; an unknown key is refused with 422), or to a role the admin chose in the rule (`targetRole`, data); without a target to the system admin. The default reorder alert targets `warehouse.view`, a won sales lead notifies the holders of `crm.manage` except its sender, and the rule editor picks the permission from the catalog. Code never names a recipient role other than `SYSTEM_ADMIN_ROLE`; rules already stored were left as they are.
- **Due Follow-up Reminders (v9.0.433, TD-709):** the notification bell reminds a user only of their own due follow-ups (`generateCrmDueReminders` / `ownDueFollowupCondition` in `src/services/notifications/crmDueReminders.ts`, on the follow-up condition of TD-428), scanning them at most once an hour per user (`generateCrmDueRemindersThrottled`, the workshop reminder's throttle with its own map; v10.1.62, OBS-R2-85, decision ت۴۰): the assigned personnel's linked user (`personnel.user_id`), else, for a legacy row without a personnel id, the trimmed assignee (or logger when empty) equal to the user's full name or username; never «contains». Dismissing a notification (`DELETE /notifications/:id`) keeps its row and sets `notifications.dismissed_at`; the bell list and the unread count (in SQL) skip dismissed rows, and a due reminder is inserted with ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING on the partial unique index `uq_notifications_due_reminder` (user, type, link of `crm_due_task` rows, dismissed ones included; migration 0089, created only on data without duplicates, which the financial health check lists as `notification_reminder_uniqueness`), so a dismissed reminder never comes back and concurrent bell requests make one (v9.0.434, TD-717, product-owner decision ت۸).
- **Read Scope by Section Permission (v7.0.53, audit P2-10, product-owner decision — existing permissions only):** Global search returns each section only to holders of its view permission (items `products.view`, customers `customers.view`, documents/invoices `documents.view`, projects `projects.view`; a section without permission is empty, never an error); it folds Arabic «ي» / «ى» / «ك» and Persian and Arabic digits on both sides with `normalizeSearchText` (`src/lib/search/searchText.ts`, also used by `SearchableSelect`) and ranks each section exact match, then starts with, then contains (v9.0.291, TD-675); its text is at most `GLOBAL_SEARCH_MAX_LENGTH` (100) characters, and a query of at least four digits also finds customers by the TD-296 phone key (v10.1.55, OBS-R2-99). Kardex list `GET /api/transactions`: `warehouse.view` OR `accounting.view`. Warehouse dashboard statistics (`/api/stats`, `/api/dashboard-bi-stats`) and the «وضعیت انبار» page: `reports.view` OR `warehouse.view` (v7.0.55). Menu items and page routes use the same permissions as their API: menu links, page routes (`ProtectedRoute page=`), dashboard shortcuts, the accounting page tabs and the settings tabs read one table, `PAGE_ACCESS` / `SETTINGS_TAB_ACCESS` in `src/lib/permissions/pageAccess.ts`, where each page names its main API; every key that opens a page or tab passes the guards of those APIs and a system-admin page or tab has system-admin APIs (`sec_page_access_matches_api_td_668`, Vitest `pageAccessTable.test.tsx`), a settings tab the user cannot open is not rendered even from `?tab=`, and the WooCommerce tab edits the store URL and keys only for the system admin (`SYSTEM_ADMIN_SETTING_KEYS` in `src/lib/settings/settingKeyAccess.ts`, shared with `SystemSettingsService`) (v9.0.131, TD-668, product-owner decision ت۲ of package 16). A key may name route parameters (`:name`, and `:name?` for an optional segment): `pageAccessRule` reads the key itself first, then the address without its query string, then matches a real address against the parametrised keys, so `/projects/:projectId/:tab?` and `/projects/7/progress` both reach the project row (v10.2.9, TD-1311). A new page gets a row there with its main API; a tab inside a page is shown by the keys of its own API too (the «تخصیص مواد به پروژه‌ها» tab of `/audit` by `READ_PERMISSIONS.bomAllocations`, the guard of `GET /inventory/allocations`; v9.0.415, TD-760). There is no per-role menu hiding: the menu is built from permissions only, and the retired `menu_visibility` setting is ignored on save (v9.0.132, TD-884, permission model decision ت۱۱). The workflow widget `GET /workflow/instance/:entityType/:entityId` also requires the entity's own read permission (`WORKFLOW_ENTITY_READ_PERMISSIONS` in `src/lib/recordReadPermissions.ts`: document, journal voucher, item, bank account, purchase requisition; else 403; v9.0.38, TD-458, product-owner decision ت۹). Daily work log statistics count only the logs the user may see in the list (`canSeeDailyLog`, v8.0.125, TD-406). Reviewing, editing or deleting another user's daily work log and reading private ones need `daily_logs.manage_all` (`canManageAllDailyLogs` in `src/services/dailyLogs/dailyLogAccess.ts`, never a role code; the review route is guarded by it), and every daily log write runs in one transaction with its notifications and its audit row (`src/services/dailyLogs/dailyLogWrite.service.ts`; v9.0.231, TD-626, product-owner decision ت۱). A mention notifies only a mentioned user who may read the log (`mentionNotifies` in `src/lib/dailyLogs/dailyLogVisibility.ts`), so a private log notifies nobody and the form says so; an edit notifies only new mentions (v9.0.233, TD-633, product-owner decision ت۲). Mentioned and allowed users and the project of a daily log are positive ids (400 otherwise) of existing, non-deleted rows (422 `DAILY_LOG_USER_NOT_FOUND` / `DAILY_LOG_PROJECT_NOT_FOUND`, nothing written), and the project name is the project's title (v9.0.234, TD-629). A daily log is never public: its visibility is `mentioned_only` (default), `private`, `managers` or `custom` (`DAILY_LOG_VISIBILITIES`, CHECK `chk_daily_work_logs_visibility`), the form offers the first two, and migration 0075 moved public, `all` and empty logs to `mentioned_only` with the old value recorded in `daily_log_visibility_repairs` (v9.0.236, TD-900, product-owner decision ت۷). The daily log list and its statistics are read in SQL with the visibility rule (`listDailyLogs` / `dailyLogStats` in `src/services/dailyLogs/dailyLogRead.service.ts`): the list is paged (`page`, `limit` at most 100, `{ data, total, page, limit }`), today's hours count only logs whose work date is today, and the management summary reads one day or one Jalali month (v9.0.249, TD-630). A daily log's title (200), text (20,000), tags (20 of at most 50 characters) and manager notes (20,000) are capped by `src/lib/dailyLogs/dailyLogLimits.ts`, shared by the schemas and the form (v9.0.258, TD-644). Its start and end are times of day (Persian digits read) with the end after the start, so an overnight shift is two logs (422 `DAILY_LOG_WORK_TIME_INVALID`, `workTimeError` / `workHoursBetween` in `src/lib/dailyLogs/workHours.ts`, shared with the form; v9.0.259, TD-634, product-owner decision ت۵). Its work mode is `onsite` or `remote` (`DAILY_LOG_WORK_MODES` in `src/lib/dailyLogs/workMode.ts`); legacy leave, mission and hybrid logs are kept, the summary counts them as «سایر» and a leave's hours are not work hours (v9.0.260, TD-635, product-owner decision ت۳). User input in LIKE / ILIKE patterns goes through `containsLikePattern` / `startsWithLikePattern` / `escapeLikePattern` (`src/lib/sqlLike.ts`); a unit test rejects `%${…}`, `'%' +` and template literals passed straight to `like` / `ilike` (v7.0.57, TD-222).
- **Read Permissions for All Read Routes (v7.0.59, TD-223, product-owner decision — existing permissions only):** Every read route that returns business data is guarded with `authorizePermission(...READ_PERMISSIONS.<section>)` from `src/lib/recordReadPermissions.ts` (any one permission suffices: the section's view permission plus the permissions of pages/forms that pick from that list — add a page's visibility permission there when a new page consumes a list; a section with an options endpoint gives forms that endpoint instead, see Pick Lists for Forms). Payroll amounts: `piecework.payroll` / `piecework.pay` / `personnel.manage` / `accounting.treasury` (own payslips via `/piecework/payrolls/mine`; `piecework.pay` since v9.0.320, TD-805). The personnel list and detail (`GET /personnel`, `/personnel/:id`) give pick-list readers only id, names, personnel code, job title, status and linked user id; the full dossier only to `personnel.view` / `personnel.manage`, the salary only to the payroll-amount permissions, and the Nobitex password only in the detail (`personnelReadScope` / `scopePersonnelRow` in `src/services/personnel/personnelFieldScope.ts`; v9.0.23, TD-434, product-owner decision D1). The list is searched (only over the fields its reader sees, spaces, zero-width marks, «-» and «_» dropped and «ة» / «ۀ» read as «ه» on both sides, `compactSearchText` / `compactFoldedSearchSql`) and filtered in SQL (`activeOnly=1` leaves out personnel who left): with `page` it answers one page `{ data, total, page, limit, stats }` and without it the pick-list array, uncapped without a `limit` and capped at `PERSONNEL_PICK_REQUEST_LIMIT` (`listPersonnelRows` in `src/services/personnel/personnelList.ts`, contract `src/lib/personnel/personnelList.ts`); the personnel page reads one page (`usePersonnelPageQuery`, its range label following the rows shown) and every personnel only while its form or Excel window is open, whose user picker and file upload wait for that list; a pick list keeps `PERSONNEL_PICK_LIMIT` (5000, newest first; the sales seller list only personnel who have not left) and asks one row more, so only a list the server cut says so in Persian, once per session (`takePersonnelPick`), and a voucher row or sales file whose personnel the list does not hold keeps it as its chosen option with its stored name, else the name read by id (`usePersonnelPickName`) (v10.2.14, TD-1321). A system user is linked to at most one active personnel and only when the user exists and is not deleted: `resolvePersonnelUserLink` (`src/services/personnel/personnelUserLink.ts`) locks the user row in the personnel save transaction (422 invalid user, 409 linked elsewhere), backed by the partial unique index `uq_personnel_user_active` (migration 0053, created only on data without duplicates; the financial health check lists duplicates as `personnel_user_link_uniqueness`), and `/piecework/payrolls/mine` refuses a user with a legacy duplicate link with 409 (v9.0.24, TD-435, product-owner decision D2). In the personnel Excel import an empty gender, employment-status or nationality cell leaves an existing personnel's value unchanged and gives a new personnel the default (`src/lib/personnel/personnelImportCells.ts`, shared by the server and the preview; v9.0.25, TD-436, product-owner decision D3). Every personnel create, edit, delete and Excel row writes its own audit row with `req` and `details` — `after` on create, `before` on delete, only the changed fields on edit (`personnelAuditChanges` in `src/services/personnel/personnelAudit.ts`); the Nobitex password never enters the audit, only `nobitexPasswordChanged` (v9.0.26, TD-437). A personnel's `monthlySalary` goes through `decimalInput` and is non-negative; an empty form field is 0 and a missing field on edit keeps the current salary (v9.0.27, TD-438). A non-empty personnel code is unique among active personnel: the key is `lower(btrim(personnel_code))`, checked by `assertPersonnelCodeAvailable` (`src/services/personnel/personnelCode.ts`, also the Excel import's lookup key) and enforced by the partial unique index `uq_personnel_code_active` (migration 0054, created only on data without duplicates; the financial health check lists duplicates as `personnel_code_uniqueness`) (v9.0.28, TD-439, product-owner decision D6). A non-empty national ID is unique among active personnel the same way, on create, edit (only when it changes) and each Excel row: key `btrim(national_id)`, `assertPersonnelNationalIdAvailable` (`src/services/personnel/personnelNationalIdUnique.ts`, 409 `PERSONNEL_NATIONAL_ID_TAKEN`), partial unique index `uq_personnel_national_id_active` (migration 0122, created only on data without duplicates; the health check lists them as `personnel_national_id_uniqueness`), and once the duplicates are gone the system admin builds a skipped index, this one or `uq_personnel_code_active`, from the missing-constraints card (`CONDITIONAL_CONSTRAINT_RULES`) (v10.1.57, OBS-R2-56, product-owner decision ت۴۰). A personnel is never deleted while it has open business (v9.0.29, TD-441, product-owner decision D4): `DELETE /personnel/:id` locks the personnel row and `assertPersonnelDeletable` (`src/services/personnel/personnelDeleteGuard.ts`) refuses with 409 `PERSONNEL_HAS_OPEN_ITEMS` and Persian reasons a draft, approved or partially paid payroll, a work log free of a live payroll, a nonzero approved/permanent balance in any currency on non-temporary accounts of the personnel's detailed rows, or a draft voucher row; the message suggests «قطع همکاری». `PUT /personnel/:id` requires the record's `version` (400 without it) and writes with the version in its WHERE (409 `OCC_CONFLICT` when stale or changed meanwhile); the Excel import advances the version too (v9.0.30, TD-442, product-owner decision D5). Persian personnel UI text has no English words or «اکانت» / «کلیپ‌بورد» (Vitest `personnelWording.test.ts`, v9.0.31, TD-440). A party's bank details (`bankInfo`: bank, account, Sheba, card) go only to admins and holders of `customers.view`, `customers.manage` or any `accounting.*` permission (`PARTY_BANK_INFO_PERMISSIONS` / `partyRowsForUser` in `src/services/customers/partyBankInfoAccess.ts`); `GET /customers` (list and `export=true`) and «تبدیل به مشتری» drop them for every other reader (v9.0.21, TD-433, product-owner decision ت۶). The full treasury account list (`GET /accounting/bank-accounts`, `/accounting/banks`) with account, card and Sheba numbers and balances goes only to treasury readers (`READ_PERMISSIONS.bankAccounts`: `accounting.treasury`, `accounting.reports`, `accounting.view`); forms that only pick an account read `GET /accounting/bank-accounts/options` (id, code, title, type, bank name, currency, `hasLedgerAccount`; `READ_PERMISSIONS.bankAccountOptions`, `getBankAccountOptions` in `src/services/accounting/treasury/bankAccountOptions.ts`) (v9.0.97, TD-505, product-owner decision ت۷). Treasury receipts, payments, bank transfers, a cheque's issue date and every cheque action date go through `resolveTreasuryWriteDate` (`src/services/accounting/treasury/treasuryDate.ts`): empty is the business today, the date is read with `requireStorageDate` (a non-existent day such as 1404/12/30 is 422, never moved to another day or fiscal year) and a date after the business today is 422 `TREASURY_DATE_IN_FUTURE`; a cheque's due date may be in the future. The cheque status form sends the action date (default today). `jalaliToIsoDate` / `normalizeDateToIso` calls in `MONEY_STOCK_PATHS` only decrease (Vitest `looseDateConverters.test.ts`) (v9.0.98, TD-506 / TD-669, product-owner decision ت۶). A treasury account's currency comes from `TREASURY_CURRENCIES` (`src/lib/treasury/treasuryCurrency.ts`, the §6 list; the bank account form offers it, another code is 422 `BANK_ACCOUNT_CURRENCY_INVALID`) and is fixed once the account has a treasury row (voided ones included), a cheque or an opening balance: `updateBankAccount` refuses a change under the bank row lock with 422 `BANK_ACCOUNT_CURRENCY_LOCKED` (`assertBankCurrencyChangeAllowed` in `src/services/accounting/treasury/bankAccountCurrency.ts`; v9.0.99, TD-508, product-owner decision ت۵). Its ledger account is fixed after the same first movement too (422 `BANK_ACCOUNT_LEDGER_LOCKED`, `assertBankLedgerChangeAllowed`, same file; an account without a ledger account may take one; v10.0.254, TD-969, product-owner decision of 17 Mehr 1405). An account that issued a paid cheque not yet cleared (received, in the treasury, in the safe or in collection) keeps a bank or cash type, since that cheque clears only from it (422 `BANK_ACCOUNT_TYPE_HAS_OPEN_PAID_CHEQUES`, `assertBankTypeChangeKeepsPaidCheques`, same file; v10.1.66, OBS-R1-60). A treasury account's ledger account, on create and when it changes, is an active, non-deleted subsidiary under general account 10 «موجودی نقد و بانک» (`requireBankLedgerAccount` in `src/services/accounting/treasury/bankLinks.ts`, else 422 `BANK_LEDGER_ACCOUNT_INVALID`), and a cheque's bank account is an active, non-deleted treasury account read `FOR SHARE` (`requireChequeBankAccount`, else 422 `CHEQUE_BANK_ACCOUNT_INVALID`) (v9.0.100, TD-510). Treasury and cheque amounts, opening balances and exchange rates in `src/routes/accounting/accounting.schemas.ts` use `decimalInput` (a positive amount required) and their currency is `TREASURY_CURRENCIES` after `normalizeTreasuryCurrency` (v9.0.101, TD-514). Bank balances (`computeBankBalances`) are summed in SQL with `GROUP BY`, reading only the treasury accounts' ledger accounts and bank-tagged rows; `GET /accounting/treasury` with `page` / `limit` returns `{ data, total, page, limit }` filtered by type, method, account, date and search in SQL, with each row's running balance for a single-account filter (`pageTreasuryTransactions` in `src/services/accounting/treasury/treasuryTransactionList.ts`); the running balance counts the rows the bank's treasury balance counts — a voided row on its own date and its reversal on the void date, never a legacy cheque-method row — so the last row equals that balance (v9.0.125, TD-860, product-owner decision), and the treasury page and the reconciliation window never load the whole list (v9.0.102, TD-509). Bank statement reconciliation (`POST /accounting/treasury/reconcile`, `reconcileTreasuryRows` in `src/services/accounting/treasury/bankReconciliation.ts`) refuses an id that is missing, of another bank or voided with 422 `TREASURY_RECONCILE_ROWS_INVALID` listing them and writes nothing; it writes and audits (with `tx`) only the rows whose state changes, and `reconciled_at` is the server UTC timestamp (v9.0.103, TD-511). A cleared cheque is a reconciliation candidate too: `GET /accounting/treasury/reconcile-cheques` lists the bank's cleared cheques with their clearing date (`listReconcilableCheques`, contract `src/lib/treasury/reconcilableCheque.ts`) and the same route reconciles them with `chequeIds` (`cheques.reconciled` / `reconciled_at` / `reconciled_batch`, migration 0108; v10.0.254, TD-971). A cheque status change and a cheque delete are audited inside their transaction with `before` / `after` (status, its Persian label from `CHEQUE_STATUS_LABELS` in `src/lib/treasury/chequeTransitions.ts`, bank account) and the vouchers issued, deleted or reversed (`src/services/accounting/treasury/chequeAudit.ts`; v9.0.104, TD-512). A treasury receipt or payment, a bank transfer, a treasury void, a new cheque, a treasury account's create, edit and delete and the bank balance sync write their audit row in their own service transaction with the request (`src/services/accounting/treasury/treasuryAudit.ts`; a refused audit row refuses the write), never from the route after commit (v10.0.262, TD-934). Cheque messages, the default history note and the cheque page name statuses only through `chequeStatusLabel`, never their codes; the history window reads `notes` (v9.0.105, TD-513). The treasury, transfer and cheque forms and the treasury and cheque date filters use `JalaliDateInput` with ISO state; a treasury form for an account without a ledger account says registration is impossible and does not submit; the treasury Excel export uses `treasuryExportRows` / `treasuryExportFileName` (`src/lib/treasury/treasuryExport.ts`: Persian party type and method, Jalali file date) (v9.0.106, TD-515). Full user and role directory: `users.manage` / `roles.manage` / `personnel.manage` / `workflow.manage` / `settings.manage`; the «مدیریت کاربران و نقش‌ها» page and menu open for `users.manage` or `roles.manage`, each tab offers only its own permission's actions, and the forms apply the TD-520 grant rule (only roles whose keys the user all holds, own account's role locked, no unheld key ticked; v9.0.130, TD-525, product-owner decision ت۳); `/users/list-simple` (id, user name for mentions, full name, picture and `role_name`, the role's stored Persian name — never the role code; v9.0.223, TD-534), categories, warehouses and the user's own data stay login-only, and so does `GET /settings`, which gives the system admin and holders of `settings.manage` every setting (secrets masked for all but the system admin) and any other user only the shared settings (company, currency, time zone, price lists, movement days, project presets), plus the WooCommerce connection with its keys masked for `woocommerce.view` (`isSettingReadable` in `src/lib/settings/settingReadScope.ts`; v10.1.58, OBS-R2-96, decision ت۴۰).
- **Pick Lists for Forms (v9.0.137, TD-887, permission model decision ت۱۰):** A form that picks a record of another section reads that section's options endpoint, which carries only what picking needs (URLs and fields in `src/lib/permissions/pickLists.ts`, permissions in `READ_PERMISSIONS`); the section's full list opens only with the section's own keys. Vitest `pickLists.test.ts` fails when a full list gains another section's key or browser code outside the section fetches the full list. Customers: `GET /customers/options` (contact card without notes or version; bank details by the TD-433 rule) for document, warehouse, CRM, project, procurement, reorder and accounting forms; `GET /customers` needs `customers.view`. Items (v9.0.138, TD-888): `GET /items/options` (code, name, unit, category, attributes and per-warehouse stock; the average cost only for `products.view`) for document, warehouse, stock count, project, procurement, CRM and transfer forms; `GET /items` and `/items/next-code` need `products.view` or `products.edit_price`, `/items/reorder-alerts` the page's keys. Projects (v9.0.139, TD-889): `GET /projects/options` (code, title, status, customer name) for stock document, project inventory, material allocation, piecework and daily log forms; `GET /projects` needs `projects.view` and is one summary page (`page`, `limit` default 50 and at most 200, `search`, `status`, `priority` in SQL; `{ data, total, page, limit, statusCounts }`, each row only `PROJECT_LIST_ROW_FIELDS` and its stage summary; `listProjectPage` in `src/services/projects/projectList.ts`, contract `src/lib/projects/projectList.ts`; v9.0.412, TD-743), whose products, inventory control, schedule and attachments come only with the project record; a project record and its attachments need `projects.view` or `warehouse.view` (project inventory page). Documents (v9.0.140, TD-890): the full list needs a `documents.*` view, create or edit key and always answers one page (`page`, `limit`; default and `limit=0` give 50, `{ data, total, page, limit, totalPages }`; the whole list only with `export=true`; the stock count history and transfer tabs page through it with `typedDocumentListUrl` in `src/lib/documents/documentListPage.ts`; v9.0.339, TD-787); a record and its attachments also open for `workflow.view`; `audit.view` lists and reads only stock count and transfer documents (`readableDocumentTypes` in `src/services/documents/documentReadScope.ts`, else 403 `DOCUMENT_TYPE_NOT_READABLE`); the stock count sheet needs `audit.view`; a party's documents `documents.view`, `customers.view` or `crm.view`; a document's treasury rows (`settlements`: number, method, tracking number, bank account, description) go only to treasury readers (`READ_PERMISSIONS.treasuryTransactions`), other readers get its paid amount, balance and settlement status (`documentForReader` in `src/services/documents/documentReadScope.ts`; v9.0.335, TD-781, product-owner decision ت۸ of package 8). Item prices (v9.0.141, TD-891): all prices and the price history need `products.view` or `products.edit_price`; one item's sale prices also open for `documents.create` / `documents.edit` (invoice and remittance form). Piecework (v9.0.142, TD-892): every personnel's work logs need `piecework.view`, `piecework.log` or `personnel.manage`; `projects.view` reads only one project's logs by its id (else 403 `PIECEWORK_LOGS_PROJECT_ONLY`); a personnel's custom rates (`GET /piecework/personnel-rates/:id`) need `piecework.view`, `piecework.log`, `piecework.manage_tasks` or a payroll amount key (`READ_PERMISSIONS.payrolls`), and `projects.view` / `settings.manage` read only task titles and categories (v9.0.321, TD-806, product-owner decision ت۳).
- **Audit Logging & Snapshots:** All state mutations (customers, items, prices, documents, stocks, permissions) MUST log via `logActivity` in `src/lib/auditLogger.ts` with before/after snapshots. Every audit row carries the trace id of the request that wrote it (`activity_logs.trace_id`, migration 0102, filled by `logActivity` from `req.requestId` or the request context; v10.0.198, TD-963), the id of the error answer and the log files. A document's create, edit, finalize, notes change and void each write one audit row with the change's own `tx`, holding the stored document before and after it (`documentAuditSnapshot` / `documentAuditDetails` in `src/services/documents/documentAudit.ts`; the edit reads «before» under the document row lock, `updateDocument` / `updateDocumentNotes` / `finalizeDocument` return the pair, a finalize of an already final document writes none, and the void's single `DELETE` row is written by `deleteDocument` with the caller's actor and note) (v9.0.337, TD-785). Order delivery and «دریافت کالا» write the same finalize row, with the same `tx`, for each order they finalize (entity «اسناد انبار», the document id, `documentAuditDetails` of the pair `finalizeDocument` returns, plus their `operation`), so delivery writes one row per order (v9.0.458, TD-917). The audit purge (`POST /activity-logs/purge`, system admin) deletes only rows older than the fixed 90 days whose entity is one of the operational sections in `PURGEABLE_AUDIT_ENTITIES` and whose action is not in `CRITICAL_AUDIT_ACTIONS` (`src/lib/audit/auditRetention.ts`, shared with the purge form); every other entity name (financial, security, role and user events, old or unknown names) is never purged, and the API has no option to turn that off or to go below 90 days. Every entity name the code writes is classified there (Vitest `auditEntityRetention.test.ts`), so a new name is classified in the same change (v9.0.212, TD-522, product-owner decision ت۴).
- **Third-Party Secrets Encrypted at Rest (v7.0.139, TD-189, product-owner decision):** Passwords of third-party accounts (the personnel Nobitex password) are stored only through `encryptSecret` / read through `decryptSecret` (`src/lib/secretBox.ts`, AES-256-GCM, key `ERP_SECRETS_KEY` separate from `JWT_SECRET`, format `enc:v1:`). Without the key a new secret is refused (503), never stored in plain text; ciphertext never leaves the server; the personnel form and detail view show the Nobitex password masked until «نمایش رمز» (`NobitexPasswordFields.tsx`; v10.0.264, OBS-R2-54); plain-text legacy values are encrypted with `npm run secrets:encrypt -- --apply`. `install.sh` writes a random `ERP_SECRETS_KEY` and `ERP_WEBHOOK_SECRET_TOKEN` through `scripts/ensure-env-secrets.sh` (adds only missing keys; `update.sh` runs it for `ERP_SECRETS_KEY` alone before its backup when that key is missing, an `export` or indented line counting as present, v10.1.68, TD-1286) and `go-live-verify.sh` fails without the key (v9.0.187, TD-609); the financial health check warns while the key is missing (`secrets_key_missing` in `secrets_at_rest`, also with no stored secret). The same rule covers integration secrets (v9.0.361, TD-898, product-owner decision ت۷): the WooCommerce consumer key, consumer secret and webhook secret (`ENCRYPTED_SETTING_KEYS` in `src/services/settings/settingSecrets.ts`) and each webhook subscription's signing key and custom header values (`src/services/events/webhookSecretStorage.ts`; header names stay plain), and an action rule's webhook token and header values (`actionConfigJson.secretToken` / `headers`, `sealRuleConfig` / `openRuleConfig` in the same file; v10.1.59, OBS-PR-07, decision ت۴۰), are written with `encryptSecret` and opened only inside the server; a rule whose token or header the key cannot open fails without a retry, and the financial health check counts plain rule values with the webhooks (`secrets_at_rest`); the system admin's settings answer carries them decrypted (one that cannot be decrypted as «********»). A value the current key cannot decrypt is never sent: the webhook delivery is recorded as failed without a retry, the webhook ping and the WooCommerce connection read are 503 `INTEGRATION_SECRET_UNREADABLE`, and the WooCommerce webhook fails. `npm run secrets:encrypt` also encrypts their legacy plain values (`IntegrationSecretEncryptionService`), and the CI test jobs run with a test `ERP_SECRETS_KEY`.
- **Route Access Policy (v8.0.55, TD-298, V8 area H):** The route → permission table is built from the Express routers (`buildRouteGuardTable` in `src/lib/routeGuardTable.ts`; `npm run routes:permissions` writes `docs/security/ROUTE_PERMISSIONS.md`). Test `sec_route_access_policy_td_298` requires: every unauthenticated route is in `PUBLIC_ROUTES` and every login-only route in `LOGIN_ONLY_ROUTES` (`src/tests/security/routeAccessPolicy.ts`; add a route there only when it reads or writes the user's own data, is a public pick list, or checks permission in its handler); no POST/PUT/PATCH/DELETE route passes with a `*.view` permission alone; every permission key in a guard is in `PERMISSION_CATALOG`. Only an admin grants or removes the `admin` role or changes or deletes an admin account (TD-299); a non-admin holder of `roles.manage` never edits its own role and gives a new or another role only keys it holds, and a non-admin holder of `users.manage` never changes its own account's role, gives a user only a role whose keys it all holds, and changes (password included) or deletes no account whose role holds a key it lacks (403 `OWN_ROLE_CHANGE_REFUSED` / `GRANT_BEYOND_OWN_PERMISSIONS`, `src/services/users/grantBoundary.ts`, rule shared in `src/lib/permissions/grantBoundary.ts`; v9.0.129, TD-520, product-owner decision ت۳); the last active admin is neither deleted nor moved to another role: user edit and delete run in one transaction under the admin-set lock (`lockSystemAdminSet`, advisory key 91030) and `assertAnotherActiveAdmin` (`src/services/users/lastAdminGuard.ts`) refuses with 409 (v9.0.75, TD-524); a username starting with `test_`, `e2e_` or `testuser_` (any case, `isSyntheticTestUsername` in `src/lib/syntheticUsers.ts`) is refused by `POST /users` and `/setup` with 422, the user lists show every non-deleted user, only setup detection skips such names, and the financial health check lists active ones as `synthetic_test_users` (v9.0.76, TD-521); a new username (`POST /users`, `/setup`, the user form and the setup wizard) is 3 to 50 Latin letters, digits, «.», «_» or «-» (`usernameFormatError` in `src/lib/users/usernameRules.ts`, else 422 `USERNAME_FORMAT_INVALID`) and is unique by `lower(btrim(username))`, so «Ali» beside «ali» is taken: a new user takes no key another user holds, deleted ones included (TD-519), a deleted user is not restored while a live user holds its key (409 `USERNAME_HELD_BY_LIVE_USER`), and the partial unique index `uq_users_username_key` over live users (migration 0124, built only on clean data) backs it; the financial health check lists live duplicates as `username_case_uniqueness` until one account is deleted; old usernames are never changed and log in as stored (v10.1.61, OBS-R1-30, decision ت۴۰); a new user always gets a new id: `POST /users` refuses the username of a deleted user with 409 `USERNAME_OF_DELETED_USER` naming that user and leaves the deleted row as it is, and restoring that same person is the separate `POST /users/:id/restore` (`users.manage`; a new role and a temporary password that must be changed, a new token version, the admin-account and TD-520 rules; the user form offers it) (v9.0.178, TD-519, product-owner decision ت۲); a role gets only catalog permission keys (TD-304). A user's role code references its role (`fk_users_role` on `roles.code`, ON UPDATE CASCADE, migration 0100); only a soft-deleted user may hold no role (`chk_users_role_active`), and deleting a role clears the role of its soft-deleted users in the delete transaction and names them in the audit row (`clearedDeletedUserIds`) (v10.0.164, TD-962, product-owner decision of 10-09 item 7). A role is created, edited and deleted only through `src/services/users/roleAdmin.service.ts`, in one transaction under the role row lock with its audit row written with the same `tx`, so a refused audit row changes nothing (v10.0.49, part of TD-960).
- **Log Sanitization:** Sensitive fields (`password`, `token`, `secret`, `jwt`, `cookie`) MUST be sanitized to `[PROTECTED]`. In audit details (`sanitizeSensitiveData` / `sanitizeAuditValue` in `src/lib/audit/auditSanitizer.ts`) a secret key is recognised by "contains" in any spelling (`password`, `secret`, `token`, `apiKey`, `cookie`, `jwt`, `authorization`; camelCase, snake_case, kebab-case) and its text value becomes `[PROTECTED]` (boolean and numeric flags stay), and card, account and Sheba numbers keep only their last four digits (`****5678`), field-by-field changes included, so a Sheba change stays visible (v9.0.213, TD-530, product-owner decision ت۷).
- **Soft Deletes:** Always filter with `.where(eq(table.isDeleted, 0))` on reads — including `journal_voucher_items.is_deleted`: editing or re-syncing a draft voucher soft-deletes its old rows, which stay under the voucher after approval (v8.0.15, TD-270).

## 6. Localization & UI Guidelines
- **Dates & Numbers:** Server dates must use `src/lib/businessClock.ts`. UI dates are always Jalali (`formatPersianDate`, `JalaliDateInput`; storage rule in §1.10). Numbers formatted via `formatPersianPrice` or `formatPersianNumber`, which write the Persian thousands separator «٬» and decimal separator «٫» (v9.0.246, TD-687); browser number parsers (`parseCleanNumber` and the amount field, `src/lib/amountText.ts`) read them through `normalizeDecimalString` and never turn an invalid entry into 0 (v9.0.244, TD-666). A number or amount field (`DecimalInput`, `FinancialAmountInput`) shows Persian digits, «٫» and «٬» between three-digit groups while typing, with any keyboard, keeps the caret after the same digit (and after a «٬» the user typed) and sends the Latin value (`decimalDisplayText` / `persianNumberText` in `src/lib/numberFieldDisplay.ts`; an identifier field takes `grouped={false}`); only the separators the field put there itself are dropped before reading (`withoutFieldSeparators`; an edit that inserts a separator keeps them all for the check and an accepted number is kept without separators, so «۱٬۲۵۰٬۰۰۰» typed key by key is 1250000), so a «٬» the user types or pastes outside three-digit groups («۱۲٬۵») is an error in the number and the amount field alike, never 125 (the amount field keeps it on the field like the number field and reports 0, meaning empty while the field is invalid, never a number; it blocks the form, Ctrl+S of the voucher form included, and any other value set from outside clears it, while a reset to the same 0 it reported keeps it, so a list of such fields keys its rows with a browser-only `rowKey` (`src/lib/rowKey.ts`) that no draft or request carries and that a restored draft renews; v10.2.4, TD-1291), and a screen picks a date only through `JalaliDateInput`, the one file that imports `react-multi-date-picker` (v10.1.72, TD-1288; Vitest `persianNumberFieldsTd1288.test.tsx`). Iranian identifiers (national ID, phones) preserve leading zeros via `formatPersianPhone` and `formatPersianNationalId`. A national ID is exactly ten digits with a valid check digit and a form or the server never zero-pads it (`requirePersonnelNationalId`, 422 `NATIONAL_ID_INVALID`); only the Excel import pads 8 or 9 digits and lists the row as a warning (`readImportedNationalId` in `src/lib/personnel/nationalIdCell.ts`; v9.0.248, TD-673, product-owner decision ت۵).
- **Dynamic Currencies:** Respect `currency` on records (IRR, USD, EUR, AED, GBP); do not hardcode "ریال". The `currency` setting is only the display unit of rial amounts, IRR or TOMAN (toman = rial ÷ 10, label «تومان»): a rial amount is shown through `useRialDisplay` / `rialDisplayOf` (`src/lib/rialDisplay.ts`: `number`, `amount`, `money(amount, currency)`), a record with its own foreign currency keeps it, amount inputs and Excel exports stay in rial, and no component labels or formats an amount with the setting itself (Vitest `rialDisplayUnitTd667.test.tsx`; v9.0.275, TD-667, product-owner decision ت۱). Business settings are checked on save with the rules in `src/lib/settings/settingValues.ts`, shared by the server and the settings form: the movement days are whole numbers from 1 to 3650 with fast < slow < dead (422 `SETTING_MOVEMENT_DAYS_INVALID`, checked together with the stored values), `currency` is IRR or TOMAN (422 `SETTING_CURRENCY_INVALID`), and the dashboard reads a stored invalid set as the defaults 30 / 90 / 180 (`resolveMovementDays`) (v9.0.276, TD-672, product-owner decision ت۴).
- **«ارتباط با مشتری», Not «CRM» (v9.0.20, TD-432, product-owner decision ت۷):** Persian UI text never says «CRM»; package-9 text uses «پیگیری»، «پرونده فروش»، «اشاره» and «قیف فروش», never «تسک»، «لید»، «منشن» or «کانبان». Stored keys keep their text (audit entity names «فرصت فروش CRM» / «اقدام و تماس CRM», account 7006). Enforced by Vitest `crmWording.test.ts`. Workflow UI text and messages follow the owner glossary (ت۱۰): «گردش کار», «مهلت انجام», «گام», «اقدام», «آغاز / میانی / پایان», «یک امضا», «همه اعضای نقش», «یکی از اعضا», «K امضا از N», «گلوگاه», «دارای تأخیر», «مواد اولیه», Persian role and entity names (`workflowEntityTypeLabel` in `src/lib/workflow/workflowEntityLabels.ts`) and Persian digits; a `WF_*` code goes only into the error `code` field (third argument of `NotFoundError` / `ValidationError` / `ConflictError` / `ForbiddenError`), never into the message; stored audit keys keep their text (Vitest `workflowWording.test.ts`; v9.0.65, TD-470, product-owner decision ت۱۰). The users, roles, audit log, login, setup and profile screens, the permission catalog and the package-2 server messages have no English word or transliteration («پیشخوان», «نمایه», «بخش», «تصویر نمایه», «رویداد», «دستگاه کاربر», «راه‌اندازی اولیه», «تصویر داده»; only «نشانی IP», the system name, file formats and currency codes stay Latin), show counts in Persian digits and name a role by its stored name (`roleDisplayName` in `src/lib/users/roleDisplayName.ts`, also stored as `roleName` in the login event), never its code (Vitest `packageTwoWording.test.tsx`; v9.0.224, TD-540, product-owner decision ت۸ of package 2).
- **Rial Cost Rows in Foreign Documents (v8.0.18, TD-261, product-owner decision):** In a foreign-currency voucher, rows that carry a rial Kardex cost (cost of sales, inventory, donated goods) stay in the document currency and get their own exchange rate = rial value ÷ foreign amount (`foreignCostRow` / `rowExchangeRate` in `src/services/accounting/foreignCostRow.ts`), so amount × row rate equals the Kardex value exactly; cost of sales is the sum of the inventory rows so the voucher stays balanced in its currency. Never convert a rial cost at the document rate alone. Since v10.0.12 (TD-1030) both the amount and the row rate are stored to 4 decimals, so each such row takes the 4-decimal rate nearest to rial ÷ amount at which amount × rate rounds to its rial value rounded to the rial, and the opposite row (cost of sales, work in progress, donated goods against the supplier at the document rate) is worth exactly their sum (`balancedCostRows` / `rateForRial` in `src/services/accounting/foreignCostRow.ts`): the voucher balances in rials under the TD-260 rule, so its year closes.
- **Report Currency Rule (v8.0.16, TD-260):** A voucher row's currency is its own, else its voucher's, else IRR. Reports that sum voucher rows (trial balance, account card, party ledger, project reports, financial health check) use one rule from `src/services/accounting/voucherItemAmount.ts`: the «all currencies» view converts a foreign row to IRR at that row's own exchange rate (rounded to the rial) and keeps IRR rows as they are; a single-currency view shows only that currency's rows at their own amounts. Never add a raw foreign amount to rials.
