- **Structured VAT (TD-197, v7.0.32):** Sales VAT lives only in `documents.vat_percent` / `documents.vat_amount` (resolved via `src/services/documents/documentVat.ts` on create, update and finalize). The server computes the amount from `vat_percent`; an explicit amount sent with a positive percent must equal it, else 422 `VAT_AMOUNT_MISMATCH`, and the sales invoice form sends only the percent (v8.0.104, TD-381). The percentage amount is rounded to the document currency's smallest unit (`currencyScale` in `src/lib/currencyScale.ts`: rial without decimals, other currencies two decimals; v8.0.105, TD-382). A sales return carries VAT too (v9.0.274, TD-774, decision ت۵ of package 8): with an original invoice its percent is the invoice's and its amount the invoice VAT's share of the returned net, counted over the invoice's final returns — share(n) = min(invoice VAT, invoice VAT × n ÷ invoice net rounded like TD-382), this return's VAT = share(earlier + this) − share(earlier) — so returning the whole invoice in parts gives back exactly its VAT; another percent or amount is 422 `RETURN_VAT_MISMATCH` (`resolveReturnVatFromInvoice` in `src/services/documents/salesReturnVat.ts`, on create, draft edit and finalize). A return without an original invoice takes the user's percent. The return voucher debits sales returns with the net and VAT payable (3203) with the VAT and credits the customer with both. The sales voucher reads `vat_amount` only — NEVER parse amounts, rates or taxes out of free-text `notes`, and never write VAT into notes. Payable amount = net of lines + `vat_amount` (+ `service_charge_amount`, see below).
- **Line Discount Within the Line (v8.0.103, TD-380):** A document line's discount is at most its quantity × unit price; create and update refuse a larger one with 422 `LINE_DISCOUNT_EXCEEDS_AMOUNT` (`assertLineDiscountsWithinAmount` in `src/services/documents/lineDiscount.ts`), and the sales invoice form checks the same rule before adding a line (`lineDiscountError` in `src/lib/invoices/invoiceLine.ts`).
- **Service Charges (v7.0.103, TD-191, product-owner decision «ثبت کامل»):** A sales invoice's shipping and fee charges live only in `documents.service_charge_amount` (non-negative; set by the WooCommerce sync from `shipping_lines` / `fee_lines`, while the order's `total_tax` goes to `vat_amount`, with the percent `total_tax` ÷ net × 100 to two decimals when that percent rebuilds the tax exactly, else no percent (`wooOrderVatPercent` in `src/services/woocommerce/wooOrderVat.ts`; v10.0.231, TD-936)). The sales voucher credits them to «درآمد حمل و خدمات» (`serviceRevenueAccountCode`, default 5004). Payable amount = net of lines + `vat_amount` + `service_charge_amount`; a WooCommerce order whose invoice total differs from the order `total` is rejected as a whole.
- **Structured Exchange Rate (TD-198, v7.0.63, product-owner decision):** The exchange rate of a non-IRR document (IRR per one unit of its currency) lives only in `documents.exchange_rate` and is required on create, update and finalize (`src/services/documents/documentExchangeRate.ts`). Vouchers take it via `VoucherSyncService.resolveVoucherExchangeRate` (explicit rate, document column, `exchange_rate_<cur>` setting) and never from `notes`; without a rate no voucher is issued (never rate 1). Pre-v7.0.63 documents were not migrated. Foreign-currency treasury receipts, payments and transfers follow the same rule (v8.0.20, TD-274, `resolveTreasuryExchangeRate` in `src/services/accounting/treasury/treasuryExchangeRate.ts`): explicit rate, else the rate of the invoice being settled (`documentId`, same currency), else the `exchange_rate_<cur>` setting; without a rate the transaction is refused, and its voucher rows carry the transaction's currency and rate.
- **Terminal Output Is English (owner rule t9, B01-45, v9.0.211):** everything a terminal shows is English, because terminals render Persian badly: shell and hook output, `npm run` and `scripts/*.ts` output and errors (`check:version`, `release`, `simulate:year`), server log and console lines, test names and test-runner and invariant messages, commit messages and PR titles. Persian stays in UI text, API error messages for users, the changelog, `TECH_DEBT.md`, documents and `AGENTS.md`, text a script writes into a file, and test data. `npm run ratchet:terminal-english` (`scripts/terminal-english-ratchet.ts`, baseline `terminal-english-baseline.json`, run by Vitest `terminalEnglishRatchet.test.ts`) counts per file the places that still print Persian and fails when a file gains one; a lower count fails until the baseline is lowered with `-- --update`. Never raise the baseline. Since v9.0.450 (TD-625) the baseline is empty, so no counted place prints Persian; a runner test name held in a constant and the label or message of a test assertion helper (`check`, `expect…`, `assert…`) count too, and Persian a test compares with the application's output goes in a named constant. `errorHandler` logs an error on the terminal as `HTTP <status> <code> <method> <route>` (`errorLogEntry` in `src/middleware/logger.ts`; a 5xx adds its English message and stack frames), the user's Persian message goes only to the response and the JSON log file (`userMessage`), and the console transport prints each Persian run of a log line as `[Persian text in the log file]` (`terminalLine`).
- **Persian Validation Messages (v9.0.78, TD-529):** `validate()` parses with the per-parse error map `persianIssueMessage` (`src/lib/validationMessages.ts`): every Zod issue without a schema-written message gets a Persian sentence that names the field (`FIELD_LABELS`, else the raw key in «») and says what to change, with Persian digits; Zod's own `fa` locale is only the fallback. Add a label to `FIELD_LABELS` for a new user-facing field, and write UI words in Persian (no transliteration).
- **Sales and Stock Document UI Wording (v9.0.346, TD-803):** The invoice, documents list, stock document and print screens print every number inside a message or label with `formatPersianNumber` and every currency code with `formatCurrencyLabel`, use «سامانه», «رزروشده», «شماره عطف», «شرط‌های جست‌وجو», «گردش کار», «خودکار», «پایگاه داده» and «فهرست» (never «سیستم», «فریز», «رفرنس», «فیلتر», «ورکفلو», «اتومات», «سرور», «ترنزیشن», «دیتابیس» or «لیست») and no English word (Vitest `documentsWording.test.tsx`); the receipt summary says stock changes on the final save and the voucher stays a draft until the accountant approves it.
- **Warehouse UI Wording (v9.0.96, TD-496, product-owner decision ت۶):** Warehouse and Kardex UI text uses «موجودی دفتری» (never «موجودی سیستمی» / «اسمی»), «اضافی» / «کسری» for a count above / below it, «میانگین موزون بها» (never «WAC» or «میانگین بهای خرید»), «کاردکس», «به‌روزرسانی» and the package 16 glossary (ت۷: «پیشخوان»، «هشدار»، «مواد اولیه»، «پالایش»), with Persian digits; Excel exports take Persian file names and report a failure to the user (`src/lib/inventoryAudit/exportFileNames.ts`), and a confirmation uses `confirmAction`, never `window.confirm` (Vitest `inventoryWording.test.ts`). The «وضعیت انبار» warehouse chart shows the number of items with stock per warehouse (`locationItemCounts` of `/dashboard-bi-stats`), never a sum of quantities of different units.
- **Item and Pricing UI Wording (v9.0.210, TD-664, product-owner decision ت۱۰):** Item, Excel import and pricing UI text and item server messages use the B05-18 words («میانگین موزون بها», «الگو», «فهرست قیمت», «کدگذاری», «دسته‌بندی», «بی‌تداخل», «هشدار», «قالب», «بارگذاری», «فهرست», «بایگانی», «به‌روزرسانی»), never «WAC», «Template», «استراتژی», «کدینگ», «کتگوری», «اتمیک», «آلارم», «ایمپورت», «دیتابیس», «فرمولاسیون», «کاتالوگ», «آپلود», «فرمت», «لیست» or «آرشیو»; «ترنسفر» stays (Vitest `itemsWording.test.ts`). The Excel reorder header is «حد نقطه سفارش (هشدار کسری)» (`ITEM_REORDER_POINT_COLUMN` in `src/lib/items/itemExcelColumns.ts`) and the old header is still read.
- **Shell and Settings UI Wording (v9.0.278, TD-686, product-owner decision ت۷ of package 16):** The shell, dashboard, settings and setup UI text and the browser's connection messages use «پیشخوان», «گردش کار», «کار», «مواد اولیه», «هشدار», «پایگاه‌داده», «سامانه», «فهرست», «پالایش», «کارساز», «نمایه», «برخط», «بارگذاری», «خودکارسازی», «راهبرد», «بخش», «شیوه ارتباط», «نشانه ورود», «گزینه» and «پیکربندی», never the transliterations they replace, and show no English word; only «کاردکس», «ترنسفر» and «وبهوک» stay transliterated and «اکسل» / «ووکامرس» are product names; the settings search keywords keep the old words as hidden aliases (Vitest `shellWordingTd686.test.ts`).
- **UI Wording Ratchet (v10.0.51, TD-1168):** user-facing text (string and template literals and JSX text in `src/pages`, `src/components`, `src/hooks`, `src/contexts` and `src/lib`, never comments) says «سامانه», «فهرست», «جست‌وجو» and «شرط‌های جست‌وجو» / «پالایش», never «سیستم», «کاتالوگ», «فیلتر», «دوبل», «کانبان» (the projects board is «نمای ستونی», v10.1.63, OBS-PR-16) or «جستجو» with their suffixes, nor the formal «نمایید» / «می‌باشد» / «نمی‌باشد» (v10.1.26, TD-1275: «کنید» / «است» / «نیست»); no Persian heading takes letter spacing (`tracking-*`), and no screen calls the browser's `alert` or `confirm` (Vitest `vibeFarsiWordingTd1275.test.tsx`). `npm run ratchet:ui-wording` (`scripts/ui-wording-ratchet.ts`, baseline `ui-wording-baseline.json`, run by Vitest `uiWordingRatchet.test.ts`) counts such texts per file and fails when a file gains one; a lower count fails until the baseline is lowered with `-- --update`. Never raise the baseline; stored keys that keep such a word (audit entity names, the «سیستم» actor) stay counted in it. A button whose only child is an icon carries a Persian `aria-label` (or `title`): «بستن», «صفحه قبل» / «صفحه بعد», «حذف ردیف», «پاک کردن جست‌وجو» (v10.1.27, TD-1276; `scripts/icon-button-names.ts`, Vitest `iconButtonNamesTd1276.test.ts`). A hand-built window (a `fixed inset-0` overlay) has `role="dialog"` and `aria-modal="true"`, a name (`aria-label` or its heading) and `<DialogLayer onClose>` as its first child (`src/components/common/DialogLayer.tsx`): Esc closes only the topmost window (`useDialogEscape` in `src/lib/a11y/dialogEscapeStack.ts`, also used by `Modal.tsx`), a control that handles Esc itself calls `preventDefault()`, and `disabled` (the save or submit flag) keeps Esc from closing that window or the one under it while a request runs (v10.1.30, TD-1277; `scripts/dialog-roles.ts`, Vitest `dialogRolesTd1277.test.tsx`). A form label wraps its field or points to it with `htmlFor` and the field's `id` (one `useId()` per form, the row index or key inside a list); `JalaliDateInput`, `SearchableSelect`, `AccountSearchSelect`, `DecimalInput`, `FinancialAmountInput` and `MentionTextarea` take an `id` (v10.1.31, TD-1278; `scripts/label-links.ts`, Vitest `labelLinksTd1278.test.tsx`). The party form picks province and city from Iran's 31 provinces and each province's cities (`IranAddressFields`, data in `src/lib/customers/iranDivisions.ts`, no library): the city waits for a province and clears when it changes, and a stored province or city outside the list stays (v10.1.33, TD-1281; Vitest `iranAddressPickerTd1281.test.tsx`).
- **Latin Digits Ratchet (v10.1.25, TD-1274):** a number a page or component shows is written with `formatPersianNumber`, an id or document number with `formatPersianCode` (no thousands separator) and a percent with «٪». `npm run ratchet:latin-digits` (`scripts/latin-digits-ratchet.ts`, baseline `latin-digits-baseline.json`, empty; run by Vitest `latinDigitsRatchetTd1274.test.ts`) asks the TypeScript checker for every shown value of `src/pages` and `src/components` whose type is a number: a JSX child (both branches of a conditional, both sides of `||` / `??`, inside `map`), a number inside a template literal shown as a child or in a text attribute (`title`, `placeholder`, `aria-label` …) and `.toFixed(…)` as a child; a file may not gain one. Never raise the baseline.
- **Setup Wizard and Health Page Wording (v9.0.392, TD-622, product-owner decision ت۸ of package 1):** the setup wizard, the system health page, its integrity check and the factory reset dialog show no English word, transliteration or Latin digit: «رمز راه‌اندازی» (the variable name only in its help text as `code`), «پایگاه‌داده», «بررسی», «صف رویدادهای ناموفق», «صف ارسال رویدادها», «اجرای دوباره», «حافظه» and «مگابایت»; Node.js and directory paths only in the technical details (`data-technical`); numbers through `src/lib/system/healthText.ts` and `toPersianDigits`. The company name has no default and is required by the form and `POST /setup` (`src/lib/auth/setupRules.ts`), so no invoice header prints a placeholder name, and the factory reset is confirmed by typing «حذف همه» (`isFactoryResetConfirmed` in `src/lib/system/factoryReset.ts`). Step 1 of the wizard needs the setup token, and a 401 from `POST /setup` takes the wizard back to step 1 with the message under the token field (v9.0.393, TD-621). `POST /setup` writes the first system admin, the default warehouse, the company settings and its audit row in one transaction under the transaction advisory lock 79234 (`runInitialSetup` in `src/services/auth/initialSetup.service.ts`), so a failed write leaves no admin and the setup can run again (v10.0.46, part of TD-960).
- **Accounting UI Wording (v9.0.287, TD-579):** accounting UI text (the accounting page, vouchers, reports, year closing, chart of accounts and account mapping; treasury and cheques have their own test) has no English word except key names, file formats and currency codes, and uses «پیشخوان», «سند حسابداری» / «دوطرفه», «ردیف سند», «گردش کار», «خودکار», «درخت حساب‌ها», «صفحه‌کلید», «بررسی», «بخش» and «جست‌وجو», never «داشبورد», «دوبل», «آرتیکل», «ورکفلو», «اتوماتیک», «اتوماسیون», «مپینگ», «سوییچ», «چارت», «کیبورد», «اسکن», «تب» or «جستجو»; the voucher form, which saves a draft, never says «ثبت قطعی» (Vitest `accountingWording.test.ts`).
- **Project UI Wording (v9.0.424, TD-769, product-owner decision ت۱۰ of package 11):** the project and production screens, the BOM allocation tab, the inventory-control and workflow preset settings and the project server messages say «فهرست مواد» (never BOM, not even in parentheses), «رزرو» and «ثبت نهایی و رزرو انبار» (never «فریز»), «نمودار زمان‌بندی» (never «گانت» / Gantt), «خط زمانی», «نمودار», «سامانه», «قلم», «مواد اولیه», «مرحله» and «فهرست کالا», never Timeline, «چارت», «سرور», «آیتم», «متریال», «فاز», «کاتالوگ» or SKU, and show counts with Persian digits and percents with «٪» (Vitest `projectWording.test.ts`).
- **Events UI Wording (v9.0.443, TD-734, product-owner decision ت۹):** the events, webhook, dead-letter, notification and WooCommerce UI text (`EVENTS_UI` in Vitest `eventsWording.test.ts`) keeps «وب‌هوک» as the third allowed loanword and uses «اقدام», «آزمایش», «گزارش», «داده رویداد», «صف ارسال رویداد», «صف خطا», «پردازشگر پس‌زمینه», «سرآیند», «آزمایش اتصال», «کلید», «اشاره» and «خودکارسازی» with the shell glossary («سامانه», «کارساز», «برخط», «پایگاه‌داده», «جست‌وجو», «نشانی», «پالایش»); an English label of the WooCommerce or WordPress dashboard appears only inside parentheses.
- **Persian and Arabic Digits (v8.0.108, TD-385):** `fin()` normalizes Persian/Arabic digits, «٫» and thousands separators (`normalizeDecimalString` in `src/lib/numericInput.ts`). A money, rate or quantity field accepted as number-or-string in a Zod body uses `decimalInput(label)` (`src/middleware/validate.ts`: Latin decimal string, empty = undefined, text = validation error), never a bare `z.union([z.number(), z.string()])` that a service silently turns into 0 — document lines too (quantity, prices, discount, book and counted stock in `documentItemInputSchema`, `src/routes/documents.routes.ts`; a price sent empty is 400, a missing one stays 0; v9.0.240, TD-784); numeric identifiers typed as text (cheque number, Sayad id) go through `latinDigitsString`.
- **Double Submissions:** Always use `isSaving` or `loading` to disable submit buttons.
- **Large Selects:** Use `SearchableSelect` (`src/components/SearchableSelect.tsx`) for large datasets. A preselected value takes its label from the fetched options, else from `valueLabel`, and the search request sets `search` and `limit` once (`pickListRequestUrl`; v9.0.309, TD-680).
- **Form State:** Use dedicated `editingId: number | null` and `isEditing = editingId !== null` instead of holding `id: 0`.

## 7. AI Agent Auto-Changelog Updates & Release Tracking
- **Mandatory Update Logging:** Whenever modifying code, adding features, optimizing, or fixing bugs, you MUST append a new release entry to the **active** changelog file (currently `src/data/changelogs/10.ts`, series `v10.x.y` — see §13 and §23) with Jalali date, version bump, title, summary, changes, and fixes.
- **Short Entries, Important Points Only (v7.0.54, product-owner decision):** Every version still gets exactly one entry, but it records only important changes and important / critical bugs — no file lists, test names, debt bookkeeping or "version bumped" lines (those belong in the commit and `TECH_DEBT.md`). Limits in `src/data/changelogs/compactRule.ts` (title ≤ 90, summary ≤ 300 chars, ≤ 4 changes, ≤ 4 fixes, each ≤ 150 chars) are enforced by `npm run check:version`.
- **Every Claim Has a Test (v7.0.64, audit P3-13):** Every fix or behaviour change recorded in the changelog is backed by at least one automated test that fails on the previous version and passes on the new one. The test id is named in the commit message and in the `TECH_DEBT.md` / `TECH_DEBT_ARCHIVE.md` row (never in the changelog file, per the rule above). A claim without such a test (e.g. a refactor or a documentation change) is worded as such and never as a fix.

## 8. Server Startup & Background Seed Execution
- **Port 3000 Ingress:** In AI Studio preview / Cloud Run, `server.ts` MUST bind and listen on port 3000 immediately. Until migrations, seed and the engines finish, every `/api` request except `/api/health/*` and the WooCommerce webhook answers 503 `SYSTEM_STARTING` with `Retry-After` and `/health/ready` is 503 (`startupGate` in `src/middleware/startupGate.ts`, turned on only by `server.ts` with `beginStartup`); the browser shows a waiting page and resends the request (`fetchThroughStartup` in `src/lib/systemStarting.ts`, `SystemStartingOverlay`) (v9.0.164, TD-584, product-owner decision t2).
- **Background Seeding:** Seeding (`runSeed()`) must run asynchronously in background IIFE without blocking server startup. The boot runs its data steps through `runBootSequence` (`src/services/system/bootSequence.ts`, steps in `bootDataSteps`): only the migrations are essential and stop the process after their attempts; a non-essential step (base data, default engines, the time zone cache) that keeps failing is left out, the server starts and `/health/ready` / `/health/startup` name it in `degradedSteps`. `runSeed` names each failed section in `failedSections` with `success: false`, never a silent success; the factory reset answers 409 `FACTORY_RESET_SEED_INCOMPLETE` then (v10.0.180, TD-958 / TD-959).

## 9. Standard 22 Categories & Default Units
- **22 Categories:** `DEFAULT_CATEGORIES` (`src/data/defaultCategories.ts`) is the one list: 9 product categories (گردنبند، گوشواره میخی، گوشواره آویز، انگشتر، دستبند، گوشواره آویز بزرگ، گردنبند بزرگ، گوشواره دو تکه، گردنبند دو تکه) and 13 raw_material categories (ترنسفر، مهره، مهره کریستالی، سنگ، مهره حدید، مهره چوبی، خرج کار، خرج کار طلایی، خرج کار برنزی، خرج کار استیل، بند چرمی و زنجیر، «کیلر، رنگ، گلیز»، سایر اقلام); the boot seed and «بازگردانی دسته‌های پیش‌فرض» read it.
- **Category Changes (v9.0.204, TD-659, product-owner decision ت۸):** A category is soft-deleted (`categories.is_deleted`, migration 0072) and the list reads only live ones; a category that active items use is not deleted (422). Create, edit, delete and the default reset run in one transaction with a «دسته‌بندی کالا» audit row (before / after / changes) through `src/services/items/itemCategory.service.ts`; the reset restores a deleted default category instead of adding a second row. A live category name is unique by `lower(btrim(name))` (`assertCategoryNameAvailable` in `src/services/items/itemCategoryIdentity.ts`, partial unique index `uq_categories_name_active`, migration 0073, created only on data without duplicates; a concurrent duplicate is the same Persian 409). Items point to their category by name, so a rename moves the category's active items to the new name in the same transaction (items locked in id order, each item's version advanced, the item ids in the audit row) and a type change of a category with active items is 422 `CATEGORY_TYPE_HAS_ITEMS`; the default reset keeps such a category's type. Old duplicate names and active items whose category no longer exists are only listed by the financial health check (`category_name_uniqueness`) (v9.0.205, TD-658, product-owner decision ت۸).
- **Auto-Fill Default Units:** When category is selected in `ItemFormModal.tsx`, unit automatically updates to category's `defaultUnit` (جفت، ریسه، برگ، متر، عدد و...).

## 10. Transfers & Image Management
- **Dual-Route API:** Transfer routes in `src/routes/transfers.routes.ts` accept both `POST` and `PUT` across `/transfers` and `/transfers/:code`. A transfer code has one row: saving a soft-deleted code revives that row under its row lock in one transaction, `GET /transfers/:code` answers 404 for a deleted design no product uses, and the page reads every code (`/transfers?all=true`) (v9.0.95, TD-493). Since v10.0.182 (OBS-R1-82) `GET /transfers` answers one page of codes (`page`, `limit` at most 200, `search` over code, title, notes and the linked products' names and codes, `image`) with only that page's products and a `summary` of every code, all in SQL (`listTransferCodes` in `src/services/transfers/transferCodeList.ts`, contract `src/lib/transfers/transferCodeList.ts`), and the page reads one page at a time.
- **Attachments on Disk (v7.0.56, audit P2-9, product-owner decision):** Attachment files of documents, journal vouchers, cheques, treasury transactions, projects and payrolls live in `public/uploads/.attachments/` (never served by `/uploads`; `public/uploads` never enters `dist/`, the Docker image or a source package: `copyPublicAssets` in `scripts/publicAssets.ts`, v9.0.180, TD-588) and are registered in `file_attachments`; the JSONB `attachments` columns hold metadata and `/api/attachments/<id>` only — never Base64. Every save path passes incoming attachments through `AttachmentStorageService` (`attachToNewRecord` after insert, `normalizeForRecord` on update). Files are downloaded only via `GET /api/attachments/:id`, which requires the read permission of the owning record (`RECORD_READ_PERMISSIONS` in `src/lib/recordReadPermissions.ts`, also used by those records' read routes). In the browser, attachment images and downloads go through `AttachmentImage` / `downloadAttachment` (`src/lib/attachments/attachmentDisplay.ts`), which add the Bearer header when the token is held in memory (v7.0.100, TD-224). A stored file that cannot be read answers 404 (missing or not a file) or 500 `ATTACHMENT_READ_FAILED` and never stops the server (`openAttachmentFile` in `src/routes/attachments.routes.ts`; v9.0.254, TD-627). A JSON body is at most 5 MB, except the save routes of records with attachments (`acceptsAttachmentBody` in `src/lib/attachments/attachmentBodyLimit.ts`: 14 MB, for files of at most 10 MB, the same limit as the upload box); a larger body is 413 `PAYLOAD_TOO_LARGE` with a Persian message (v9.0.255, TD-641, product-owner decision ت۴). An image field (`uploadBase64ToStorage` in `src/lib/storage.ts`) takes only a JPG, PNG, WEBP or GIF data URL of at most 3 MB; anything else is 422 `IMAGE_FORMAT_INVALID` / `IMAGE_TOO_LARGE` and is never stored as text (v9.0.256, TD-642). Such an image is served by `/uploads` only with a session; without one only the public asset folders and a legacy company logo file at the root (`logo-<uuid>.<image>`) are open (`isPublicUploadPath` in `src/lib/attachments/publicUploadPath.ts`, matched only on the decoded, normalized path: an encoded slash or backslash, a backslash or a `..` segment is 404), and the site root static handler of `public/`, mounted in `createApp`, refuses any path that decodes and normalizes into `/uploads` (`isUploadsRequestPath`) (v10.1.58, OBS-R2-60, product-owner decision ت۴۰).
- **Orphan Attachment Cleanup (v7.0.83, TD-224):** Files whose save transaction rolled back have no `file_attachments` row. Only the manual `npm run attachments:cleanup` / `POST /api/attachments/cleanup-orphans` (admin, dry-run by default, advisory lock 91005) removes them: `<uuid>.<ext>` files with no row at all, older than 60 minutes. A requested minimum age is never below 5 minutes (`MIN_ORPHAN_AGE_MINUTES`), so a file of a save still in progress is never removed (v9.0.257, TD-643). Files of attachments detached from their record (`is_deleted = 1`) are kept and only counted.
- **Sales File and Activity Attachments (v10.2.5, N-02 §3.3, CRM plan decisions ت۶ / ت۷ / ت۸):** A sales file (`crm_leads`) and an activity or follow-up (`crm_activities`) take image and PDF attachments through `GET` (`crm.view`), `POST` (`crm.manage`, the raw file body with `X-File-Name`) and `DELETE` on `/crm/leads/:id/attachments` and `/crm/activities/:id/attachments` (`src/routes/crmAttachments.routes.ts`, `CrmAttachmentService` in `src/services/crm/crmAttachments.service.ts`): JPG, PNG, WEBP or PDF whose content matches its type, at most 10 files per record and `MAX_ATTACHMENT_FILE_MB` per file (422 `CRM_ATTACHMENT_FORMAT` / `CRM_ATTACHMENT_TOO_LARGE` / `CRM_ATTACHMENT_LIMIT`; the rule is shared with the browser in `src/lib/crm/crmAttachments.ts`). They are stored through `file_attachments` (entity `crm_lead` / `crm_activity`, read permission `crm.view`) with the original kept and, for an image, a light WEBP of long side `MEDIA_LIGHT_LONG_SIDE` made by the media library's sharp pipeline (`file_attachments.light_storage_path`, migration 0127, served with `?variant=light`); each addition and removal is written under the record's row lock with its audit row in the same `tx`, and the files never enter the media library. The sales file window and each activity's paperclip button show them (`CrmAttachmentsPanel`), and a window does not close while a file is being sent or removed.
- **Modal Viewports:** Large modals must use fixed headers/footers with internal scrollable bodies (`max-h-[85vh]` or `max-h-[90vh]`) for iframe preview compatibility.

## 11. Modular Accounting Services & Facade Pattern
- **6 Sub-Services in `src/services/accounting/`:**
  1. `ChartOfAccountsService`: Coding hierarchy, standard accounts, tree generation.
  2. `VoucherService`: Journal vouchers, atomic sequence (`voucher_number_seq`), balanced debit/credit validation.
  3. `TreasuryService`: Bank accounts, cash funds, petty cash, Sayad cheques lifecycle, treasury transactions.
  4. `AccountingReportService`: Trial balance (2/4/6/8-col), detailed ledger, balance sheet, income statement.
  5. `VoucherSyncService`: Automated vouchers for sales, purchases, warehouse movements, payroll.
  6. `FiscalYearService`: Closing temporary/permanent accounts, opening/closing vouchers.
- **Unified Facade:** `AccountingService` in `src/services/accounting.service.ts` binds all sub-services for 100% backwards compatibility.
- **AccountNature Consistency:** Standard type is `AccountNature = 'debit' | 'credit' | 'both'`.
- **Fiscal Periods (v7.0.49, audit P2-5, product-owner decisions):** Whether a (Jalali) fiscal year is closed lives ONLY in `fiscal_periods` (`FiscalPeriodService`); never infer it from voucher reference numbers. Every voucher create/update/delete/reverse/finalize calls `VoucherService.checkFiscalPeriodOpen(date, tx)`, which locks the year's row `FOR SHARE`; `FiscalYearService.executeFiscalYearClosing` locks it `FOR UPDATE` before computing balances and marks it closed in the same transaction. No voucher type (including `closing`) bypasses the check. The double-entry balance tolerance is the single constant `VOUCHER_BALANCE_TOLERANCE = 0.01` in every path (`src/lib/voucherBalance.ts`; the voucher forms use `computeVoucherBalance` from the same file, v7.0.76).
- **Manual Voucher Currency and Rial Balance (v9.0.190, TD-551, product-owner decision ت۷):** A manual voucher (`POST` / `PUT /accounting/vouchers`, which pass `manualEntry`, never read from the body) and a correction voucher resolve each row's currency as the row's own, else the voucher's, else IRR, and need a positive rate on every non-rial row (422 `VOUCHER_ROW_RATE_REQUIRED`, never rate 1); rows of one currency at one rate balance on their own amounts, any other voucher in rials by the TD-260 rule (`computeVoucherCurrencyBalance` in `src/lib/accounting/voucherCurrencyBalance.ts`, `resolveManualVoucherRows` / `assertVoucherRowsBalanced` in `src/services/accounting/voucherRowBalance.ts`). Every path balances a multi-currency voucher in rials, and finalizing accepts one whose rows balance in rials (`isVoucherBalancedForFinalize`). The journal book filters rows by the TD-260 currency rule and shows the «all currencies» view in rials with the row's own amount and rate; the financial health check balances multi-currency vouchers in rials and lists vouchers with a non-rial row at rate empty, 0 or 1 (`voucher_foreign_rows_without_rate`), never rewriting them. The manual voucher form, its edit and the correction form send and keep each row's currency and rate (v9.0.191, TD-564): the voucher and row currencies come from `TREASURY_CURRENCIES` (no «تومان»; the manual voucher routes refuse another code with 400), an empty row currency follows the voucher and an empty rate of a row in the voucher currency takes the voucher rate (`withVoucherRowCurrency` in `src/lib/accounting/voucherFormCurrency.ts`), and the forms balance and add the balancing row with the server's rule (`voucherFormBalance`, `voucherBalancingAmount`). A voucher row's debit, credit and rate are read with `decimalInput` (v9.0.192, TD-557). Its detailed type comes from `VOUCHER_DETAILED_TYPES` (`src/lib/accounting/voucherDetailedTypes.ts`, shared by the schema and both forms through `VoucherDetailedPicker`; a legacy `custom` row is «متفرقه» `other`), and the project detail reads the project pick list, which `accounting.vouchers` opens (v9.0.193, TD-569). The voucher print follows the same balance rule (`voucherPrintAmounts` in `src/lib/accounting/voucherPrintAmounts.ts`: a single-currency voucher in its currency with its rate, a multi-currency one in rials with each row's own amount and rate), and voucher types are named only through `VOUCHER_TYPE_LABELS` (`src/lib/accounting/voucherTypes.ts`, settlement «تسویه» included) (v9.0.195, TD-573). The voucher, correction and reversal forms and the accounting report filters keep ISO dates in `JalaliDateInput`; no accounting component imports `react-multi-date-picker` directly (v9.0.196, TD-578).
- **Draft Vouchers at Closing and Void (v8.0.2, TD-252 / TD-251, product-owner decision):** Automatic vouchers stay `draft` until an accountant approves them. A fiscal year is never closed while a draft voucher dated in that (Jalali) year exists: `FiscalYearService.findDraftVouchersOfYear` runs after the year lock, `executeFiscalYearClosing` refuses with `FISCAL_YEAR_HAS_DRAFT_VOUCHERS`, and the preview returns `draftVouchers` / `draftVoucherCount` (the closing button stays disabled). Voiding a source — document/invoice, treasury transaction, cheque, payroll — goes through `VoucherService.voidSourceVoucher`: a draft voucher is soft-deleted (fiscal period checked), an approved or permanent one gets a reversal voucher; never issue an approved reversal for a draft. A voucher issued by a source (document, treasury transaction, cheque, payroll, BOM allocation: the `source_*` columns, `cheques.voucher_id` and `treasury_transactions.voucher_id`, a reversal taking its original's source; `voucherSourceKinds` in `src/services/accounting/voucherSource.ts`) is only approved and finalized from the voucher page: delete, a manual edit, back to draft (page or workflow), reverse, correction and void-and-repost are refused with 409 `VOUCHER_HAS_SOURCE` naming the source, which is voided instead; the voucher list and detail carry `sourceKind` (v9.0.294, TD-552, product-owner decision ت۸). `PUT /accounting/vouchers/:id` requires the voucher's `version` (400 without it) and writes with the version in its WHERE (409 `OCC_CONFLICT` when stale); a deleted voucher is neither read nor edited; voucher and account edit and delete audit rows carry before / after, rows included (`src/services/accounting/accountingAudit.ts`) (v9.0.295, TD-555). Batch finalize answers the vouchers it made permanent and each refused id with its reason (`BatchFinalizeResult` / `batchFinalizeMessage` in `src/lib/accounting/voucherBatch.ts`), never the count of ids sent (v9.0.296, TD-556). The automation status (`getAutomationStatus` in `src/services/accounting/automationStatus.service.ts`, rules in `src/lib/accounting/automationStatus.ts`) counts final documents only, a stock count as needing a voucher only with a valued Kardex difference and a transfer never; its coverage is 100 only when every needed voucher exists (v9.0.297, TD-560). The voucher row menu offers only what the server accepts (`voucherRowActions` in `src/lib/accounting/voucherRowActions.ts`): edit and delete a draft, back to draft an approved voucher, reverse an approved or permanent one and correct an approved one, never reverse or correct a reversal; a voucher with a source or of a closing run shows only why it is locked and the workflow (v9.0.298, TD-568, product-owner decision ت۸). A reversal voucher (`REV-V`, `VOID-REPOST-V`, `RE-REV-V`) never goes back to draft (409 `VOUCHER_REVERSAL_LOCKED` in `applyVoucherStatus`, page and workflow), and a voucher with an active reversal carries its number (`reversedByVoucherNumber`, `voucherActiveReversals` in `src/services/accounting/voucherSource.ts`), so its menu offers no reverse, correction or revert and says why (v10.0.167, TD-1239). Approving, finalizing (one or a batch) and putting back to draft need `accounting.vouchers_approve` (requires `accounting.vouchers` and, since v10.1.11 (TD-1263), `workflow.approve`, which signs the journal voucher workflow; migration 0098 gave it to every role holding `accounting.vouchers`), and the creator or last editor (`journal_vouchers.updated_by_id`) of a manual voucher never approves or finalizes it (403 `VOUCHER_MAKER_CANNOT_APPROVE`; a batch skips it with its reason); a voucher with a source, a closing-run voucher and the system admin are exempt, a manual voucher is recorded as approved only by the system admin, and the default journal voucher workflow asks the same key, upgraded at boot only on an install still holding the old seed (`upgradeLegacySeedGuards` in `src/services/workflow/seedGuardUpgrade.ts`; `assertNotApprovedByMaker` in `src/services/accounting/voucherMakerChecker.ts`) (v10.0.27, TD-965, payroll duties plan decisions ت۹ / ت۱۰ / ت۳); the voucher list shows that maker «در انتظار تأیید دیگری» instead of the approve button (`isViewerVoucherMaker` in `src/lib/accounting/voucherListViewer.ts`, v10.0.155, TD-1231).
- **Closing Dates Are the Year's Bounds (v8.0.47, TD-310, product-owner decision):** A fiscal year is the Jalali year. Closing vouchers keep each balance's detail: one row per account and detail of the level-4 trial balance, a party, project or bank account with its type and id (`closingRowDetail` in `src/services/accounting/closingRowDetail.ts`; v10.0.267, OBS-R1-45), and the opening voucher mirrors them. Closing vouchers are always dated the year's last day (30 Esfand in a leap year, else 29 Esfand) and the opening voucher 1 Farvardin of the next year; balances are taken up to that last day. `resolveFiscalClosingDates` (`src/services/accounting/fiscalYear.service.ts`, built on `jalaliYearBounds` in `src/utils/calendarDate.ts`) derives them from the year and refuses any other date sent by a client (422); the closing form only displays them.
- **Closing Vouchers Are Linked to Their Year (v9.0.159, TD-545, product-owner decision ت۳):** The closing and opening vouchers a fiscal-year closing issues carry `journal_vouchers.source_fiscal_year` = the closed year (migration 0069 linked the earlier runs of closed years); a closing voucher is only such a linked `closing` voucher, never a voucher found by type or reference number. The trial balance, income statement, balance sheet and ratios leave a linked closing voucher out when it is dated on or after the report's end date (income statement: its start date), unless `includeClosing` («همراه اسناد اختتامیه», `yearEndClosingVoucherSql` / `yearEndClosingCutoff` in `src/services/accounting/yearEndClosingVouchers.ts`); the opening voucher always counts, and the closing computation reads its balances with `includeClosing: true`. A manual voucher (`POST` / `PUT /accounting/vouchers`) never takes the type `closing` (the form offers «افتتاحیه», `opening`) nor a reference with a reserved prefix of the system (`isReservedVoucherReference` in `src/lib/accounting/manualVoucherRules.ts`); whether a year is closed is read only from `fiscal_periods`, never from reference numbers; a voucher of a closing run (its opening voucher included) is never reversed, corrected or put back to draft (409 `FISCAL_CLOSING_VOUCHER_LOCKED`), while a legacy manual `closing` voucher is an ordinary voucher listed by the financial health check (`manual_vouchers_closing_type`) (v9.0.160, TD-559).
- **Close After the Year Ends, Reopen the Last Closed Year (v9.0.161, TD-543, product-owner decisions ت۱ / ت۲):** A fiscal year is closed only when the business today is past its last day (422 `FISCAL_YEAR_NOT_ENDED`, `assertFiscalYearEnded` in `src/services/accounting/fiscalYearOrder.ts`); the preview carries `yearEnded`, and the closing form takes its years from `GET /accounting/fiscal-closing/years` (ended years only, with status and whether they have vouchers; never the current or a future year). Only the last closed year (every later year open) is reopened, through `POST /accounting/fiscal-closing/reopen` with a required reason and the permission `accounting.fiscal_reopen` (granted to no role by default): `reopenFiscalYear` (`src/services/accounting/fiscalYearReopen.ts`) takes the fiscal-year sequence lock (`ADVISORY_LOCK_KEYS.FISCAL_YEAR_SEQUENCE` 91020, also taken by closing) and the year's `fiscal_periods` row lock, opens the year, voids the active vouchers of its latest closing run (linked by `source_fiscal_year`, neither reversals nor reversed) through `voidSourceVoucher` with a reversal on each voucher's own date, and writes the audit row with `tx`. Years closed before their end are only listed by the financial health check (`fiscal_year_closed_early`). Years close in order (v9.0.162, TD-544, decision ت۱): a year closes only when every earlier year holding an active voucher of any status is closed (`assertEarlierYearsClosed`, same file, after the year row lock; else 422 `FISCAL_YEAR_EARLIER_OPEN` naming them); the preview carries `earlierOpenYears`, the form starts on the oldest open year with vouchers (`defaultYear`), and years closed before an earlier year with vouchers are only listed by the health check (`fiscal_year_closed_out_of_order`).
- **Cheque ↔ Voucher Link (v8.0.19, TD-271):** Every voucher of a cheque's lifecycle (registration, in collection, cleared, bounced, spent) carries `journal_vouchers.source_cheque_id` (migration 0047); find a cheque's vouchers by it, never by the cheque number (not unique). `deleteCheque` refuses while an unlinked legacy voucher of a number shared by another cheque remains. A cheque with a permanent voucher that has no reversal is never deleted (409 naming the voucher; v9.0.69, TD-502, product-owner decision ت۹), and the cheque row menu offers no status change or delete in a terminal status (`chequeHasNextStep` in `src/lib/treasury/chequeTransitions.ts`, the one transition table of the server and the browser). A bounced paid cheque posts Dr cheque payables / Cr the supplier (v8.0.21, TD-272); returning a bounced received cheque to its drawer posts Dr the customer / Cr the protested-cheques account (v8.0.22, TD-273, product-owner decision). Cheques are rial only: `createCheque` refuses any other currency (v8.0.23, TD-275, product-owner decision); foreign receipts and payments go through the treasury form at an exchange rate. A cleared (`passed`) cheque counts in its bank account's treasury balance — received adds, paid subtracts — and always keeps the bank it cleared through (v8.0.24, TD-276); clearing into a bank account without a ledger account is refused (v8.0.25, TD-277), and a cheque step whose voucher needs an account the mapping cannot resolve (clearing, collection, bounce of a received cheque, spending) is refused with 422 `CHEQUE_ACCOUNT_MAPPING_MISSING` before anything changes (`requireChequeAccounts` in `chequeLifecycle.service.ts`; v10.0.254, TD-970). The treasury and invoice-settlement forms have no cheque method: `createTreasuryTransaction` refuses `method: 'cheque'` and cheques are registered only from the cheque book; legacy cheque-method transactions stay voidable and never count in a bank's treasury balance (v8.0.26, TD-278, product-owner decision). A receipt or payment of party type «other», and a personnel one with purpose «other», posts to a counter account the user chooses from `GET /accounting/treasury/contra-accounts` (an active subsidiary account other than trade receivables / payables, wages payable, the cheque, inventory and closing accounts and any treasury account's ledger account; `requireChoosableContraAccount` in `src/services/accounting/treasury/partyContraAccount.ts`, else 422); a personnel receipt or payment requires its purpose, and both are stored on the row (`treasury_transactions.purpose` / `contra_account_id`, migration 0060; v9.0.82, TD-507, product-owner decision ت۴). A treasury receipt or payment is linked only to what exists: its party id must be in the table of its party type (customer / supplier in `customers` with that type or «both», personnel in `personnel`, «other» has none) and its document, locked `FOR SHARE` under the bank lock, must be active, a sales invoice or proforma for a receipt and a purchase document for a payment, with a customer / supplier party of the document — the document's `party_id` when the row carries a party id (v9.0.336, TD-778), else the party name equals the document's trimmed buyer name (`resolveTreasuryPartyName` / `assertTreasuryDocumentLink` in `src/services/accounting/treasury/treasuryLinks.ts`, else 422; v9.0.83, TD-501); a personnel receipt, payment or cheque needs the personnel id (422 `TREASURY_PERSONNEL_REQUIRED`, `assertPersonnelPartyId`, same file; v10.0.259, TD-954). A receipt or payment linked to a document and sent without a party id takes, in its own transaction before the link check and the voucher, the document's party id and the party's current name when the row is on the document's side, the name sent is the document's buyer name or the party's current name, and the party is live and of that type or «both» (`documentPartyOfTreasuryLink`, same file; the invoice settlement form also sends the document's `partyId`), so the treasury row and its voucher row stay on the party's account card and delete guard after a rename; another name is still 422 `TREASURY_DOCUMENT_PARTY_MISMATCH` and a legacy document without a party keeps the exact-name rule (v9.0.459, TD-907). The linked document's currency (empty = IRR) is the row's currency on create as on relink, one rule in `assertTreasuryDocumentLink` (422 `TREASURY_DOCUMENT_CURRENCY_MISMATCH` before any write; v9.0.460, TD-908). A cheque's party row follows its party type with the treasury form's rule (customer → trade receivables, supplier → trade payables, personnel → wages payable or employee advance by its required purpose, «other» and personnel «other» → the chosen counter account, detail of the party's own type); the account is stored on the cheque (`cheques.party_account_id` / `purpose`, migration 0061) and its registration, paid-cheque bounce and return post to it (`chequePartyPosting` in `src/services/accounting/treasury/chequePartyAccount.ts`); a cheque from before v9.0.84 keeps the old rule and, when its party type differs, is listed by the financial health check (`cheque_party_account_legacy`), never rewritten (v9.0.84, TD-497, product-owner decision ت۲). Spending a received cheque needs the supplier's id (`transfereePartyId`, a supplier or «both» party; else 422 `CHEQUE_TRANSFEREE_REQUIRED` / `TREASURY_PARTY_INVALID`): its voucher debits trade payables with that supplier's detail and the payee name is read from the supplier row; the status form picks the supplier from the list, never a typed name (v9.0.85, TD-498, product-owner decision ت۳). A treasury receipt or payment, a bank transfer or a cheque recorded without a voucher (`createVoucher: false`, e.g. opening balances) needs the permission `accounting.treasury_no_voucher` (granted to no role by default), computed in the route with `userHasRoleOrPermission` and passed as `allowNoVoucher` (never read from the body), else 403; the forms show the option only to its holders and the financial health check lists such entries (`treasury_without_voucher`, `src/services/accounting/treasury/noVoucherTreasury.ts`; v8.0.118, TD-409, product-owner decision). A bank-to-bank transfer is voided as a whole (v8.0.73, TD-341, product-owner decision «ابطال هر دو»): `voidTreasuryTransaction` finds the other side (same shared voucher, opposite type, `bank_transfer`, `transferSideIds`), locks both banks then both treasury rows, and reverses both rows and both balances and voids the shared voucher once; the remaining side of a transfer half-voided before v8.0.73 is voided without touching the voucher. A reversal row (the void of another treasury row) is never voided (409, v9.0.67, TD-499, product-owner decision ت۱): to record it again, enter a new transaction; legacy revived rows without a voucher (even reversal depth ≥ 2) are listed by the health check `treasury_without_voucher`, and the business invariants I15 / I16 (`src/tests/invariants/bankInvariants.ts`) check each bank against its ledger rows of every voucher status. A document's paid amount counts its completed treasury rows plus each voided row whose reversal row is on the same document (`settledAmount` in `src/services/documents/documentSettlement.ts`), so a void nets to zero and a later receipt counts in full (v9.0.68, TD-500). Its remaining amount and settlement status also subtract its final, non-voided sales returns (net of lines + VAT + service charge, `returnedAmount`, `fetchReturnCredits` in `src/services/documents/documentReturnCredit.ts`), as the return voucher does on the customer's account (v10.0.106, TD-909, product-owner decision ت۶ الف). A bank's current balance is its treasury balance — opening balance + treasury transactions + cleared cheques (v8.0.74, TD-340, product-owner decision «از تراکنش‌ها»): «همگام‌سازی مانده بانک‌ها» (`recalculateAndSyncBankBalances`) locks every bank in id order and writes that balance; the general ledger difference is only reported (an opening voucher counts only when approved or permanent; a row tagged with a bank detail belongs only to that bank), and an account whose ledger with its draft vouchers equals its treasury balance is shown «در انتظار تأیید سند» (`pending_approval`, `src/lib/treasury/bankSyncStatus.ts`), never as a discrepancy (v10.1.63, OBS-PR-11, decision ت۴۰). Bank account maintenance runs under the bank row lock (v8.0.78, TD-325): `updateBankAccount` locks the row `FOR UPDATE` and computes the opening-balance difference under it; treasury account codes come from `assignTreasuryAccountCode` (`src/services/accounting/treasury/bankAccountCode.ts`: an atomic counter per prefix in `document_ref_counters`, a custom code unique among active accounts, case-insensitive, `TREASURY_ACCOUNT_CODE_TAKEN`), never max + 1; `deleteBankAccount` locks the bank and is refused while it has treasury transactions or non-deleted cheques. In the same transaction it voids the account's standing opening and opening-adjustment vouchers through `voidSourceVoucher` (draft soft-deleted, approved reversed) and is refused with 409 naming the voucher when one is permanent (`voidBankOpeningVouchers` in `src/services/accounting/treasury/bankOpeningVoucher.ts`; v9.0.70, TD-503, product-owner decision ت۹). While the account's `bank_account` approval workflow is `IN_PROGRESS`, a change of its opening balance is refused with 409 (`assertNoPendingOpeningApproval`, same file; v9.0.71, TD-504, product-owner decision ت۸), so the opening voucher is issued only by the approval. The cheque reconciliation report compares 3101 by its credit balance and expects only paid cheques that are not cleared, bounced or returned (v8.0.27, TD-280).
- **Petty Cash Is a Fluctuating Fund (v10.1.32, D-16, TD-1280, product-owner decision ت۳۰ «متغیر»):** A petty cash fund is a treasury account of type `petty_cash`, rial only (create and edit refuse another currency with 422 `PETTY_CASH_RIAL_ONLY`, `assertPettyCashCurrency` in `src/services/accounting/treasury/bankAccountCurrency.ts`, rule `pettyCashCurrencyRefusal` in `src/lib/treasury/pettyCash.ts`, shared with the form), whose ledger account defaults to 1006 «تنخواه‌گردان». Any amount is charged to it and returned from it with an ordinary bank transfer (the fund card's «شارژ تنخواه» / «برگشت وجه» open the transfer form with the fund as destination or source), so its balance varies. Its expenses are recorded only as a statement (`POST /accounting/treasury/petty-cash/:id/expenses`, `accounting.treasury`, idempotent; `createPettyCashExpense` in `src/services/accounting/treasury/pettyCashExpense.service.ts`): one treasury payment from the fund under the fund row lock, never above its balance, which earlier statements with a draft voucher have already lowered (422 `PETTY_CASH_INSUFFICIENT_BALANCE`), with lines stored in `petty_cash_expense_lines` (migration 0116): each line needs an account and a positive amount of at most four decimals (else 422 `PETTY_CASH_LINES_INVALID`), while its description, seller and invoice number are optional and no VAT row is made (purchase VAT is not recorded, ت۱۳); a line's account is an active posting expense or cost-of-sales account the counter-account list allows (`GET /accounting/treasury/petty-cash/expense-accounts`, else 422 `PETTY_CASH_EXPENSE_ACCOUNT_INVALID`). Its draft treasury voucher debits each line's account without a detail and credits the fund's ledger account with the fund's bank-account detail (so I15 holds), written with the treasury row, the audit row, the attachments and the outbox event in one transaction; the statement is voided with the treasury void like any payment, and `GET /accounting/treasury/:id/petty-cash-lines` reads its lines. No new permission.
- **One Treasury Path (v10.2.2, OBS-R2-52, 10.2 package 4):** A treasury receipt or payment and its void are written only through `postTreasuryRow` / `reverseTreasuryRow` (`src/services/accounting/treasury/treasuryPosting.ts`): the bank balance (never negative, 422 `TREASURY_BALANCE_INSUFFICIENT` unless the caller names its own refusal), the number from `treasury_tx_number_seq`, the voucher (a callback of the caller, in the same `tx`), the row and its `TreasuryTransactionApproved` outbox event; the reversal copies the original's links (document, cheque, payslip, counter account) and moves the balance back unless the original never moved it (a treasury-form cheque-method row, TD-148; a payslip cheque-method payment did, `movedBankBalance`). The treasury form, its void, the payslip payment and its void, the petty cash statement and the monthly insurance or tax payment use them; the caller takes the bank lock first (`lockTreasuryBank` or its own `FOR UPDATE`) and checks its own domain rules before. Only the bank-to-bank transfer pair writes its two rows itself; no file outside `src/services/accounting/treasury/` writes `treasury_transactions` or `bank_accounts` (Vitest `treasuryOnePathObsR252.test.ts`). The `TreasuryTransactionApproved` event of a treasury receipt, payment, transfer, petty cash statement or monthly insurance or tax payment, and of each void, carries no amount, description or reason (`treasuryEventPayload` / `TREASURY_EVENT_PRIVATE_KEYS` in `treasuryPosting.ts`), in the outbox and to rules and webhooks alike; instead it carries `isHighValue`, the row's rial value (amount × its own rate when foreign; none is false) at or above the setting «آستانه تراکنش کلان خزانه» (`treasury_high_value_threshold`, a whole rial amount above zero, default 500,000,000, `src/lib/settings/settingValues.ts`, read with the write's `tx` by `src/services/accounting/treasury/treasuryHighValue.ts`), except on a payslip payment and its void. The default rule «ثبت ممیزی تراکنش‌های کلان خزانه‌داری» compares `payload.isHighValue eq true`, and an installed copy still holding exactly its seeded amount condition is moved to it once at boot with an audit row (`repairSeededHighValueTreasuryRule` in `src/services/events/highValueTreasuryRuleRepair.ts`, the one exception to TD-715); a numeric comparison (`gt` … `lte`) on a field the event does not carry never matches (`RuleEngineService.evaluateSingleRule`; a null value is still 0, and «not equal» / «not in» still match a missing field), the boot logs each saved rule of the treasury event (or of every event) whose numeric comparison reads the amount, description or reason, one line per rule asking for its review (`logRulesOnMissingNumericFields` in `src/services/events/ruleMissingFieldLog.ts`), and the financial health check lists every other stored rule of the treasury event that reads such a field by kind, with the engine's own missing-field outcome (`missingFieldOutcome` in `src/lib/events/ruleMissingField.ts`, inverted under a «NOT» group, `event.payload.` read as `payload.`, node shapes, group logic and children read with the engine's own `isSingleRuleNode` / `isRuleGroupNode` / `ruleGroupParts`): a condition that no longer matches, one that now matches every treasury event (`neq`, `not_in`, an absence check, an empty «contains» or pattern; score impact) and action text printed empty (`event_rule_treasury_private_fields`, `src/services/accounting/treasuryRuleFieldHealth.ts`); a seeded rule whose action settings the owner rewrote to print the amount (its text or title) is not moved (v10.2.7, TD-1297).
- **Payroll Status (v8.0.28, TD-281):** Only `draft` and `approved` are set by hand (`updatePayrollStatus`); `paid` / `partially_paid` come only from `registerPayrollPayment` and voiding only from `deletePayroll`, and a payroll with any payment keeps its status; only an approved or partially paid payslip takes a payment (`isPayablePayrollStatus` in `src/lib/payroll/payrollPayable.ts`, shared with the payslip list and payslip; a draft is 409 `PAYROLL_NOT_APPROVED` and the screens offer «تأیید فیش» instead), and the payment date, method and reference are written only by the payment (the status route refuses them with 400) (v9.0.269, TD-816). A payslip is issued as `draft` with its work logs; approving it, or putting an approved payslip without payment back to draft, needs `piecework.payroll_approve` (requires `piecework.view`; migration 0101 gave it to every role holding `piecework.payroll`, logged in `activity_logs`), and the payslip's issuer (`created_by_id`) never approves it, pays it or voids its payment (403 `PAYROLL_ISSUER_CANNOT_APPROVE` / `PAYROLL_ISSUER_CANNOT_PAY`, `assertPayrollDutyNotByIssuer` in `src/services/piecework/payrollDuties.ts`, rule `payrollIssuerDutyRefusal` in `src/lib/payroll/payrollDuties.ts` shared with the payslip screens, which disable the button and say why); the system admin is exempt (v10.0.193, TD-1083 / TD-1084, payroll duties plan decisions ت۱–ت۳). A draft payslip is edited only through `PUT /piecework/payrolls/:id` (`piecework.payroll`, strict body: title, bonuses, deductions with their description, advance deduction; `editDraftPayroll` in `src/services/piecework/payrollDraftEdit.ts`): under the personnel then payslip row locks, with the issue rules, its draft voucher voided through `voidSourceVoucher` and rebuilt in the same transaction and its audit row written with `tx`; an approved payslip is 409 `PAYROLL_NOT_DRAFT` and one whose voucher is no longer a draft 409 `PAYROLL_VOUCHER_NOT_DRAFT` (v10.0.200, TD-1242). Insurance, payroll tax and VAT are supported but never required, per person and per document (product-owner rule of 23 Mehr 1405, TD-1269): a payslip suggests them only for personnel marked for them (off by default), an invoice carries VAT only when its form ticks it (off by default), and no report treats their absence as an error. Statutory deductions come from the Jalali year of the payslip's end date (`payroll_statutory_rates`, migration 0104, one row per year: employee and employer insurance percent, monthly insured wage cap, monthly tax brackets and whether the employee share leaves the tax base; written only with `payroll.rates_manage` from the «نرخ‌های بیمه و مالیات حقوق» settings tab, OCC by `version`, audited) for a personnel marked «مشمول بیمه» / «مشمول مالیات حقوق» (`personnel.is_insurance_covered` / `is_tax_covered`): `suggestStatutoryDeductions` (`src/lib/payroll/statutoryDeductions.ts`, shared with the form) takes the insurance base min(gross, cap × month fraction) and scales the brackets by the month fraction; issue and draft edit store `employee_insurance`, `employer_insurance` and `income_tax` (`resolvePayrollStatutory` in `src/services/piecework/payrollStatutory.ts`), an amount not sent is the suggestion and another one needs `statutoryOverrideReason` (422 `PAYROLL_STATUTORY_OVERRIDE_REASON_REQUIRED`), a year without rates issues with `statutoryWarning` and zero amounts; net = gross − employee insurance − income tax − other deductions − advance, the employer share is never taken from net; the voucher credits insurance payable (3204, `insurancePayableAccountCode`) with both shares, debits «هزینه بیمه سهم کارفرما» (7013, `employerInsuranceExpenseAccountCode`) and credits payroll tax payable (3206, `payrollTaxPayableAccountCode`), or the payslip is refused (`PAYROLL_STATUTORY_ACCOUNT_MISSING`); the form reads `GET /piecework/statutory-preview` and sends only the amounts the user typed (`statutoryPayloadOf` in `src/lib/payroll/statutoryForm.ts`) (v10.0.210, TD-1087, payroll duties plan decisions ت۴–ت۶). «فهرست بیمه و مالیات» (`GET /piecework/reports/statutory?year&month`, payroll readers; `payrollStatutoryReport` in `src/services/piecework/payrollStatutoryReport.ts`, contract and Excel rows in `src/lib/payroll/statutoryReport.ts`) sums per personnel in SQL the approved, partially paid and paid payslips whose period ends in that Jalali month, counts the drafts it leaves out and exports an xlsx file, listing only personnel whose month has insurance or payroll tax and counting the others (`withoutStatutory`; v10.1.19, TD-1269); a month's insurance or payroll tax is paid to the organisation in one treasury payment from that list («پرداخت بیمه ماه» / «پرداخت مالیات حقوق ماه», `accounting.treasury`; `GET /accounting/treasury/statutory-remittance/options` (`statutoryRemittanceOptions.ts`) and `POST /accounting/treasury/statutory-remittance`, `createStatutoryRemittance` in `src/services/accounting/treasury/statutoryRemittance.ts`, rules shared with the window in `src/lib/treasury/statutoryRemittance.ts`): under the bank then the personnel row locks it writes one rial payment and one draft voucher with a debit row per person on the mapped 3204 or 3206 account with that personnel's detail, an optional late penalty on a chosen counter account other than 3204, 3206 or a treasury ledger account (422 `STATUTORY_REMITTANCE_PENALTY_ACCOUNT_INVALID`), and the lines in `statutory_remittance_lines` (migration 0118); read after those locks, each person's amount is at most what the month's list leaves after the live lines of that kind and month (else 422 `STATUTORY_REMITTANCE_EXCEEDS_MONTH`, so a paid month is never paid again while a later month keeps the balance up) and at most their credit balance on that account (else 422 `STATUTORY_REMITTANCE_EXCEEDS_BALANCE`); while the account's rows without a personnel detail (treasury form, cheque book, manual voucher) still carry a net debit, the list names those vouchers and the payment needs `acknowledgeUnassignedPayments` («بررسی کردم», else 409 `STATUTORY_REMITTANCE_UNASSIGNED_PAYMENTS`); the list offers only personnel with that month's insurance or tax, a voided payment's lines no longer count, it is voided like any treasury row, the treasury form and the cheque book warn when their counter account is 3204 / 3206 (`statutoryKind` of the counter-account list), and the financial health check lists the vouchers of such an account (`statutory_payments_without_personnel`) until a correction voucher moves them to each person (v10.1.36, TD-1243, decision ت۳۳ الف); payslips issued before the split (`is_statutory_split = 0`) whose deductions still sit on 3205 are only listed by the financial health check (`payroll_deductions_before_statutory_split`) until a correction voucher moves them (v10.0.211, TD-1088, decision ت۷). Eidi is optional per person too: a personnel with «مشمول عیدی» off (`personnel.is_eidi_covered`, migration 0117, on by default, kept on an edit that does not send it) gets no eidi share, eidi year or rate warning on a regular payslip, no yearly entitlement in the eidi payslip suggestion (only the accrual recorded earlier) and no row of its own in «عیدی سال» (`isEidiCovered` in `src/lib/payroll/eidi.ts`; v10.1.35, TD-1282, decision ت۳۴ الف). Eidi comes from the method of the Jalali year of the payslip's end date (`payroll_eidi_rates`, migration 0105, one row per year, written with `payroll.rates_manage` in the same settings tab, OCC by `version`, audited): «درصدی از کارکرد تا سقف سال» (`percent_of_work`), «قانون کار» (`labor_law`) or «مبلغ ثابت» (`fixed_amount`) (`src/lib/payroll/eidi.ts`, shared with the forms); with the percent method each regular payslip stores its share, round((piecework + fixed salary) × percent) up to what the year cap leaves after the personnel's other live shares, in `eidi_amount` / `eidi_year` (`resolvePayslipEidiShare` in `src/services/piecework/payrollEidi.ts`; a year without rates issues with `eidiWarning`), never taken from net, and its voucher debits «هزینه عیدی و پاداش» (7014, `eidiExpenseAccountCode`) and credits «عیدی پرداختنی» (3207, `eidiPayableAccountCode`); an «فیش عیدی» (`POST /piecework/eidi-payslips`, `piecework.payroll`, `is_eidi = 1`, `issueEidiPayslip` in `src/services/piecework/payrollEidiPayslip.ts`) takes the server's suggestion (the approved accrual not yet paid out, else the year's entitlement pro rata by days of service less earlier eidi payslips), another amount only with a reason (422 `PAYROLL_EIDI_OVERRIDE_REASON_REQUIRED`), debits 3207 with the accrued part and 7014 with the rest, carries no payroll tax and posts nothing to 3206, whatever the personnel's tax coverage (product-owner decision ت۲۱ «معاف», v10.0.234: the rates and the payslip take no tax field, migration 0106 dropped `tax_exempt_cap`), and follows the payslip's approval and payment rules; an eidi payslip is never edited as a draft (409 `PAYROLL_EIDI_NOT_EDITABLE`) and a payslip whose share an eidi payslip has paid is neither deleted nor put back to draft (409 `PAYROLL_EIDI_ALREADY_SETTLED`, under the personnel then payslip row locks); a payslip voucher is dated the earlier of its period end and the business today, so an eidi or prepayment payslip issued early is never dated in the future (`payrollVoucherDate`; v10.0.273, TD-1244); «عیدی سال» (`GET /piecework/reports/eidi?year`, `payrollEidiReport`) lists accrued, paid and remaining per personnel with an xlsx export (v10.0.222, TD-1089, payroll duties plan decisions ت۱۴–ت۱۶). A new payroll counts only work logs with no payroll or whose payroll is deleted, and links them in the same transaction. Editing or deleting a work log locks its row in a transaction and accepts only such a free, unpaid log (`lockEditableWorkLog` / `workLogFreeOfLivePayroll` in `src/services/piecework/workLogPayrollLink.ts`, v8.0.76, TD-328). An advance deduction larger than the employee's outstanding advance (`getPersonnelAdvanceBalance`, read only from the personnel's rows on the advance account, mapped 1301; no rows = 0, never treasury rows — v9.0.267, TD-807) is refused (v8.0.29, TD-282, product-owner decision). Fixed salary is granted for every Jalali month of the payroll period, a partial month pro rata by days (`computeFixedSalaryShares` in `src/lib/payroll/fixedSalaryProration.ts`, shared by the server and the payroll form): a month's share is round(salary × cumulative days of that month ÷ its days) minus earlier payrolls' shares of it, recorded per month in `piecework_payrolls.fixed_salary_months` (migration 0048); a payroll issued before v8.0.30 counts as the full month of its start date (v8.0.30, TD-284, product-owner decision). A personnel whose employment status is «قطع همکاری» earns fixed salary only up to its end date (`serviceEndOf` / `fixedSalaryPeriodEnd` in `src/lib/payroll/payrollPeriod.ts`, shared with the payroll form; the same pro rata rule) and nothing after it; such a personnel without a valid end date gets no fixed-salary payslip (422 `PAYROLL_SERVICE_END_DATE_REQUIRED`), and a payslip whose period ends after the business today is refused (422 `PAYROLL_PERIOD_IN_FUTURE`, `payrollPeriodFutureError`) (v9.0.268, TD-808, product-owner decision ت۴), unless it is a prepayment payslip (`is_prepayment`, migration 0103) with a required reason (`prepayment_reason`, kept in its notes, audit row and print): it pays fixed salary only and leaves the period's work logs free, and a prepayment without a reason, of an ended period or of a personnel without fixed salary is 422 `PAYROLL_PREPAYMENT_REASON_REQUIRED` / `PAYROLL_PREPAYMENT_NOT_FUTURE` / `PAYROLL_PREPAYMENT_FIXED_SALARY_ONLY` (`payrollPrepaymentError` in `src/lib/payroll/payrollPeriod.ts`, shared with the payslip form). Fixed salary starts on the personnel's service start date (`personnel.start_date`, ISO, empty = the period's first day; form, detail, Excel import and export), the first month pro rata by days (`serviceStartOf` / `fixedSalaryPeriodStart`, same file) (v10.0.199, TD-1085 / TD-1086, payroll duties plan §3.4). A payroll payment is voided only through `PayrollPaymentVoidService.voidPayrollPayment` (`POST /piecework/payrolls/:id/payments/:transactionId/void`, reason required; the treasury void still refuses payroll payments): in one transaction (locks bank → payroll → treasury row) the bank balance comes back, the payment voucher goes through `voidSourceVoucher`, a reversal treasury row (`reversal_of_id`) is recorded and the payment marked `voided`, and the payroll's paid amount, status and paid work logs step back; a payroll whose payments are all voided can be deleted (v8.0.31, TD-283, product-owner decision). A payroll payment and its void write their «پرداخت حقوق» audit row with the payment's `tx`, the request and the amount (`auditPayrollPaymentRegistered` / `auditPayrollPaymentVoided` in `src/services/piecework/pieceworkAudit.ts`), and the void writes the reversal row's `TreasuryTransactionApproved` event to the outbox in the same transaction (v10.0.259, TD-949 / TD-950). A payroll payment has no cheque method: `registerPayrollPayment` refuses `method: 'cheque'` and the payment form offers bank transfer, cash and POS only (v8.0.120, TD-411, product-owner decision). A payroll payment's date goes through `resolveTreasuryWriteDate` before its transaction, like every treasury write: empty is the business today, a non-existent day is 422 and a date after the business today 422 `TREASURY_DATE_IN_FUTURE`, with nothing written; the payment form sends an ISO date from `JalaliDateInput` (default today) and shows the server's message (v9.0.461, TD-927). Salary is settled only through the payslip payment: a treasury payment to personnel with purpose «settlement» is refused with 422 `TREASURY_PAYROLL_SETTLEMENT_VIA_PAYSLIP` before anything is written (`isSalarySettlementPayment` in `src/lib/treasury/partyPurpose.ts`, shared with the treasury form, which does not offer that purpose for a payment); a personnel receipt with purpose «settlement» stays accepted (v10.0.57, TD-925, decision ت۸ «الف» of the phase 5 review). A payslip's other deductions (3205) are remitted by a personnel payment with purpose «deductions» («واریز کسورات حقوق», offered for payments only), which debits the mapped `employeeDeductionsPayableAccountCode` with the personnel detail (migration 0109 widened `chk_treasury_transactions_purpose` and `chk_cheques_purpose`; v10.0.257, TD-952). A treasury advance (a payment to personnel with purpose «advance») that a live payslip has deducted is not voided: `voidTreasuryTransaction` locks the personnel row after the bank and before the treasury row (the lock payslip issue takes) and refuses with 409 `TREASURY_ADVANCE_DEDUCTED` when the personnel's advance balance without this advance's voucher rows would fall below zero (`assertAdvanceVoidKeepsBalance` in `src/services/accounting/treasury/advanceVoidGuard.ts`); an advance not yet deducted, or one whose payslip was deleted, is voided (v10.0.58, TD-926, decision ت۸ «الف»). A treasury receipt or payment linked to a document is at most the document's remaining amount (payable less its settled amount, `documentEventAmounts` and `settledAmount`), on create and on relink: more is 422 `TREASURY_AMOUNT_EXCEEDS_DOCUMENT_REMAINING` and the excess is recorded on account; the document is locked `FOR NO KEY UPDATE` after the bank, so concurrent payments to one document are checked one after the other (`lockSettlementDocument` / `assertWithinDocumentRemaining` in `src/services/accounting/treasury/documentRemaining.ts`; v10.0.59, TD-938, decision ت۸ «الف»). A payslip's bonuses, deductions and advance deduction are non-negative (route schema 400, `generatePayroll` 422 `PAYROLL_NEGATIVE_COMPONENT`, the form's fields `min="0"`); the payslip voucher credits wages payable with exactly the net or is refused (`PAYROLL_NEGATIVE_COMPONENT` / `PAYROLL_VOUCHER_NET_MISMATCH`, never a silent null); a payslip is paid only with its live voucher (409 `PAYROLL_WITHOUT_VOUCHER`); invariant I10 (`src/tests/invariants/payrollInvariants.ts`) checks both, and older payslips that miss them are only listed by the financial health check (`payroll_voucher_mismatch`, `src/services/piecework/payrollVoucherHealth.ts`) (v9.0.266, TD-804, product-owner decision ت۱).
- **Piecework Rates and Work Logs (package 12 PR ب):** A piecework task's base rate is a non-negative number (`parsePieceworkRate` in `src/lib/piecework/pieceworkRate.ts`, shared by the server and the Excel preview): the task routes refuse a text or negative rate with 400, the service with 422 `PIECEWORK_RATE_INVALID`, and the Excel import writes no row with one and lists it in its `errors` with the row number (v9.0.279, TD-813). A work log needs positive personnel, task and project ids, a quantity above zero or «hh:mm» (`parseWorkQuantity` in `src/lib/piecework/workQuantity.ts`) and a non-negative manual rate (route 400; `normalizeWorkLogEntries` in `src/services/piecework/workLogEntry.ts`, 422 `PIECEWORK_LOG_INVALID`), and its personnel, task and project must be live, read `FOR SHARE` (`assertWorkLogParentsLive`, 422 `PIECEWORK_LOG_PERSONNEL_INVALID` / `PIECEWORK_LOG_TASK_INVALID` / `PIECEWORK_LOG_PROJECT_INVALID`); `logWorkEntries` writes a batch in one transaction, so one bad row saves nothing, and editing a log follows the same quantity, rate and project rules (v9.0.280, TD-812). A log posted from the project workshop schedule (`ProjectScheduleTab`) carries its schedule row (`scheduleRef`: stage, product and row id; `scheduleLogItem` in `src/lib/projects/scheduleWorkLog.ts`) and no rate: the server always gives it the personnel custom rate, else the task base rate, and ignores a sent rate even from a rate manager (v9.0.281, TD-735, package 11 decision ت۴). Such a log is written once per schedule row (v9.0.282, TD-736, package 11 decision ت۵): `logWorkEntries` locks the project row `FOR UPDATE` before the parents' shared locks, requires the row in the saved schedule with the same personnel and task (422 `PIECEWORK_SCHEDULE_ROW_NOT_FOUND` / `PIECEWORK_SCHEDULE_ROW_MISMATCH`), refuses a row that already has a live log (409 `PIECEWORK_SCHEDULE_ROW_LOGGED`) and writes the new log id into the row (`pieceworkLogId`, `src/services/piecework/scheduleRowLink.ts`); deleting the log or moving it to another project frees the row, a project save never sets or drops a link (`keepScheduleLogLinks`), and the tab saves the schedule before logging and reads the row state only from the server (`isScheduleRowLogged`). A schedule log above its row's quantity is 422 `PIECEWORK_SCHEDULE_ROW_QUANTITY_EXCEEDED` (v10.0.185, TD-955), and no work log is dated, or moved, after a terminated personnel's service end, nor recorded for one without a valid end date (422 `PIECEWORK_LOG_AFTER_SERVICE_END`, `assertWorkLogsWithinService` in `src/services/piecework/workLogEntry.ts` with the payroll's `serviceEndOf`; v10.0.186, TD-956; both product-owner decision ت۱۰ of phase 5). The tab dates its logs with its own Jalali picker «تاریخ کارکرد برای ثبت کارمزد» (`JalaliDateInput`, default `getTodayIsoDate`), never the row or project start date (v9.0.283, TD-747, package 11 decision ت۶). A personnel custom rate is saved only through `savePersonnelRate` (`src/services/piecework/personnelRate.ts`, v9.0.284, TD-809): in one transaction under the live personnel row `FOR UPDATE` and the live task `FOR SHARE` (else 422 `PIECEWORK_RATE_PERSONNEL_INVALID` / `PIECEWORK_RATE_TASK_INVALID`), a non-negative required rate, one active row per personnel and task (partial unique index `uq_piecework_personnel_rates_active`, migration 0076, created only on data without duplicates; the financial health check lists duplicates as `piecework_personnel_rate_uniqueness`), and each change writes a `piecework_task_rate_history` row with `personnel_id` (left out of the task base rate history) and a «نرخ اختصاصی پرسنل» audit row with before and after in the same transaction; a work log and the rates page both read the newest active row (`activePersonnelRate`). Every work log create, edit and delete and every payslip issue, status change and delete, and every task title create, delete, restore, clear-all and Excel import (entity «عناوین پرکیسی», v10.1.66), writes its own audit row inside its transaction (`src/services/piecework/pieceworkAudit.ts`, v9.0.285, TD-810): the routes pass `req`, the details carry the snapshot after a create, only the changed fields with before and after on an edit and the snapshot before a delete, and payslip and log statuses use the Persian labels of `src/lib/payroll/payrollStatusLabels.ts`, never the status code. The work log list is filtered in SQL (`GET /piecework/logs`, `workLogConditions` in `src/services/piecework/workLogList.ts`: personnel, project with `none` for no project, status with `processed` for any log on a payslip, a Jalali or ISO range and search; a malformed filter is 400); with `page` it returns one page `{ data, total, page, limit, totalAmount }` counted and summed in SQL, `GET /piecework/logs/summary` gives the page cards and the project labor costs, and the piecework page reads one page of the current Jalali month (`usePieceworkLogList`, contract `src/lib/piecework/workLogList.ts`) and the payslip preview from the server, never the whole ledger (v9.0.330, TD-811).
- **Payroll Actions by Their Own Keys (v9.0.320, TD-805, product-owner decision ت۲ «الف»):** Each payroll write asks the catalog key of its own name through `requirePermission` and the constants of `src/lib/permissions/pieceworkPermissions.ts`: task titles, categories, the Excel import, base and custom rates `piecework.manage_tasks`; work log create, edit and delete `piecework.log` (a manual rate on create or edit only with `piecework.manage_tasks`, otherwise a new log gets the server rate and an edited one keeps its rate); payroll issue, notes, voucher sync and delete `piecework.payroll`, its approval and return to draft `piecework.payroll_approve` (v10.0.193); the yearly insurance and tax rates `payroll.rates_manage` (requires `piecework.view`, granted to no role by default; v10.0.210); a payroll payment and its void the key `piecework.pay` (requires `piecework.view`; its holders also read payrolls and the bank pick list). `personnel.manage` covers the personnel dossier only and writes no piecework record; the task-titles settings tab opens for `piecework.manage_tasks`. Migration 0078 gave every role holding `personnel.manage` piecework.view / manage_tasks / log / payroll / pay and every role holding `settings.manage` piecework.view / manage_tasks, each change logged in `activity_logs`. The piecework page, the payslip and payment windows and the project workshop schedule show each button by the same key (`usePieceworkPermissions`).
- **Voucher Accounts Come From the Account Mapping (v8.0.14, TD-259):** Automatic vouchers resolve inventory (raw materials, work in progress, finished goods), trade payables and trade receivables through `AccountMappingService` — sales, purchase, production receipt, remittance/waste and sales return alike; a hard-coded code is only the fallback when the mapped account is missing, and a stored mapping or disabled list that cannot be read is refused with 422 `ACCOUNT_MAPPING_UNREADABLE`, never replaced with the defaults (v10.0.251, TD-968). The project reports count approved and permanent vouchers only, and the accounting dashboard's cash and bank card sums each currency apart, `totalCashAndBank` being the rial accounts (v10.0.251, TD-966 / TD-967). The waste voucher debits «ضایعات و افت کیفی» (6004, mapping `wasteExpenseAccountCode`) and 6001 is «بهای تمام‌شده کالای فروش‌رفته» (v8.0.114, TD-413, product-owner decision). Only a project remittance debits work in progress (1402, detail the project), its project read only from `documents.project_id`, never from the notes (v10.0.270, OBS-R1-47); a remittance without a project debits «مصرف مواد بی پروژه» (mapping `unassignedConsumptionAccountCode`, default 6003) and a production receipt without a project credits it (v10.1.6, N-06) (v10.0.175, TD-948, product-owner decision ت۴ ب of phase 5). A remittance or waste voucher posts the Kardex cost of its own `out` rows (`documentOutflowCost` in `src/services/accounting/outflowVoucherCost.ts`; an item without a Kardex row at its current WAC, never the line price), so re-syncing a draft never changes it (v8.0.115, TD-400). Payslip «other deductions» (loan instalments, fines) credit «سایر کسورات حقوق پرداختنی» (3205, mapping `employeeDeductionsPayableAccountCode`; v9.0.286, TD-554, product-owner decision ت۶; insurance and income tax have their own rows since v10.0.210, TD-1087); migration 0077 adds 3205 to an existing chart and moves a stored mapping still at the old default 3202 «پیش‌دریافت‌ها از مشتریان» (with an audit row). Payslip vouchers posted to 3202 before are never moved: the financial health check lists each personnel's remaining 3202 balance (`payroll_deductions_in_customer_prepayments`) until a correction voucher with the personnel detail moves it.
