- **Chart of Accounts Integrity (v9.0.197, TD-546, product-owner decision ت۴):** An account that any active row of an active voucher (any status) uses is never deleted: `deleteAccount` locks the account row and refuses with 409 `ACCOUNT_HAS_VOUCHER_ROWS`, pointing to deactivation. `createAccount` always inserts a new row and never revives a deleted account by its code; deleted accounts that still carry rows are only listed by the financial health check (`deleted_accounts_with_voucher_rows`). A manual, edited or correction voucher row goes only on a posting account: active, subsidiary or detailed, without an active sub-account (`postingRefusal` in `src/lib/accounting/postingAccount.ts`, shared with the forms; `assertPostingAccounts` reads the accounts `FOR SHARE`, else 422 `VOUCHER_ACCOUNT_NOT_POSTABLE`), and the voucher forms offer only those; legacy rows elsewhere are listed by the health check (`voucher_rows_on_non_posting_accounts`) (v9.0.198, TD-549). The account mapping concepts (label, default code, allowed account types) live in `src/lib/accounting/accountMappingConcepts.ts`, shared by the server and the page; `saveMappings` stores a code only when it is a posting account of the concept's types (else 422 `ACCOUNT_MAPPING_INVALID` naming each wrong concept; disabled concepts are not checked), and `resolveAccount` and the voucher sync fallbacks take the mapped code, else the concept's default code, never a group or general account; wrong mappings are listed by the health check (`account_mapping_invalid`) (v9.0.199, TD-550). A treasury or cheque party row whose mapped account and its default are both missing gets no account and is refused, never the general account (`resolveContraAccount`; v10.0.262, OWN-03). `updateAccount` locks the account row and checks only the fields that change: a system account takes only a new name and description (409 `ACCOUNT_IS_SYSTEM`), an account with voucher rows keeps its type, nature, level and parent (409 `ACCOUNT_HAS_VOUCHER_ROWS`), and a parent is a non-deleted account exactly one level up outside the account's own subtree (`assertAccountPlacement` in `src/services/accounting/accountPlacement.ts`, 422 `ACCOUNT_PARENT_INVALID` / `ACCOUNT_LEVEL_INVALID`); the tree and the trial balance roll-up make the accounts of a legacy cycle roots (v9.0.200, TD-553). An account code is Latin digits only, at most twenty (`ACCOUNT_CODE_PATTERN` in `src/lib/accounting/accountCode.ts`; Persian and Arabic digits are converted, anything else is 422 `ACCOUNT_CODE_INVALID`), unique among active accounts by its digit-normalized key (`assertAccountCodeAvailable` in `src/services/accounting/accountCodeGuard.ts`; a violation of `uq_accounts_code_active` is the same 409 `ACCOUNT_CODE_TAKEN`) and fixed after creation (422 `ACCOUNT_CODE_IMMUTABLE`; a legacy Persian-digit code turns Latin when its account is saved); codes still not Latin are listed by the health check (`account_code_not_latin`) (v9.0.201, TD-558). Migration 0071 adds CHECK constraints on a live voucher row (non-negative debit and credit, one of them non-zero), a voucher's status and type and an account's level, type and nature (soft-deleted rows exempt), and the foreign key `fk_accounts_parent`, each `NOT VALID` and validated only on clean data; what is left is listed by the health check (`accounting_integrity_constraints`) (v9.0.202, TD-562).
- **Transactional Voucher Generation (DB-008):** `VoucherSync` calls triggered from documents/treasury MUST execute inside the same database transaction (`tx`).
- **Document ↔ Voucher Link (TD-193, v7.0.31):** Vouchers issued by `VoucherSync` for a document carry `journal_vouchers.source_document_id` (unique among active vouchers, migration 0017). Always find a document's voucher by `source_document_id`, never by (`reference_module`, `reference_id`) — reversal/correction/repost vouchers (`REV-V…`, `CORR-V…`, `VOID-REPOST-V…`, `REPOST-V…`) keep `reference_module='invoice'` but store the ORIGINAL VOUCHER id in `reference_id`. A piecework payroll's voucher is found the same way through `journal_vouchers.source_payroll_id` (v7.0.119, TD-242, migration 0035; `src/services/accounting/payrollVoucherLink.ts`), never by `reference_id`.
- **Unique Voucher Numbers (v7.0.91, TD-195):** `journal_vouchers.voucher_number` comes only from `journal_voucher_number_seq` (`VoucherService.getNextVoucherNumber`, also in test factories) and is unique through `uq_jv_voucher_number` (migration 0031, created only when existing data has no duplicate). Old voucher numbers are never renumbered automatically; duplicates are listed by the financial health check (`voucher_number_uniqueness`).
- **No Bulk Accounting/Inventory Work at Boot (TD-193):** Server startup must not run bulk voucher sync or Kardex backfill. Bulk voucher issuance runs only manually via `VoucherSyncService.syncMissingDocumentVouchers` (missing vouchers only, never rewrites existing ones) under advisory lock 91001; Kardex initial backfill via `KardexBackfillService.runExclusive` under lock 91002 (`src/lib/advisoryLock.ts`).

## 12. Kardex Event Sourcing & Inventory Reconciliation
- **Event-Driven Verification:** Calculate true stock balances from sequential `stock_movements` log.
- **Three-Way Invariant:** `item_warehouse_stocks` (truth) = `items.current_stock` (database-maintained sum) = Kardex ledger balance (active rows, excluding reversals of soft-deleted rows). Kardex locations and legacy JSON keys map to warehouses with one rule (`createLedgerLocationResolver`: '' / 'default' = default warehouse, then code, then name, all warehouses). The Kardex rebuild refuses (never silently drops) items with movements in an unresolvable location or a negative per-warehouse balance.
- **Running Kardex Report (v8.0.7, TD-266, product-owner decision «نمایش با برچسب»):** The item Kardex report (`buildItemRunningKardex` in `src/services/inventory/runningKardex.service.ts`, `GET /inventory/item-kardex/:itemId`) lists rows in (day, id) order and shows a voided document's original row on its own date (`isVoided`, label «باطل‌شده») and its reversal on the void date (`isReversal`, «معکوس ابطال»); the running balance counts both, the in/out totals count only ledger rows, each row's WAC is the live engine's WAC right after that row was registered (`createKardexReplayer`), and warehouses are keyed with `createLedgerLocationResolver`. The stock movement chart (`getMonthlyMovementTrends`) counts the same ledger: `LEDGER_ROW_FILTER`, only `in` / `out` rows, no warehouse transfer, from the first day of the window to the end of the current Jalali month (v9.0.94, TD-492). The warehouse dashboard counts the same ledger (`src/services/inventory/dashboardMovementStats.ts`): «recent documents» are distinct documents of `LEDGER_ROW_FILTER` rows, fast-moving, slow-moving and dead stock read only ledger `out` rows without warehouse transfers, and «the last N days» are the business today and the N − 1 days before it (v9.0.289, TD-671).
- **Warehouse Stock Reconciliation (TD-200, v7.0.33):** `WarehouseStockReconciliationService` compares `item_warehouse_stocks` with the Kardex ledger (active rows, excluding reversals of soft-deleted rows). Repairs are manual only, dry-run by default, correct quantities only (never WAC), never auto-adjust negative ledger balances or items with unresolvable Kardex locations, and log every change/refusal to `inventory_reconciliation_anomalies`. Data-fixing migrations must never clamp or overwrite silently: add (`existing + EXCLUDED`) and record anomalies.

## 13. Scalable Changelog Architecture
- **Version Partitioning:** `src/data/changelogs/0.ts` (`v1.0.0`), `archive_1_6.ts` (condensed `v1.x`–`v6.x`, v7.0.11), `7.ts` (`v7.0.0`–`v7.0.140`, closed and frozen in v8.0.0), `8.ts` (`v8.0.0`–`v8.0.128`, closed and frozen in v9.0.0) and `9.ts` (`v9.0.0`–`v9.0.461`, closed and frozen in v10.0.0) are archives; `10.ts` is active. The browser bundles no changelog: the «معرفی و به‌روزرسانی‌ها» page is removed and the menu footer and login page show the version from `GET /api/health` (`useAppVersionLabel`; v10.1.15).
- **Active File:** For version 10.x releases, append to `src/data/changelogs/10.ts` and bump the version in all synced locations listed in §23.
- **Series Closure (v8.0.0, product-owner decision):** The active series and the closed series are declared once in `src/data/changelogs/index.ts` (`ACTIVE_CHANGELOG`; `CLOSED_CHANGELOG_SERIES` with final version, entry count and SHA-256 fingerprint). `npm run check:version` (`src/data/changelogs/seriesGuard.ts`) rejects a `package.json` major outside the active series, an entry of another series in the active file, and any change to a closed series file. Opening the next series repeats v8.0.0 / v9.0.0 / v10.0.0: freeze the current file with its fingerprint, add the new file, archive the roadmap with a closing report and open a new one; `scripts/release.ts` follows `ACTIVE_CHANGELOG` and needs no change.

## 14. Workflow Engine, Visual Canvas & SLA Analytics
1. **Rule Engine (`ruleConditionsJson`):** Evaluate context variables using operators (`eq`, `neq`, `gt`, `gte`, `lt`, `lte`, `in`, `contains`). A document's `amount` / `finalAmount` / `totalAmount` is its payable amount in IRR — net of active lines + VAT + service charge, times the document's exchange rate, else the `exchange_rate_<cur>` setting (`workflowDocumentAmount`, v8.0.123, TD-404); a foreign document without any rate has amount `NaN`, so no amount comparison passes. The stepper shows that amount in IRR and a foreign document's own amount in its currency beside it (v9.0.61, TD-466). A transition's rule is validated when the design is saved (422), and a stored node that is neither a rule (`field` + `operator`) nor a group (`conditions` / `rules` array) evaluates closed (`INVALID_RULE_OPERATOR` in `src/services/ruleEngine.service.ts`); only an empty expression (`null`, `[]`, `{}`) means no condition (v9.0.48, TD-456). A numeric comparison (`gt` / `gte` / `lt` / `lte`) reads both sides with Persian or Arabic digits and thousands separators, and `eq` / `neq` compare a numeric value with a typed number as numbers (`ruleComparableNumber` / `numericRuleEquality` in `src/lib/events/ruleConditionValue.ts`, built on `normalizeDecimalString`); text keeps its exact comparison, and the rule editor saves a numeric comparison's value as a number and refuses one that is not a number (`checkRuleConditionValue`; v9.0.409, TD-727).
2. **Parallel Approvals (`approvalProgressJson`):** Support `SINGLE`, `AND_ALL`, `OR_ANY`, and `K_OF_N` multi-signature rules.
3. **Immutable DSL Versioning, Drafts and Publishing (`snapshotDsl`, v7.0.87, TD-112; drafts v10.1.21, TD-1270, D-06 part D, product-owner decision ت۱۰ of the workflow plan):** Every definition save (`saveWorkflowDefinition` / `createDefinition`) writes states, transitions and a new `workflow_definition_versions` row in ONE transaction (`recordDefinitionVersion` in `src/services/workflow/workflowSnapshot.ts`: snapshot of the tables with database ids, `workflow_definitions.version` bumped). The first save of a new definition (designer, template, seed) is published at once; every later save of an existing definition is a draft (version status `draft`; the live step and action tables hold it) that no new record sees until it is published. A design is saved only with exactly one initial step, at least one terminal step, unique step keys, every action's source and target found and no action out of a terminal step except the `rejected` one (`workflowDesignErrors` in `src/lib/workflow/workflowDesignRules.ts`, also checked by the designer before saving; else 422; v9.0.45, TD-452). `startInstance` stores the snapshot of the published version on the instance (`workflow_definitions.published_version`, `publishedVersionOf`; one `published` row per definition by `uq_wdv_definition_published`, migration 0114, which published every existing definition's latest version), a published version without a usable snapshot is 409 `WF_PUBLISHED_VERSION_UNUSABLE`, and a running instance keeps executing against its own snapshot. An entity has at most one `IN_PROGRESS` instance: `startInstance` takes the transaction advisory lock (namespace `WORKFLOW_ENTITY_START` 91009, `hashtext(type:id)`) before looking for one (same definition returns it, another definition is 409 `WF_INSTANCE_ALREADY_OPEN`), backed by the partial unique index `uq_workflow_instances_open_entity` (migration 0056, created only on data without duplicates; the financial health check lists duplicates as `workflow_open_instance_uniqueness`) (v9.0.37, TD-455). The workflow tables carry foreign keys only among themselves (migration 0059: steps, actions, instances and versions to their definition, action endpoints to steps, history, tasks and pending approvals to their instance with `ON DELETE CASCADE`; added `NOT VALID`, validated only on clean data, gaps listed by the financial health check as `workflow_reference_integrity`); step, action and user ids held by instances, history, tasks and approvals have none, because they are snapshot ids or users that may be removed. A version number is unique per definition (`uq_wdv_definition_version`) and `recordDefinitionVersion` takes it under the definition row lock `FOR NO KEY UPDATE`, which does not block the foreign-key lock of an instance start (v9.0.51, TD-461). A snapshot without database ids (pre-v7.0.87 version 1 rows holding the raw designer payload) is never used (`isUsableSnapshot`; the current tables are read instead). Version history (`GET /workflow/definitions/:id/versions[/:version]`, «تاریخچه نسخه‌ها» on the workflow page) shows each version's status (draft, published, retired) and is never rewritten; `GET /workflow/definitions/:id/draft` gives the draft and what it changes against the published version (`workflowVersionDiff` in `src/lib/workflow/workflowVersionDiff.ts`: steps by key, actions by their steps and key, never by id). Only `workflow.admin` (`WORKFLOW_SWITCH_PERMISSION`, decision ت۴) publishes the draft it looked at (`POST /workflow/definitions/:id/publish` with `draftVersion`: a draft saved meanwhile 409 `WF_DRAFT_CHANGED`, none 409 `WF_NO_DRAFT`, one against the design rules or naming a missing role 422) or reverts to an earlier version (`POST /workflow/definitions/:id/revert`: that version's snapshot becomes the design and is published as a new version, any draft set aside, the audit row naming the version reverted from; a version without a usable snapshot or of another record type is 422 `WF_VERSION_NOT_RESTORABLE`), each in one transaction under the record type lock and the definition row lock with its audit row written with `tx` (`src/services/workflow/workflowVersionPublish.ts`). The lifecycle switches, the role delete guard, the boot seed guard upgrades (which skip a definition with a draft) and the unguarded-action health checks read the published version, never the draft (`publishedDesignOf`, `ensurePublishedSnapshot`, `publishedWorkflowActions`).
4. **Canvas Position Persistence:** Persist node coordinates (`positionX`, `positionY`), `slaHours` and `stepOrder` in `workflow_states`; a step saved without a positive `stepOrder` takes its list position, and the designer keeps the stored order (v9.0.47, TD-454). `POST /workflow/positions` takes the definition id and moves only that definition's steps, in one transaction under the definition row lock with an audit row (another definition's step is 422); the write routes `/transition`, `/definitions`, `/positions` and `/delegations` validate their bodies with the Zod schemas in `src/routes/workflowRouteSchemas.ts` (v9.0.50, TD-459). A workflow approval or transition invalidates the documents, procurement, accounting and inventory queries (`workflowChange` in `src/lib/queryInvalidation.ts`), and saving a design or step positions refetches the definition detail (`QUERY_KEYS.workflow.definition`) (v9.0.64, TD-469).
5. **SLA Analytics:** SLA compliance and bottleneck states derived from `workflow_history_logs`: time in the current step counts from the latest history row that entered it (`currentStepEnteredAt` in `src/services/workflow/workflowSlaEvaluator.ts`; a partial signature `<action>_SIGN` is no entry, so it never restarts the count), compared with `slaHours`, and history is read in (`created_at`, `id`) order (v10.1.54, OBS-R2-72 / OBS-R2-69). A running instance's step and its `slaHours` come from the instance's own snapshot, counted under the current step with the same key (v9.0.49, TD-457). Every `WorkflowEngineService` facade method is bound to its own class (`.bind`), so a static method's `this` is never the facade (v9.0.43, TD-450).
7. **SLA Reminder, No Auto-Expiry (v7.0.101, TD-085, product-owner decisions):** A task whose `due_at` passed stays `pending`; `WorkflowSlaReminderService` (every minute, advisory lock 91006) sends its assignee — assigned / candidate users plus their active delegate, else the users of the task's role (a roleless `ALL` task: only admins and holders of `workflow.approve` / `workflow.execute`, v9.0.44, TD-460, product-owner decision ت۱۰), in both cases only those holding the transition's required permission, read from the instance's snapshot (v9.0.128, TD-542) — ONE in-app notification and stamps `sla_reminded_at` (no repeats, no admin copy, no SMS/email). Tasks auto-expired before v7.0.101 were reopened (current step of a running instance) or kept, all listed in `workflow_task_reopen_log` and the SLA analytics tab. Never reintroduce automatic expiry.
6. **Instance API Contract (v7.0.88, TD-085):** `GET /workflow/instance/:entityType/:entityId` returns `{ instance, definition, currentState, allStates, availableTransitions, history, approvalProgress, entityContext }` (`WorkflowInstanceData` in `useWorkflowQueries.ts`, read by `WorkflowStepperWidget`), or `{ instance: null }`; `allStates` come from the instance's own snapshot ordered by `stepOrder`. Signature progress shows the server's `requiredCount`. Transitions the user's role allows but whose `ruleConditionsJson` fail come back in `blockedTransitions` with Persian `unmetConditions`; every transition carries Persian `conditions` (`src/lib/workflowRuleText.ts`, also used for rule-failure errors) — never English field/operator text (v7.0.89).
8. **Inbox Tasks Run Their Own Transition (v8.0.90, TD-370):** `executeTaskById` approves with the task's own transition (a task no longer of the current step gets 409 `WF_TASK_STALE`) and rejects only with a reject transition of the current step, read from the instance's snapshot (`transitionsFromState`): the chosen `transitionId`, else the only one the user may run; a step without one is refused (422 `WF_TASK_NO_REJECT_TRANSITION`), never approved. The task closes only when its transition fires (v8.0.91, TD-371): a signature that does not meet the quorum, or a repeated one, leaves it pending in every signer's inbox. Tasks and the deadline of a newly entered step come from the instance's own snapshot (`refreshPendingApprovals` with `snapshotDsl`, v8.0.92, TD-372), never from the live tables, whose ids change on every design save. Entering a step clears the signatures of that step's transitions (v8.0.93, TD-373), so after a return or rejection the same user can act again and an old signature never counts toward a new quorum. A step with a required role passes only for that exact role (case-insensitive) and the system admin; no equivalent role, department posting permission or `*` passes it, and who may sign is the transition's `requiredPermission` (`checkUserRoleMatch`, v9.0.128, TD-542, permission model §4.4; same-department roles and posting permissions passed from v8.0.94, TD-374). A design is saved only with defined roles, stored with the roles table's code (`resolveTransitionRoles` in `src/services/workflow/transitionRoles.ts`, else 422 `WF_UNKNOWN_ROLE`), and catalog permission keys (`workflowDesignErrors`); a role named by a live transition or an unfinished instance's snapshot is not deleted (409 `ROLE_USED_BY_WORKFLOW`); the default journal-voucher workflow asks permissions only (approve, finalize and back to draft `accounting.vouchers_approve` since v10.0.27, TD-965; reject `accounting.vouchers`). Migration 0065 turned each stored guard that only the removed rules made work into its permission and listed in `activity_logs` every role step whose role is missing or that another role used to sign, for the admin to decide; the financial health check shows those still standing (`workflow_role_review`, `src/services/workflow/workflowRoleReview.ts`). A live or published action whose `requiredPermission` is not a catalog key (such as the removed `audit.create`) is listed by the financial health check until its design names a catalog key (`workflow_unknown_permission_action`, `src/services/accounting/workflowUnknownPermissionHealth.ts`; v10.1.66). Since v10.2.0 the boot rewrites `audit.create` to `audit.apply` in a published definition without a draft, publishing a new version with a «طرح گردش کار» audit row in its transaction (`replaceRemovedWorkflowPermissions` in `src/services/workflow/removedPermissionKeys.ts`; its failure is logged and never stops the boot seed), a definition holding a draft is left alone and stays listed, and a running instance's snapshot that still asks the removed key is signed by the holders of its replacement (`REPLACED_PERMISSION_KEYS`, read only through `effectivePermissionKey`, an own-property lookup used by `holdsRequiredPermission`, the inbox and its counts and the task recipients, and a refusal names the replacement; v10.2.10, TD-1320). The engine reads the signer's role permissions itself (`signerPermissions`, never only `req.user.permissions`, which the token does not carry), and the inbox, its counts and task execution apply the same `checkUserRoleMatch`; only the `admin` role signs every step — `workflow.manage` / `workflow.admin` are design permissions and sign no one else's step (v9.0.34, TD-444, product-owner decision ت۱). The default document workflow is guarded by permissions only — warehouse review and its reject `warehouse.out`, accounting review and its reject `accounting.vouchers`, direct approval `workflow.admin` (`DOC_APPROVAL_STEP_GUARDS` in `src/services/workflow/docApprovalGuards.ts`); an installed definition is upgraded only when it is exactly the old seed, otherwise the financial health check lists document approvals with neither role nor permission (`workflow_unguarded_document_approval`). A step that runs a domain action also needs the entity's own permission from the signer (or the delegator's role): finalize `documents.edit` / `warehouse.in` / `warehouse.out`, voucher status `accounting.vouchers` (approved, permanent or back to draft `accounting.vouchers_approve`, v10.0.27), item opening `products.create`, bank opening `accounting.treasury`, a purchase requisition entering `received` `warehouse.in` (v9.0.455, TD-904) (`requiredPermissions` of the domain's registration, 403 `WF_ENTITY_PERMISSION_REQUIRED`; v9.0.35, TD-445, product-owner decisions ت۲ «فقط مجوز» and ت۳). The inbox tabs (`GET /workflow/tasks/my-tasks?status=`, `MY_TASK_FILTERS`) are pending, overdue (pending with `due_at < now()` in SQL, `isOverdue` in each row), delegated (pending tasks reached only through a delegation) and completed (the user's own step actions and signatures from `workflow_history_logs`, paginated in SQL); `/tasks/stats` counts each with the same rule (v9.0.41, TD-448, product-owner decision ت۶). The inbox has only this tasks view: the instances view and `GET /workflow/inbox` were removed (v9.0.42, TD-449, product-owner decision ت۷). Opening or closing an inbox task resets its decision, comment and chosen reject action (v9.0.57, TD-462). Each inbox row carries the current step's reject actions (`rejectTransitions`: id, title, from the instance snapshot); with more than one, the task form asks which and sends it as `transitionId` (v9.0.58, TD-463). The task form reads its document or requisition through `useApprovalTaskEntity` (`src/hooks/useApprovalTaskEntity.ts`), which aborts the previous task's request and ignores a late response (v9.0.59, TD-464). An inbox row carries `currentStepTitle` (from the instance snapshot), `delegationInfo`, `instance.startedByName` and a numeric IRR `amount` (`inboxEntityFields` in `src/services/workflow/workflowInboxRows.ts`; v9.0.60, TD-465). Task execution locks the instance row before the task row (v8.0.95, TD-375), the same order as a direct transition. An AND_ALL step passes when every non-deleted user holding its required role has signed (`andAllMemberIds`, product-owner decision «همه اعضای نقش», v8.0.96, TD-376); a signer outside the role never fills a member's place, and a step without a role (or a role without members) needs the designer's K (at least 1). During an active delegation (not revoked, within its window, scope `ALL` / `*` / the workflow code, neither user deleted) the delegate sees and runs the delegator's tasks — assigned, candidate or the delegator's role (`delegationForTask`, `WorkflowDelegationService.activeDelegations`); a transition the delegate's own role does not cover is signed through `resolveSigner` in the delegator's name with `signedBy` = the delegate, and neither of them signs that step again (product-owner decision «کارهای نقش او», v8.0.97, TD-377). A new delegation's scope is `ALL` / `*` or the code of a defined workflow (else 422), and the delegation form offers only these (v9.0.62, TD-467, product-owner decision ت۶). Only the delegator or an admin revokes a delegation (403 otherwise, under the delegation row lock); invalid delegation input is a `ValidationError` and a missing delegation or user a `NotFoundError` (v8.0.98, TD-378). Workflow API responses send server timestamps with a `Z` (`withUtcTimestamps` in `src/services/workflow/workflowTimestamps.ts`), and a delegation given as days runs from the start of its first day to the end of its last day in the display time zone (v9.0.63, TD-468). A purchase requisition workflow action runs only a transition from the instance's current step (`executeWorkflowAction`; otherwise 409 `WF_ACTION_NOT_IN_STEP`, never a direct status change), and the requisition status comes only from the transition's target step; a `REJECTED` instance continues only through a transition drawn out of its rejected step (reopen), a `COMPLETED` one never (v8.0.99, TD-379). The action never rewrites the instance's step from the requisition status (v8.0.124, TD-405), and a received requisition takes no workflow action (409). A transition with a `requiredPermission` runs and is offered only for an admin or a user whose role (read with the same `tx`) or own permissions include it (`holdsRequiredPermission`, 403 `WF_PERMISSION_REQUIRED`; a delegate is checked against the delegator's role), and the designer shows and keeps the field (product-owner decision «بررسی شود», v8.0.100, TD-391). Receiving an unapproved requisition first runs the current step's approve transition in the receiver's name through `executeTransition` (role and permission checked, recorded in the history), then receives; without approval rights it is refused and nothing enters stock (product-owner decision «تأیید با نام او», v8.0.101, TD-390). A transition with `is_initiator_excluded = 1` (migration 0050, designer option «آغازکننده تأیید نکند», off by default) is refused (403 `WF_INITIATOR_EXCLUDED`) for the instance's `started_by` — signing for themselves, as someone's delegate or through their own delegate — and is left out of their inbox, task count and offered transitions (`initiatorExcluded`); an admin is exempt (product-owner decision «گزینه در هر گام», v8.0.102, TD-392). A transition with `is_initiator_only = 1` (migration 0099, designer option «فقط آغازکننده اجرا کند», off by default, never together with «آغازکننده تأیید نکند») runs only for the instance's `started_by`, in their own name or through a delegate signing in their name; it is refused for anyone else with 403 `WF_INITIATOR_ONLY` and left out of their offered transitions and inbox (`initiatorOnlyRefused`), an instance without a starter runs it only for the admin, and the admin is exempt. The default document workflow's «ارسال به انبار» asks `documents.create` and is initiator-only, so only the seller who recorded a proforma sends it, and its «بازگشایی» asks `documents.edit`; an untouched installed definition is upgraded at boot (`DOC_APPROVAL_SUBMIT_GUARD_UPGRADE`), an edited one is only listed by the financial health check (`workflow_unguarded_document_step`, `src/services/accounting/unguardedDocumentStepHealth.ts`) (v10.0.120, TD-1220, guide test finding B-01).
9. **Transition Actions Run in the Approval Transaction (v9.0.2, TD-415, product-owner decision «همگام، درون تراکنش تأیید»):** What a transition does to its entity — finalizing a document on `approved` / `POST_INVOICE`, the item and bank-account opening vouchers, a journal voucher's status, a purchase requisition's status and goods receipt — is registered by its domain with `registerWorkflowTransitionAction` (`src/services/workflow/workflowTransitionActions.ts`; composition root `registerWorkflowDomainActions` in `src/services/system/workflowDomainActions.ts`, called by `server.ts` and the test bootstrap) and run by `executeTransition` with the transition's own `tx` before its outbox records; its error refuses the transition and the caller's transaction and reaches the approver. A domain's `lockEntity` locks the entity row before the instance row (`executeTransition` and `executeTaskById`). There is no in-process workflow emitter: the transition's audit log is written with `tx`, and `WorkflowTransitioned` / `WorkflowCompleted` / `WorkflowRejected` go only through the outbox. A requisition entering `received` by any path is received with `receiveRequisitionItems` unless already received; a missing user id is never replaced with 1.
10. **Definition Lifecycle, One Active per Record Type (v10.1.7, TD-1229, D-06 part A, decision ت۲۷):** A record type has at most one active workflow definition (partial unique index `uq_workflow_definitions_active_entity_type`, migration 0110, built only on clean data; the financial health check lists duplicates as `workflow_active_definition_uniqueness`). A definition is turned on, off, archived and restored only through `setWorkflowDefinitionActive` / `setWorkflowDefinitionArchived` (`src/services/workflow/workflowDefinitionLifecycle.ts`; `POST /workflow/definitions/:id/activate|deactivate|archive|restore`, `WORKFLOW_SWITCH_PERMISSION` = `workflow.admin`), each in one transaction under the record type's advisory lock (`WORKFLOW_DEFINITION_TYPE` 91070) and the definition row lock, with its audit row (entity «طرح گردش کار») written with `tx`; activating beside another active definition needs `replaceActive` (else 409 `WF_ACTIVE_DEFINITION_EXISTS` naming it) and turns that one off in the same transaction; an archived definition is never activated (409) and an active one never archived (409); a definition is never deleted. The purchase requisition workflow is never turned off (409 `WF_REQUIRED_WORKFLOW`, `WORKFLOW_REQUIRED_ENTITY_TYPES` in `src/lib/workflow/workflowLifecycle.ts`), and a requisition starts with the active definition of its type, never a fixed code. A new definition is active only when its type has none, otherwise saved inactive with a notice; the designer shows the record type (`WorkflowEntityTypeField`) and the save changes it only on an inactive definition no record ever entered (422 `WF_ENTITY_TYPE_LOCKED`, `workflowEntityTypeChangeRefusal`); every design save writes its audit row with `tx`. Running instances keep their own snapshot whatever happens to the definition. Part B (v10.1.11, TD-1263, decisions ت۴–ت۷): «ساخت از الگو» (`POST /workflow/definitions/from-template`, `workflow.manage` / `workflow.admin`, `makeDefinitionFromTemplate` in `src/services/workflow/workflowTemplateDefinition.ts`) makes an inactive definition from one of the four templates of `src/lib/workflow/workflowTemplates.ts` (item, bank account, purchase requisition, journal voucher; actions guarded by permission keys only; the purchase requisition and journal voucher seeds read the same templates), and the definition form offers only `WORKFLOW_ENTITY_TYPES` (`WorkflowCreateModal`). A workflow starts only on the server, in its record's create transaction: `POST /workflow/start` and the widget's start button are gone. A manual draft voucher (`POST /accounting/vouchers`) enters the active journal voucher workflow in its create transaction, and while that workflow runs the voucher page does not approve, finalize or put it back to draft (409 `VOUCHER_IN_WORKFLOW`, `assertVoucherNotInWorkflow` in `src/services/accounting/voucherWorkflowLock.ts`; a batch leaves it out with that reason); the voucher list and detail carry `inWorkflow` and the page shows «در گردش کار»; reversing or correcting a voucher closes its workflow (`terminateVoucherWorkflows`). Part C (v10.1.16, TD-1268 / TD-1265, decisions ت۸ / ت۹): an approver gives a deputy from the inbox («تفویض کارهایم», `MyDelegationsWindow`, shown by `WORKFLOW_DELEGATE_PERMISSIONS`) with the one delegation form (`DelegationForm`, users from `/users/list-simple`, scopes from `GET /workflow/delegation-scopes`: workflows not archived) and reads «تفویض‌های من» (`GET /workflow/delegations?mine=1`, given and received, revoke only what one gave); a delegation whose window and scope (`ALL` meets every scope) meet a live delegation of the same delegator is 409 `WF_DELEGATION_OVERLAP` naming it, checked under the transaction advisory lock `WORKFLOW_DELEGATION_FROM_USER` (91080) and written with its audit row in one transaction (`findOverlappingDelegation` in `src/services/workflow/workflowDelegationService.ts`); a deputy signing in the delegator's name is the delegator for a domain rule about who may act (`actingForUserId` on `WorkflowTransitionEvent`), so the maker rule of a journal voucher is checked for both and a maker never approves their own voucher through a deputy. The system admin closes a running record that cannot move on («بستن فرایند» in the workflow widget; `POST /workflow/instances/:id/terminate`, `workflow.admin`, a reason of at least `WORKFLOW_TERMINATE_REASON_MIN` characters): under the instance row lock only, instance `TERMINATED`, pending tasks canceled, one `terminate` history row with the reason and an audit row with `tx` (`terminateWorkflowInstance` in `src/services/workflow/workflowInstanceTerminate.ts`); only a sales document, a journal voucher or a raw material request is closed (`workflowTerminateRefusal` in `src/lib/workflow/workflowLifecycle.ts`), a purchase requisition, item or bank account is 409 `WF_INSTANCE_TERMINATE_REFUSED`, since its orders, receipt or opening voucher come only from its workflow.

## 15. Enterprise Domain Event Bus & Outbox Pattern
- **Event Catalog:** Domain events follow `BaseDomainEvent` contract (`eventId`, `eventType`, `aggregateType`, `aggregateId`, `payload`, `metadata`, `occurredAt`). A sales or purchase document event (`InvoiceCreated` / `InvoiceApproved` / `PurchaseCreated` / `PurchaseApproved`, on create and on finalize) carries the stored document's payable amount from `documentEventAmounts` (`src/services/documents/documentEventAmount.ts`, in the event's transaction): `totalAmount` in the document currency (net of active lines + VAT + service charge), `netAmount`, `vatAmount`, `serviceChargeAmount` and `totalAmountIrr` (null for a foreign document without a rate); the rule editor's field list (`src/lib/eventPayloadFields.ts`) follows the real payload of each published type (v9.0.407, TD-713). Voiding a sales invoice or proforma writes `InvoiceVoided` with the same amounts in the void transaction (`recordVoidEvents` in `src/services/documents/voidDependents.ts`), and each reversed Kardex row of a void, transfers aside, writes `StockReceived` / `StockIssued` (`applyStockReversal`), so an item's stock events add up to its stock (v10.0.229, TD-931, product-owner decision ت۱۱) A sales document recorded final directly writes `InvoiceCreated` before `InvoiceApproved`, like a proforma finalized later (v10.0.230, TD-935, same decision). A cheque status change writes `ChequeStatusChanged` (aggregate `Cheque`: old and new status, amount, party, the vouchers of the step) after its audit row, a payslip's approval `PayrollApproved` and each payslip payment `PayrollPaid` (aggregate `Payroll`, with the payment's treasury row and whether it settled the payslip, never the personnel's name or an amount, which stay behind the payroll read keys; `recordPayrollEvent` in `src/services/piecework/payrollEvents.ts`), each in the change's own transaction, so rules and webhooks can react to them (v10.1.62, OBS-R2-13, decision ت۴۰). A payment's void writes `PayrollPaymentVoided` and a payslip's delete `PayrollDeleted` the same way, and the `TreasuryTransactionApproved` of a payslip payment or of its void carries only the treasury row, its bank and the payslip, never an amount, the remaining balance, the employee's name or the free text that holds them (`payrollTreasuryEventPayload`, same file; v10.1.66).
- **DomainEventBus:** Central singleton dispatcher (`src/services/events/domainEventBus.ts`) with non-blocking handlers. An event recorded during a request carries that request's `requestId` and `correlationId` in its metadata, and a dispatch without a request (the outbox worker) runs its handlers under them (`eventRequestContext`), so the audit rows they write carry the origin trace id (v10.1.56, OBS-R2-16). «پردازش اکنون» (`POST /events/outbox/process-now`) drains the outbox outside the pressing request (`processPendingBatchAsWorker`), so each event's handlers carry that event's own ids, never the administrator's (v10.1.66). The stock-issue reorder alert is written to the outbox once per stock event (`recordReorderAlertOfIssue` in `src/services/events/domainEventHandlers.ts`, id `<stock event id>_reorder`, `OutboxService.recordEventOnce`), never only published in process (OBS-R2-17).
- **Transactional Outbox:** Write events atomically inside `tx` via `OutboxService.recordEvent(tx, event)`. `OperationalPurgeService` (`src/services/events/operationalPurge.service.ts`, started by `server.ts`, advisory lock 91100) deletes daily the expired idempotency keys and the completed events processed more than `OUTBOX_COMPLETED_RETENTION_DAYS` (90) days ago, in batches of `OPERATIONAL_PURGE_BATCH_SIZE` (`src/lib/batchDelete.ts`), never one whose dead-letter row is not yet replayed and never a dead-letter row, so the event timeline shows outbox events of the last 90 days only (v10.1.52, OBS-R2-10).
- **Exponential Backoff Worker:** Polls pending events, dispatches, retries (5s, 10s, 20s... max 5 attempts).
- **Tracked Dispatch (TD-183, v7.0.25):** `publish()` stays fire-and-forget (non-blocking, errors only logged). The outbox worker and DLQ replay MUST use `domainEventBus.dispatchTracked(event, completedHandlers)`, which awaits every handler and reports failures. Every handler is registered with a stable name (`subscribe(type, fn, name)`) and MUST rethrow its errors after logging; succeeded handler names are persisted in `outbox_events.completed_handlers` so retries never re-run a handler that already succeeded.
- **Durable Integration Deliveries (v9.0.378, TD-705, product-owner decision ت۲):** Each delivery of an event to an integration has its own row in `integration_delivery_jobs` (migration 0086): a webhook subscription × event (`webhook`, at most the subscription's retry limit, a whole number from 1 to 10, default 3; the form keeps a stored value with `??`, v10.1.66) and a matching rule's action × event (`rule_action`, `RULE_ACTION_MAX_ATTEMPTS` 5), unique per (kind, target, event), so a delivery that succeeded is never sent again. The outbox handlers only record these rows (their error goes back to the outbox); the first attempt runs at once (a rule's awaited, a webhook's not) and `IntegrationDeliveryService` (`src/services/events/integrationDelivery.service.ts`, worker started by `server.ts`) retries a failed one after `deliveryBackoffSeconds` (5, 10, 20 … at most 300 s). After the cap, or at once for a key the server cannot decrypt, the row is `failed` and moved to the DLQ on its own row (`<eventId>#webhook:<id>` / `#rule:<id>`, source `webhook` / `action_engine`, `metadata.deliveryJobId`); its replay runs only that delivery and the requeue puts it back in its own queue, not the outbox. A deleted or inactive subscription or rule closes the row (`cancelled`). One delivery keeps one `X-ERP-Delivery-Id` (`whd_<job id>`) across its attempts; never retry with in-memory timers. A rule's `execution_count` and a subscription's delivery counters are read under the row's lock (`FOR UPDATE`, taken before the log row is inserted) and written in the transaction of the attempt's log row (v9.0.379, TD-718).
- **Dead Letter Queue (DLQ):** Failed events transfer to `dead_letter_queue` with replay/dismiss endpoints. Replay and dismiss of one DLQ row run under that row's session advisory lock (`withDeadLetterRowLock`, `withRowAdvisoryLock` namespace 91007; never an open transaction around the handlers): a concurrent replay or dismiss is refused (409) and the outbox requeue skips a row being replayed. A replayed event is never replayed or dismissed again; a dismissed one may be replayed (v8.0.75, TD-342).
- **Automated Event Actions (v9.0.377, TD-712, product-owner decision ت۴):** `EventActionEngineService` runs a rule's action: an in-app notification, a webhook or an audit-log row (`RULE_ACTION_TYPES` in `src/lib/events/ruleActionTypes.ts`, shared with the rule editor). The fake `workflow_trigger` and `sms_simulation` actions are removed: a rule is never created, activated or switched to them (422 `RULE_ACTION_TYPE_RETIRED`, `assertRuleActionTypeAllowed`); migration 0085 deactivated the earlier rules of those types and recorded them in `event_action_rule_retirements`, and the financial health check lists them (`event_rule_retired_action`) until their action changes. `POST /events/action-rules/test-draft` really evaluates a draft (`evaluateRuleDraft` in `src/services/events/ruleDraftEvaluation.ts`): an unknown event type or action type, an invalid condition or an incomplete action is 422 `RULE_DRAFT_INVALID`, otherwise the conditions are evaluated on the sample event (`ruleSampleEvent`) and what the action would do is only shown; nothing is sent or written. The rule execution log and the engine stats have one contract shared by the server and the «اقدام‌های خودکار» tab (`src/lib/events/actionLogContract.ts`: `GET /events/action-logs` answers `{ data, total, limit, offset }`, `GET /events/action-rules/stats` `logsTotal` / `avgLatencyMs` …; v9.0.408, TD-721).
- **HMAC-SHA256 Signatures:** Webhooks sign payload with HMAC-SHA256 and attach `X-ERP-Signature-256` and `X-ERP-Delivery-Id`.
- **Webhook SSRF Guard and Echo Simulator (v9.0.356, TD-704, product-owner decision ت۴):** every outgoing webhook request, the connection test included (`pingTest`), uses `redirect: 'manual'` and refuses a 3xx; the echo-simulator exception of `assertSafeExternalUrl` holds only through `isLocalEchoTarget` (`src/lib/ssrfGuard.ts`): `NODE_ENV` test or development, a loopback host, this server's own `PORT` and the exact path `LOCAL_ECHO_PATH`; in production the simulator is unreachable. A host name is checked on both its A and AAAA records and every address it resolves to, each lookup waiting at most `DNS_LOOKUP_TIMEOUT_MS` (3 s, `withDnsTimeout`); an explicit port is 80, 443 or an unprivileged port outside `DISALLOWED_PORTS` (`isAllowedTargetPort`); and a name that does not resolve is accepted only under example.com in test and development (`isUnresolvedTestHost`), never by a substring; a test lets its in-process stand-in of a partner site through only by its exact loopback origin with `allowTestLoopbackOrigin`, only under `NODE_ENV` test (v10.1.59, OBS-R2-81, decision ت۴۰). A webhook delivery, its ping and a rule's webhook action are sent only through `guardedWebhookFetch` (`src/services/events/guardedWebhookFetch.ts`, Node's http / https by hand, no library): the socket resolves the name with `guardedDnsLookup` (`src/lib/ssrfGuard.ts`), so a name that answers an inside address when it connects (DNS rebinding) is refused before anything is sent, a redirect is never followed and comes back as the 3xx the caller records as a failure, the answer body is cut at `WEBHOOK_RESPONSE_MAX_BYTES`, and the request keeps `Accept: */*` and a `User-Agent` (the caller's, else `Papital-ERP/<version>`) as `fetch` sent them (`webhookRequestHeaders`); only the echo simulator and a test's own loopback origin connect without the check, and tests replace the network only with `setWebhookTransportForTests` (v10.2.7, OBS-R2-81 part 4, TD-1296).
- **No Rule Rewriting at Start (v9.0.357, TD-715, product-owner decision ت۴):** `seedDefaultRules` only seeds an empty rule table and never reads or rewrites saved rules; its echo-simulator webhook rule is seeded only in test and development (`isEchoSimulatorEnvironment`). The system's echo token (`ERP_WEBHOOK_SECRET_TOKEN` / `erp_webhook_secret_token`) is sent as `X-ERP-Signature-Token` only to `isLocalEchoTarget`; a rule holding it sends no token to another address (`systemTokenWithheld` in the result).
- **Masked Webhook Key Never Stored (v9.0.358, TD-719):** a webhook edit changes the signing key only for an entered key (`isEnteredSecret` in `src/lib/secrets/maskedSecret.ts`: not empty and not a mask such as «********» or «****…abcd»), so the masked key a form holds is never saved. `POST /webhooks/ping` with `subscriptionId` signs with the stored key and sends the stored custom headers only to the stored address; a draft ping without an entered key is signed with a one-time `generateSecretKey()`, never a fixed key (`keySource` in the answer).
- **Server-Made Webhook Key and Timeout (v9.0.359, TD-720):** the browser never makes a webhook signing key; a webhook created without an entered key gets `WebhookSubscriptionService.generateSecretKey()` (`crypto.randomBytes`). Its timeout is `timeoutMs` (legacy `timeoutSeconds` × 1000) through `resolveWebhookTimeoutMs` (`src/lib/events/webhookTimeout.ts`, shared with the form), from 1,000 to 30,000 ms on create and edit (else 422 `WEBHOOK_TIMEOUT_INVALID`).
- **Webhook Event Patterns Are Published Types (v9.0.405, TD-707, product-owner decision ت۳):** the event types the server publishes are listed once, with Persian labels, in `PUBLISHED_EVENT_TYPES` (`src/lib/events/eventTypeCatalog.ts`, shared by the server and the webhook form; Vitest `eventTypeCatalog.test.ts` compares it with every `createEvent` / `publishEvent` call, so a new event type is added there in the same change). A subscription's patterns are «*» or such types (`resolveEventPatterns` in `src/services/events/webhookEventPatterns.ts`; create, edit and activation refuse another with 422 `WEBHOOK_EVENT_PATTERN_UNKNOWN`). Migration 0088 converted stored dotted patterns with a mapping table, recorded each old list in `webhook_event_pattern_repairs` and deactivated a subscription left without a pattern or addressed to an example.com / .org / .net host; no webhook subscription is seeded. A rule's trigger event follows the same list (v9.0.406, TD-726): the rule editor offers only these types and «همه رویدادها»; `assertRuleEventTypeAllowed` (`src/services/events/ruleActionTypeGuard.ts`) refuses another type on create, on a switch, on activation and when an active rule is saved (422 `RULE_EVENT_TYPE_UNKNOWN`; an inactive legacy rule stays editable), the draft evaluation reads the same list, and stored rules of such types are only listed by the financial health check (`event_rule_unpublished_event_type`).
- **Webhook Secrets Masked in Every Answer (v9.0.360, TD-710, product-owner decision ت۶):** every answer that carries a webhook subscription or an action rule goes through `webhookSubscriptionView` / `actionRuleView` (`src/services/events/integrationSecrets.ts`): the signing key, custom header values, rule `secretToken` and rule header values are «********» for every user, the system admin included; the key is shown once, in the create answer and `POST /webhooks/:id/rotate-secret` (`events.manage`, audited). On save «********» keeps the stored value only for the stored address (`resolveMaskedHeaders` / `resolveRuleConfigSecrets`; another address is 422 `INTEGRATION_SECRET_REENTRY_REQUIRED`, a mask with nothing stored 422 `INTEGRATION_SECRET_MASKED`), so a masked secret is never sent to an address the editor chose. Secret fields are `type="password"`.
- **A Failed Webhook Action Is Failed (v9.0.375, TD-706, product-owner decision ت۴):** a rule's webhook action whose request fails (connection refused, DNS, timeout) is recorded as `failed` with the error, whatever its address; there is no simulated success, and the echo simulator is reached only through a real request (TD-704).
- **No Demo Action Handlers (v9.0.376, TD-714, product-owner decision ت۴):** boot (`registerDomainEventHandlers`) registers no `ActionHandlerService` handler; the framework (idempotency key per handler and event, «اکشن_هندلر:» audit row) stays for a handler that does real work, which a domain registers itself. A handler never writes an audit row for work another path did (the invoice voucher comes from `VoucherSync` in the document's transaction).
- **Test Tools Have No Effect (v9.0.430, TD-708, product-owner decision ت۵):** a rule test (`POST /events/action-rules/:id/test`, `testStoredRule`) and a draft evaluation evaluate the conditions on a sample event and only describe what the action would do (`previewRuleAction` in `src/services/events/ruleDraftEvaluation.ts`); the event simulation (`simulateDomainEvent` in `src/services/events/eventSimulation.ts`, contract `src/lib/events/eventSimulationContract.ts`) takes only a published event type (else 422 `EVENT_SIMULATION_TYPE_UNKNOWN`), publishes nothing and lists the matching rules and receiving webhook subscriptions; the timeline replay is a dry run only (live replay 422 `EVENT_REPLAY_LIVE_REMOVED`). None of them sends, notifies or writes an audit row; the only real send is a subscription's connection test with the `system.ping` body.
- **Event Timeline of One Entity (v9.0.431, TD-711):** the timeline (`getAggregateTimeline` in `src/services/events/eventSourcingReplayService.ts`) reads an entity's outbox and dead-letter rows by its event aggregate types (`lower(aggregate_type)`) and exact id, and its audit rows only by the section's own audit entity names and exact id (`TIMELINE_AGGREGATE_SCOPES` in `src/services/events/timelineAggregates.ts`), never a description containing the id; audit rows go only to holders of `audit_logs.view` (`auditIncluded` in the answer), and the picker gives the id the events carry, never a document number.
- **Dead Letter Edit and Outbox Retry (v9.0.432, TD-716):** a dead-letter payload edit (`DeadLetterQueueService.editPayload`) runs under that row's lock in one transaction with an audit row (`tx`): a replayed row is never edited (409 `DLQ_EVENT_REPLAYED`), a non-object payload is 422 `DLQ_PAYLOAD_INVALID`, and the outbox row is rewritten only while it has not completed. «تلاش دوباره» of failed outbox events (`retryFailedOutboxEvents` in `src/services/events/outboxRetry.ts`) locks the failed outbox rows and their unresolved dead-letter rows, sets the events pending and marks their idle dead-letter rows replayed in the same transaction; one event that is not failed is 409 `OUTBOX_EVENT_NOT_FAILED` and one being replayed 409 `DLQ_REPLAY_IN_PROGRESS`.
- **Rule Switch Target State (v9.0.438, TD-729):** an automatic rule is switched on or off only with its target state (`POST /events/action-rules/:id/toggle` with `{ active }`, else 400): `setRuleActive` (`src/services/events/ruleActiveState.ts`) writes it under the rule row lock, a repeat changes nothing (`changed: false`), activation passes the same action and event type checks, and each change writes one audit row with before / after in its transaction; the rules tab disables a rule's switch and test button until their answer.

## 16. Frontend Communication & Polling Optimization
- **Standardized `fetchJson`:** Use `fetchJson` from `/src/api.ts` with `credentials: 'include'`.
- **Guarded Background Polling:** Intervals (`setInterval`) must check active sub-tab state and clean up on unmount.
- **Form Drafts (v9.0.305, TD-677):** A draft's type and key match `DRAFT_ENTITY_TYPE_PATTERN` / `DRAFT_KEY_PATTERN` (`src/lib/drafts/draftRules.ts`, else 400) and the drafts routes leave every other error to `errorHandler`, so no SQL text reaches the browser. A draft lives 1 to 90 days (`DRAFT_EXPIRY_DAYS`), an expired draft is never returned (a legacy row without `expires_at` lives 30 days from its last save) and `FormDraftService.startCleanup` soft-deletes expired drafts daily under advisory lock 91031 (v9.0.306, TD-676, product-owner decision ت۶ of package 16).
- **Installable App (v10.0.16, D-11, owner decision ت۱۲ ب):** the app installs on a phone through `vite-plugin-pwa` with the options in `src/lib/pwa/pwaConfig.ts` (manifest, `registerType: 'prompt'`). The service worker precaches only the app shell (`PWA_PRECACHE_PATTERNS`) and keeps at run time only hashed build files under `/assets` and `/fonts` (`PWA_STATIC_ASSET_URL`); it never caches an `/api` answer, an attachment or an uploaded image, and never answers `/api`, `/uploads`, `/health` or `/metrics` with the app page (`PWA_NETWORK_ONLY_PATHS`). A new version replaces the open one only from the «نسخه تازه برنامه آماده است» banner (`PwaStatusBanners`); offline the app opens with the «اتصال قطع است» banner and queues nothing (Vitest `pwaInstallableApp.test.tsx`). Narrower than 768 px a bottom bar replaces the side menu (`MobileBottomNav`, items in `mobileNavItems.ts`, each shown by `canOpenPage`); the collapsed side menu takes no width there and the open one covers the page and closes on navigation (v10.0.17, Vitest `mobileShellNav.test.tsx`). A window opened on a phone takes the whole screen and its buttons are at least 44 px high (`PHONE_SHEET_OVERLAY` / `PHONE_SHEET_PANEL` / `PHONE_TAP_TARGET` in `src/lib/pwa/phoneLayout.ts`), and a button that records something is disabled while offline with `OFFLINE_SUBMIT_TITLE` (`useOnlineStatus`): the approvals task window (v10.0.18, Vitest `phoneApprovalInbox.test.tsx`) and the stock count sheet. A page with its own phone layout builds only one of its two layouts (`usePhoneWidth` in `src/lib/pwa/usePhoneWidth.ts`, the md breakpoint), so each input is on the page once: the stock count sheet shows one card per item on a phone (`AuditSheetCardList` in `src/components/inventory/AuditSheetCards.tsx`); its count field is text with the decimal keypad (`inputMode="decimal"`, never `type="number"`, which drops Persian digits), a count is read through `normalizeDecimalString`, and a count that is not a non-negative number is shown under its field and blocks the save (`auditCountError` / `invalidAuditCounts` in `src/lib/inventoryAudit/auditSheet.ts`), never counted as zero (v10.0.19, Vitest `phoneStockCount.test.tsx`). The piecework entry window follows the same sheet rule with a scrolling body between a fixed header and footer, one-column edit fields and the decimal keypad for a quantity (the text keyboard for hours, which take «:») (v10.0.20, Vitest `phonePieceworkLog.test.tsx`).
- **Workshop Cartable Data (v10.1.37, N-01 part 1, TD-1283, mobile workshop plan decisions ت۳ / ت۴ / ت۱۱ الف):** three catalog keys, `workshop.self` (a worker's own cartable), `workshop.review` (requires `projects.view`; a project manager reviews end-of-task reports) and `workshop.long_session` (requires `workshop.self`), in `src/lib/permissions/workshopPermissions.ts`, and the role template «همکار کارگاه» (`workshop_worker`; named «کارکن کارگاه» before v10.1.69, and a role already made keeps its stored name). A login, its token and its cookie last seven days for a role whose stored permissions hold `workshop.long_session` and no key outside `LONG_SESSION_ROLE_KEYS` (`workshop.self`, `workshop.long_session`), else 24 hours, the system admin included (`sessionLifetimeSeconds` in `src/lib/auth/sessionLifetime.ts`, `sessionLifetimeOfRole` in `src/middleware/auth.ts`); a permission change of a role that holds or held the key ends its users' sessions (`updateRole`, token version). «مسئولان پروژه» (`project_managers`, migration 0119) are saved by `POST` / `PUT /projects` (`managerUserIds`, missing = unchanged on edit) in the project's transaction after its row, audited with `tx`, and returned as `managers` with the project record; a missing, deleted or repeated user among the newly added ones is 422 `PROJECT_MANAGER_INVALID` (a manager deleted later never blocks the save) (`setProjectManagers` in `src/services/projects/projectManagers.ts`). A remittance (`documents.recipient_personnel_id`) and a material allocation (`project_bom_allocations.recipient_personnel_id`) keep their recipient personnel (`recipientPersonnelId`): only a remittance takes one (422 `RECIPIENT_REMITTANCE_ONLY`), and it is a live personnel who has not left, read `FOR SHARE` (else 422 `RECIPIENT_PERSONNEL_INVALID`, before any movement; rule in `src/lib/workshop/recipient.ts`), and the stock page sends a terminated counterparty only as the buyer name (`isRecipientCandidate`). Migration 0119 also creates `stock_receipt_confirmations` (one answer per remittance or allocation) and `workshop_task_reports` (one live report per schedule row, `uq_workshop_task_reports_live_row`) and the reminder dedup index `uq_notifications_workshop_reminder`, which the cartable routes of part 2 use.
- **Workshop Cartable Server (v10.1.39, N-01 part 2, TD-1284, mobile workshop plan decisions ت۱ الف / ت۲ الف / ت۵–ت۸ الف):** `src/routes/workshop.routes.ts` with services in `src/services/workshop/`. A worker (`workshop.self`, acting only as the live personnel linked to the user, else 422 `WORKSHOP_PERSONNEL_NOT_LINKED`) reads their open schedule rows of open projects with the deadline (`GET /workshop/my-tasks`, never an amount before approval, ت۷) and their final remittances and allocations (`GET /workshop/my-deliveries`), answers each delivery once (`POST /workshop/deliveries/confirm`: «تحویل گرفتم» or «مغایرت دارد» with a required note, notifying the recorder and `warehouse.out` holders; stock never changes, ت۱) and reports a row's final quantity (`POST /workshop/reports`, at most the row's quantity, one live report per row, edit and withdraw only while submitted, OCC by `version`; ت۵). Approve and reject (`workshop.review`) need the user on the project's managers, else 403 `WORKSHOP_NOT_PROJECT_MANAGER`, and never a report of their own work (its personnel linked to them or recorded by them, 403 `WORKSHOP_OWN_REPORT`; the system admin is exempt); under the project then report row locks the approval writes the work log through `PieceworkService.logWorkEntries` with the schedule row link and the server rate, the quantity correctable (ت۲), notifies the worker with the amount and suggests the progress matrix tick when every row of that product and stage has a live log (ت۶); a rejection needs a reason. Every write has its audit row with `tx`. The bell reminds a worker once the day before a deadline and once after it (`generateWorkshopDueReminders`, `uq_notifications_workshop_reminder`, ت۸), scanning at most once an hour per user outside any transaction (`takeWorkshopReminderScan` in `src/lib/workshop/reminderThrottle.ts`). A row without a planned quantity above zero takes no report, edit or approval (422 `WORKSHOP_ROW_QUANTITY_MISSING`, `reportQuantityError`); edit and approve re-read the row under the project lock, a row gone or reassigned is 422 `WORKSHOP_ROW_NOT_FOUND` and a row whose task changed since the report 409 `WORKSHOP_ROW_TASK_CHANGED`; live work logs of the same personnel, task and project that no schedule row links come with each review item (`manualLogs`, `manualWorkLogs`) and approval refuses them with 409 `WORKSHOP_MANUAL_LOGS_EXIST` unless acknowledged («بررسی کردم», since v10.1.40 the ids the reviewer saw, below); deleting an approved report's work log or moving it to another project turns the report «ردشده» with a note in that transaction (`revertWorkshopReportsOfLog` in `src/services/piecework/workshopReportRevert.ts`), and the stage progress counts each schedule row's live work log, an approved report's or one the project recorded from the schedule (v10.2.11, TD-1340).
- **Workshop Cartable Pages (v10.1.40, N-01 part 3, TD-1285, mobile workshop plan decisions ت۱ / ت۲ / ت۶ / ت۷ / ت۱۰ الف):** one page, `src/pages/WorkshopPage.tsx`, with tabs (`src/lib/workshop/workshopView.ts`): `/workshop` (`workshop.self`) shows «کارهای من» (deadline colour, a reminder's `?row=` card first, the end-of-task report sheet with the quantity checked by the server's `reportQuantityError`, edit and withdraw while waiting, and the approved reports with their amount) and «حواله‌های من» (`?tab=deliveries`; «مغایرت دارد» needs a note); `/workshop/review` (`workshop.review`) lists the waiting reports with approve (corrected quantity, work date; the manual work logs of the same task listed and «بررسی کردم» required before approving) and reject (reason), and after the last row offers the progress matrix tick only to a holder of `projects.edit`, never ticking for them. Every window is `WorkshopSheet` (`src/components/workshop/`): a full-screen phone sheet whose actions are off while offline (`OFFLINE_SUBMIT_TITLE`) and whose Esc and close do nothing while it saves. Both pages have `PAGE_ACCESS` rows, menu items and one phone bar button «کارگاه» that opens the review page for a manager without `workshop.self` (`fallbackPath` in `mobileNavItems.ts`); the project's schedule tab shows each stage's approved share and the waiting reports (`ProjectWorkshopPanel`, `GET /workshop/projects/:projectId/reports`). Reads and writes go through `src/hooks/queries/useWorkshopQueries.ts`; nothing is cached for offline use. The approval sends the manual work logs it showed as `acknowledgedManualLogIds`, and the server refuses with 409 `WORKSHOP_MANUAL_LOGS_EXIST` when the live set differs (`sameIdSet` in `src/services/workshop/workshopReports.ts`); a report turned «ردشده» because its work log was deleted or moved notifies its worker (`workshop_report_reverted`), and the bell's reminder scan starts its hour only after a scan that succeeded (`runThrottledReminderScan` in `src/lib/workshop/reminderThrottle.ts`).
- **Group Dashboard (v10.2.8, 10.2 package 9, TD-1300):** every signed-in user's dashboard shows colleagues' birthdays today and the next seven days (`GET /social/birthdays`: live personnel who have not left with `personnel.is_birthday_visible = 1`, day and month only, never the year or age; 30 Esfand falls on 29 Esfand in a year without it, `upcomingBirthday` in `src/lib/social/birthdays.ts`), the owner's own greeting even when hidden, and on today's birthday one reaction and short message (200 letters) per sender in `birthday_greetings` (migration 0130; 200 letters counted as the database does, code points after trimming, `greetingMessageLength`; never one's own, 422 `SOCIAL_GREETING_SELF`; a birthday that is not today is 404 like a missing one; edited and soft-deleted only by its sender with a «تبریک تولد» audit row in its `tx`). The colleague-birthday and greeting notifications are the `social` category, which `BELL_ONLY_CATEGORIES` keeps out of every messenger, written once through `createNotifications` (`uq_notifications_social`); the bell scans birthdays at most once an hour per user and again at the start of each business day (`scanRunsOfDay`). A birthday turned off, of a personnel who left or who was deleted takes its birthday and greeting notifications out of every bell in that write's transaction, the rows kept with `dismissed_at` (`withdrawBirthdayNotifications` in `src/services/social/birthdayNotificationWithdraw.ts`, called by `updatePersonnelAtVersion`, `softDeletePersonnel` and the preferences route); a birth date edited off today takes that year's rows out the same way (`withdrawBirthdayNotificationsIfGone`), the bell scan re-reads its candidates `FOR SHARE` in its insert transaction, and turning a birthday back on the same day does not bring withdrawn rows back, by design. Presence (`GET /social/presence`) is the in-memory time of the user's last request (`recordPresence` in `authenticateToken`; the browser's background polls carry `X-ERP-Background: 1` and do not count, `BACKGROUND_POLL_HEADERS`, the inbox and event monitor only on a repeat read (`repeatPollHeaders`); a user without a full name is «همکار», never the login name): «برخط است» within five minutes, else «امروز آمده» on the business day, never a page; `users.is_presence_visible` and the birthday flag are the user's own switches (`PUT /social/preferences`, audited). «تازه‌های این نسخه» is the current version's changelog entry until the user dismisses it (`users.whats_new_seen_version`). No amount appears and nothing leaves the app.

## 17. System Testing, E2E Audit & Test Runs
- **Vitest for New Unit & Frontend Tests (v7.0.76, audit P3-6, product-owner decision):** New pure-unit and React component tests are Vitest files in `src/tests/vitest/*.test.ts(x)` (jsdom + Testing Library), run with `npm run test:vitest` (CI lint job). Vitest runs in the `vmForks` pool (one jsdom per worker, each file in its own context) with 30 s test and hook timeouts, and `findBy*` / `waitFor` wait 5 s by default (`src/tests/vitest/setup/testingLibrary.ts`); a test file never passes a timeout below 10 s, and a ratchet test scans the repository once per file (v10.1.2, TD-1261; Vitest `vitestCiStabilityTd1261.test.ts`). Database and domain suites stay on the existing runner. The test bootstrap waits for the seed lock (`runSeedWithLock({ waitForLock: true })`), because the lock is one key for every schema of the database, and a catalog read in code or tests names its schema (`current_schema()`, a `regclass` / `regnamespace` cast or a schema column; Vitest `catalogSchemaScope.test.ts`) (v10.1.12, TD-1266).
- **Playwright Critical Path (v7.0.77, audit P3-7):** `e2e/critical-path.spec.ts` drives login → final sales invoice → its journal voucher against the production bundle (`dist/server.cjs`) on an empty database (`E2E_DATABASE_URL`, `npm run test:e2e`, CI job `e2e`; the web server is awaited on `/health/startup`, not `/health/ready`, because migrations and seed run in the background). UI changes on these pages must keep it green. The four business flows have their own specs in `e2e/flows/` (v10.0.9, roadmap I-02): purchase (stock page receipt and procurement delivery), project production and delivery (material allocation and «ورود به انبار»), payslip and payment, and treasury and cheque (invoice settlement and a received cheque to cleared). The browser runs the step under test, the API (`e2e/support/flowKit.ts`) prepares its data with names unique per run, and the spec reads `E2E_DATABASE_URL` only to check stock, Kardex and the voucher rows; a change to these screens, their routes or their vouchers keeps them green. Every CI job has its own `timeout-minutes` (never GitHub's 360-minute default), the e2e job caches the Playwright browser by the lock file and prints the page snapshot of a failed test in its log, and every tracked shell script passes `shellcheck --severity=error` in the lint job, and `npm run test:coverage` fails below 90 % lines and statements, 88 % functions and 73 % branches (`c8 --check-coverage`; the floors only go up) (v10.0.152, OT-B-05 / OBS-R1-19, product-owner decision; Vitest `ciGatesI06.test.ts`). The CI check jobs (lint, database tests, coverage, e2e, build, Docker build) start at once and none has `needs` on another, so a PR result waits for the longest job, not the sum; only the image push (`docker-publish`) waits for every check job; CI has no staging deploy (the empty staging jobs were removed in v10.1.65, OBS-R1-19, decision ت۴۰), and the database jobs start the runner's own PostgreSQL 16 with `scripts/ci-start-postgres.sh` instead of a service image (v10.0.192, TD-1260, product-owner decision ت۲۰; Vitest `ciParallelJobs.test.ts`).
- **CI Images From the Mirror (v10.0.38, TD-1169):** CI service images and the Dockerfile base images come from the Amazon ECR Public mirror of the Docker official images (`public.ecr.aws/docker/library/<image>:<tag>`), never anonymously from Docker Hub, whose pull limit stops jobs before any test runs (Vitest `ciImageMirror.test.ts`).
- **Modular Test Suites:** Suites isolated under `src/tests/suites/` (`unit`, `database`, `workflow`, `concurrency`, `integration`, `security`, `api`, `regression`, `criticalPath`, `businessLogicAudit`, etc.).
- **Business Invariants & Known-Findings Baseline (v8.0.1, V8 roadmap):** `src/tests/invariants/businessInvariants.ts` holds the executable business invariants (read-only SQL) and `src/tests/simulation/` the seeded one-business-year simulator (`npm run simulate:year`, one isolated schema per seed). The `business_invariants` suite (last in the runner) requires zero violations on clean flows and exactly the finding classes listed in `src/tests/simulation/knownFindings.ts`, each tied to an open `TD-###`. The baseline only shrinks: a release that fixes a finding removes its class there and closes its TD row in the same change; a new class is a regression and is never added without a new TD row. Since v10.0.10 (TD-982, I-01) the catalogue also holds I7 (project reservation releases), I8 (party receivable), I9 (cheque transition chain and vouchers), I11 (closed fiscal year), I12 (unique numbers in their own year), I17 (rial balance with a rate on every foreign row), I18 (rows only on posting accounts), I19 (reservations within stock, `checkGuardedReservations`, outside `checkBusinessInvariants` because a stock count or a void may lower stock below a reservation, TD-819) and I20 (VAT payable = invoice VAT − return VAT); each is scoped by the watermarks of `InvariantScope` and has a check (`invariantCatalogScenarios.ts`) that breaks a row by hand and expects its key. Since v10.0.11 the simulator also runs treasury receipts, payments and voids, received cheques and their transitions, payslips with payments and payment voids, a requisition through order and delivery, material allocation and release and a sales proforma approved through the document workflow (project-side operations move on business today, on items of their own), checks I19 after each step that passes the sellable gate, and `npm run simulate:year -- --close-year` approves the year's drafts and closes 1404 in its own schema (I11). Since v10.0.36 (I-03) `--users=N` runs the steps in rounds of N concurrent operations, a deadlock or a non-business error is a finding (`I0_deadlock` / `I0_unexpected_error`), and the `inv_i03_*` concurrency checks race delivery with «دریافت کالا», allocation with a remittance and two settlements of one invoice.
- **Test Runs (v7.0.64, product-owner decision):**
  - **While working:** verify with `npm run lint` and run ONLY the tests relevant to the change (`npx tsx scripts/run-tests.ts --suite <name> --filter <id-or-keyword>`), plus the same test on the previous code to prove it fails there. A run whose suite is unknown or whose filter matches no test fails (exit 1), and the environment check runs before any schema is written (v9.0.186, TD-608).
  - **Before every push:** run the fast checks (lint and typecheck, every ratchet, `check:version`, the tests that touch the change) and `npm run build`; CI runs the full suite (`npm test` with the environment of the `test` job in `.github/workflows/ci.yml` against PostgreSQL 16) and a pull request merges only when every required check is green (v10.0.89, product-owner decision of 18 Mehr 1405). A new fix still has its failing test first. Locally: `source scripts/ci-test-env.sh && npm test` (the CI test job's values). In Claude Code on the web the session-start hook (`.claude/hooks/session-start.sh`, registered in `.claude/settings.json`, v8.0.45) runs `npm ci` when `node_modules` is missing or older than the lock file, starts PostgreSQL 16, sets the `postgres` password to the CI value and creates `erp_test` / `erp_e2e`.
  - **Otherwise:** a full run happens only when the user explicitly asks for it.
  - **No Polling Loops:** never poll long background jobs repeatedly; wait for the completion notification.

## 18. Financial Reporting & Trial Balance
- **4-Level Trial Balance (`FinancialReportsTab.tsx`):** Displays group, general, subsidiary, and detailed accounts.
- **Supported Formats:** 2-column, 4-column, 6-column, and 8-column balances.
- **General Journal Book (`journal-book`):** Double-entry vouchers sorted by date and number with verified debit/credit balance.
- **Drill-Down:** All balance rows support instant navigation to detailed ledger cards (`ledger`). The card of a group or general account carries all its sub-accounts (`accountSubtreeCondition` in `src/services/accounting/accountSubtree.ts`, v9.0.117, TD-570), so it shows the balance the trial balance row shows. The card has one contract shared by the server and the browser (`src/lib/accounting/accountCard.ts`, v9.0.118, TD-574): the server sends the opening row (`isOpening`) and the view never adds its own.
- **Ledger Reports Need Accounting Keys (v9.0.225, TD-547, product-owner decision ت۵):** `GET /accounting/reports/account-card`, `/ledger`, `/party-ledger` and `/parties` need `accounting.reports` or `accounting.view`; the customer page reads its own card from `GET /customers/:id/account-card` (TD-416). An account card with no account, detailed type, id or name is 422 `ACCOUNT_CARD_FILTER_REQUIRED` and a party statement with no party 422 `PARTY_LEDGER_PARTY_REQUIRED` (`accountCardHasFilter` in `src/lib/accounting/accountCard.ts`, shared with the explorer, which sends no ledger request until a selection is made).
- **Party Statement Rows by Type and Id (v9.0.226, TD-548):** `getDetailedPartyLedger` counts for a customer or supplier the TD-416 rows (`partyDetailedRowsCondition`) and for a personnel the rows of type `personnel` with its id (`personnelDetailedRowsCondition` in `src/services/accounting/partyDetailedRows.ts`), plus legacy rows without an id under the exact current name; never an id of another detailed type or a «contains» name. The trial balance's detailed level keys a row by account, detailed type and id, and by name only for a row without an id (v10.0.125, TD-1124). A party id is read from its own table (a soft-deleted record too); a missing one is 404 `PARTY_LEDGER_PARTY_NOT_FOUND`, and a name no party has matches only rows with exactly that trimmed name. A party's statement, its account card (`party`) and the customer and personnel delete guards count permanent accounts only (`permanentAccountCondition` in `src/services/accounting/partyTemporaryRows.ts`, `TEMPORARY_ACCOUNT_TYPES` in `src/lib/accounting/partyTemporaryRows.ts`); revenue, expense and cost-of-sales rows with the party's detail come apart in `temporaryRows`, shown by `PartyTemporaryRowsPanel` and outside the balance (v10.0.257, TD-915 / TD-924, decision ت۵ الف of the phase 5 review).
- **Journal Book Paged (v9.0.227, TD-561):** `GET /accounting/reports/journal-book` returns one page (`page`, `limit`; default `JOURNAL_BOOK_PAGE_SIZE` 200, at most 1,000) once, never `{ report, ...report }`; row numbers and the running balance come from window functions over the whole range, and the totals, voucher count and row count (`total`) are summed in SQL over the whole range (contract `src/lib/accounting/journalBook.ts`).
- **Accounting Buttons Ask Their API Key (v9.0.228, TD-567):** the accounting dashboard shows the voucher, treasury and cheque buttons only for `accounting.vouchers`, `accounting.treasury` and `accounting.cheques`, the year-closing execute button only for `accounting.fiscal_close` (others see the preview and a note), and the chart-of-accounts seed button only for the system admin (`useIsSystemAdmin` in `src/contexts/AuthContext.tsx`).
- **One Trial Balance Row per Detail Account (v10.0.139, TD-1128):** the level-4 trial balance and its tree group a detail row by account, detailed type and id for customers, suppliers, personnel, projects and bank accounts and show the entity's current name (`trialBalanceDetailKey` / `currentDetailNames` in `src/services/accounting/trialBalanceDetail.ts`), whatever name each voucher stored; a legacy party row without an id keeps its exact name (TD-416), a row without an id on a manual voucher keeps the typed name, and a row without an id of an automatic voucher is keyed by its stable reference, the voucher's cheque (`source_cheque_id`, shown «چک <number>») or the linked item of an item opening row (`item_opening_voucher_items`, shown with its current name and code), else it goes to the account's general row «سایر / عمومی».
- **Party Statement Print (v9.0.229, TD-572):** the party statement answers with its own `startDate` / `endDate` (null for an open end) next to its `currency`; the print header and the final balance read them from the response, a party, range or currency change reloads it, and «ماه جاری» / «سال جاری» start on the first day of today's Jalali month and year.
- **Report Digits, Currency Names and Today (v9.0.288, TD-580):** accounting reports show voucher numbers and counts in Persian digits and name currencies with `formatCurrencyLabel` (pickers list `TREASURY_CURRENCIES`), never a raw ISO code; the party statement's issue date and the party statement and account explorer export file names take today from the business time zone (`getTodayIsoDate` / `getTodayJalaliDate`, `partyStatementFileName` / `accountExplorerFileName` in `src/lib/accounting/accountingExport.ts`), never `new Date().toISOString()` or `toLocaleDateString` (Vitest `accountingReportDigits.test.tsx`).
- **Voucher List Paged on the Server (v9.0.115, TD-565):** The «اسناد حسابداری» tab reads one page from `GET /accounting/vouchers` with its page, search, `voucherType`, status and ISO dates (`useVoucherPageQuery`, contract `src/lib/accounting/voucherList.ts`); the response carries `total` and `statusCounts` (every status under the other filters), which the status tabs show. Never filter or count vouchers in the browser.
- **Statement Dates in Their Own Header (v9.0.116, TD-566):** The balance sheet and the financial ratios take an as-of date and the income statement its own period, each in its own header (`ReportDateFields.tsx`, ISO; empty = today / from the first entry); the income statement never reads the trial balance dates, and each statement shows its date or period in Jalali.
- **One Financial Statement Contract (v9.0.114, TD-563):** The income statement and the balance sheet have one contract shared by the server and the browser (`src/lib/accounting/financialStatements.ts`): `getIncomeStatement` / `getBalanceSheet` return it and `IncomeStatementView` / `BalanceSheetView` read only its keys (revenues, cost of sales, operating expenses; current and non-current assets, current liabilities, equity and the period profit); the net margin is shown in Persian digits, and none without revenue.
