- **Party Account Card by Id (v9.0.4, TD-416, product-owner decision):** A party's account card and balance (the customers page «تراز مالی», the customer dossier) come only from `GET /customers/:id/account-card` (`getCustomerAccountCard`): voucher rows of detailed type `customer` or `supplier` with the party's `detailed_id`, plus legacy rows without an id whose name equals the current name exactly (`partyDetailedRowsCondition` in `src/services/accounting/partyDetailedRows.ts`); never a «contains» name match. The generic account card's `detailedName` stays a search filter. The customer dossier's documents come from `GET /customers/:id/documents` (v9.0.6, TD-417): only sales documents (`invoice`, `proforma`, `return`) of the party, never a text search. A sales or purchase document (`invoice`, `proforma`, `return`, `receipt`, `purchase`) keeps its party in `documents.party_id` (FK to `customers`, migration 0080) and `buyer_name` is only its display name (v9.0.336, TD-778, product-owner decision ت۶ of package 8): `POST` / `PUT /documents` take `partyId` (the invoice form and the stock page send the picker's id, WooCommerce and procurement orders the matched customer or supplier), resolved by `resolveDocumentParty` in `src/services/documents/documentParty.ts` (a missing or deleted party 422 `DOCUMENT_PARTY_INVALID`, another type 422 `DOCUMENT_PARTY_NOT_ALLOWED`, a party of the wrong kind — a purchase document takes a supplier or «both», a sales document a customer or «both» (`partyFitsDocument` in `src/lib/documents/documentPartyKind.ts`, also filtering the stock page and invoice pickers) — 422 `DOCUMENT_PARTY_KIND_MISMATCH` (v10.0.44, TD-939), no id → the single live party of exactly that trimmed name, else none); a return with an original invoice takes the invoice's party (another one 422 `RETURN_PARTY_MISMATCH`). A document without a party id whose name is held only by a party of the other kind is refused with the same 422 `DOCUMENT_PARTY_KIND_MISMATCH` on create, edit and finalize, so a procurement order or a direct receipt never names a customer as supplier (`assertNameNotOtherKind`; the purchase forms list only suppliers and «both», `supplierNameOptions`; v10.0.98, TD-1194). The sales, purchase and return vouchers (`documentVoucherPartyId`), the dossier and the party delete guard (`documentPartyCondition`) read the id, and a legacy document without one keeps the exact-name rule; migration 0080 linked old documents only to the single live party of exactly their name, and the financial health check lists the rest (`document_party_unlinked`), never rewritten. Sales lead values are summed only per currency (v9.0.11, TD-422, product-owner decision): `GET /crm/stats` returns `pipelineByCurrency`, `wonByCurrency` and `stageCounts[stage].byCurrency` (`getCrmStats` in `src/services/crm/crmStats.ts`; an empty currency is IRR), the customer dossier uses `sumByCurrency` (`src/lib/crm/leadCurrencyTotals.ts`), and no total adds amounts of different currencies. Follow-ups come only from `GET /crm/followups` (v9.0.14, TD-428, product-owner decision): no activity-date range, ordered by due date, paginated, with `dueCount` / `openCount`; the follow-ups tab, the dashboard widget, the tab badge and the stats counter use the conditions in `src/services/crm/crmFollowups.ts`, never the date-filtered activity list. The CRM customer filter sends `customerId` (v9.0.15, TD-429): `leadCustomerCondition` (`src/services/crm/crmLeadCustomerFilter.ts`) returns the party's leads plus legacy leads without an id whose trimmed customer or company name equals the party's current name. A sales lead is deleted only under its row lock (v9.0.16, TD-425, `deleteLead` in `src/services/crm/crmLeadDelete.ts`): a missing or deleted lead is 404, a lead with an active document linked by `crm_lead_id` or `proforma_id` is refused with 409, and activities of a deleted or missing lead are left out of the stats, the activity and follow-up lists and the due reminder (`liveLeadActivityCondition`). A CRM activity is saved only when its lead and party exist and are not deleted (v9.0.17, TD-426, `resolveActivityParents` in `src/services/crm/crmActivityParents.ts`, else 422); these columns have no database foreign key. A sales lead's `estimatedValue` and `probability` go through `decimalInput` (v9.0.18, TD-427): the amount is non-negative, the probability an integer from 0 to 100, and `stage`, `status` and `currency` come only from the lists in `src/lib/crm/leadFields.ts` (an empty currency or «ریال» is IRR). A follow-up is completed or reopened only through `PUT /crm/activities/:id/complete-followup` / `reopen-followup` (v9.0.19, TD-430, `setFollowupCompleted` in `src/services/crm/crmFollowupStatus.ts`): a target state under the activity row lock, a repeat changes nothing, and each change writes an audit row in the same transaction; never a two-way toggle. An activity is edited (`PUT /crm/activities/:id`, strict body with its `version`, 409 `OCC_CONFLICT` when stale; its lead and party never change) and soft-deleted (`DELETE /crm/activities/:id`) only with `crm.manage`, under its row lock with an audit row written with `tx`, and an edit notifies only newly added mentions (`updateActivity` / `softDeleteActivity` in `src/services/crm/crmActivityEdit.ts`). A lead entering «lost» needs a reason from `CRM_LOST_REASONS` and a note for «other» (`lostReasonError` in `src/lib/crm/leadFields.ts`, shared with the form and the stage dialog; else 422 `CRM_LEAD_LOST_REASON_REQUIRED`, `resolveLeadLostReason`); a legacy lost lead stays editable without one. Every stage change of a lead (create, edit, proforma link and release, conversion) writes a `crm_lead_stage_history` row in its transaction (`recordLeadStageChange`, migration 0113), and `GET /crm/reports/funnel` (`crm.view`, contract `src/lib/crm/funnelReport.ts`) reads only that history: entered, advanced, conversion and average days per stage and the lost reasons, by business-day range and seller; changes before v10.1.18 were rebuilt once from each lead's activity rows (migration 0125, `erp_backfill_crm_lead_stage_history`, `crm_lead_stage_history.source` `backfill` / `backfill_estimated`; v10.1.70, TD-1287) and the report counts the estimated entries (`estimatedEntries`). `GET /crm/followups` answers the user's own follow-ups (`ownFollowupCondition`: the linked personnel, else the legacy exact name) unless a `crm.manage` holder asks `scope=all` or a seller (v10.1.18, N-02 part 2, decisions ت۱–ت۳، ت۶، ت۷ of `CRM_PLAN.md`). The sales pipeline board has fixed-width columns (`PIPELINE_COLUMN_WIDTH_PX` in `src/lib/crm/pipelineBoard.ts`) in a horizontally scrolling row, never a grid narrower than its columns; a card is dragged to another stage through the same stage change as its picker (`stageAfterDrop`), so «won» still needs a live proforma and «lost» a reason, it shows the lead's nearest open follow-up (`nextFollowUpDate` of the lead list, `nextFollowupDatesByLead`), and a phone shows one stage per tab (v10.1.24, TD-1272, decisions ت۹ / ت۱۰; browser test `e2e/crm-pipeline.spec.ts`). A party carries tags (`customer_tags` / `customer_tag_links`, migration 0115, decision ت۱۳): a tag is created, renamed with its `version` (409 `OCC_CONFLICT`) and soft-deleted only with `customers.manage` (`/customers/tags` in `src/routes/customerTags.routes.ts`, `src/services/customers/customerTags.service.ts`), its live name unique by `lower(btrim(name))` (409 `CUSTOMER_TAG_NAME_TAKEN`, index `uq_customer_tags_name_active`), each change with its «برچسب مشتری» audit row written with `tx`; `POST` / `PUT /customers` save `tagIds` in the party's own transaction after its row lock (`setCustomerTags`, a missing or deleted tag 422 `CUSTOMER_TAG_INVALID`, a missing field keeps the tags) with the tags in the audit row, and `GET /customers` and `GET /crm/leads` filter by `tagId` (`customerHasTagCondition` / `leadCustomerHasTagCondition`) (v10.1.29). The sales page opens what `/crm?leadId=` and `/crm?activityId=` name (notifications, the dashboard) and then clears the address (`useCrmDeepLink`, rules in `src/lib/crm/crmDeepLink.ts`: an activity opens its sales file, else its party's dossier, else the activity book); a dossier opens only for a real party (`resolveDossierParty`: the file's party id, else the single party of exactly its name), never a made-up party, the customers page has the sales file window too and opens a file on the sales page, and «ویرایش» from a dossier reads the full record by id (`GET /customers/:id`, `customers.view`, 404 for a missing or deleted party) and clears the request from the address. The sales file table, the activity book and a dossier's activities page on the server (`GET /crm/leads?page=`, `GET /crm/activities?page=&limit=&partyId=&search=&type=`, `useCrmListPages`); the board reads the newest `CRM_BOARD_LEAD_CAP` (500) files and says so at the cap; the search waits for a pause, the pick lists load once, and a failed load shows an error with «تلاش دوباره» (v10.2.5, OBS-R2-24 / OBS-R2-28 / OBS-R2-31).
- **Management Reports (v10.1.8, D-03 part 1, product-owner decisions ت۲ to ت۶ of `REPORTS_PLAN.md`):** The «گزارش‌های مدیریتی» page (`/management-reports/:tab`; tabs in `src/lib/reports/managementReportTabs.ts`, each with its own `PAGE_ACCESS` row) holds the sales report, `GET /reports/sales` with `reports.sales` (contract `src/lib/reports/salesReport.ts`, service `src/services/reports/salesReport.service.ts`): final, non-voided sales documents (`invoice`, `proforma`) at their own date less final returns at the return's date, summed and paged in SQL, by item, customer, category, Jalali period, project or channel and a second dimension. The «all currencies» view converts each document at its own exchange rate and leaves out (and counts) a foreign document without one; a single-currency view shows only that currency's documents. Cost is the Kardex cost of the document's own rows (`out` for a sale, `in` for a return), and cost, gross profit and margin go only to item cost readers (`ITEM_COST_READ_PERMISSIONS`); quantity only with the item in a dimension or filter; VAT and service charge only without an item or category filter. The ledger line (`salesLedgerCheck`: the sales voucher's revenue and discount mapping and 5101, TD-260 amounts, drafts apart) goes only to `accounting.reports` / `accounting.view` holders and only without a party, item, category, project or channel filter. Jalali periods, range shortcuts and comparison ranges come from `src/lib/reports/reportPeriods.ts`, the Excel and print helpers from `reportExport.ts` / `reportWorkbook.ts` (file «گزارش-فروش-<period>.xlsx», right-to-left) and `ReportActions` / `ReportPrintHeader`, shared by the later tabs. The receivables and payables aging tab (v10.1.10, D-03 part 2, decisions ت۷ to ت۱۱) is `GET /reports/aging` with `accounting.reports` (`AGING_REPORT_PERMISSION`; contract `src/lib/reports/agingReport.ts`, SQL `src/services/reports/agingReportQuery.ts`): each party's balance as of the report date on the mapped trade receivables or payables account and its sub-accounts, from the live rows of live vouchers of any status except a closing run's vouchers (`source_fiscal_year`), the party found by the TD-416 rule and amounts by the TD-260 rule; credits settle the oldest debits first (FIFO in SQL) and what is left is aged by its voucher date into 0–30, 31–60, 61–90, 91–180 and over 180 days, the other side shown apart as an advance; drafts count unless «فقط سندهای تأییدشده» and their share is shown apart; in a rial view the party's pending and (receivables only) bounced cheques and its last receipt or payment (a completed, unreversed treasury row or a cheque that did not bounce or come back) are separate columns. Each party links to its statement (`/accounting/reports?subTab=party_ledger&partyType=…&partyId=…`), which opens on that party (`initialPartyId`). The VAT tab (v10.1.14, D-03 part 3, decisions ت۱۲ / ت۱۳) is `GET /reports/vat` with `accounting.reports` (`VAT_REPORT_PERMISSION`; contract `src/lib/reports/vatReport.ts`, SQL `src/services/reports/vatReportQuery.ts`), this Jalali quarter by default: part 1 sums the final, non-voided sales invoices (and legacy final proformas) and sales returns of the range at their own date by VAT percent (a VAT without a percent is «نامشخص»), the net of lines by the document VAT rule, a foreign document at its own rate rounded to the rial per document and one without a rate left out and counted, return VAT reducing; it flags a VAT that is not its percent of the net, a VAT without a percent and a foreign document without a rate; an invoice without VAT is shown in the «بی مالیات» row and never flagged, since VAT is optional per document (v10.1.19, TD-1269). Part 2 reads the mapped sales VAT account (`getSalesVatPayableAccount`, default 3203) and its sub-accounts like the aging tab (drafts counted and shown apart, closing runs left out): opening, credit and debit of the range by source (sales document, treasury and cheque, other; a reversal voucher takes its original's source and document, the `voucherSourceKinds` chain) and closing; the difference of the ledger's net movement with part 1 is listed per document or voucher, largest first (`vatDifferencesSql`). Purchase VAT is not recorded (ت۱۳) and the report says so; it is an aid to the accountant, never the return form. Part 4 (v10.1.17, decisions ت۱۴ to ت۱۶): the sales tab draws the net-after-returns trend by Jalali month (season or year past 24 months) and the category shares (eight and «سایر دسته‌ها») from `GET /reports/sales/charts` (`reports.sales`, the sales report's filters and counting rule; `salesChartSeries` in `src/services/reports/salesCharts.service.ts`, contract `src/lib/reports/reportCharts.ts`), and the aging tab its five buckets from its own totals; the charts are Chart.js, imported only by `ReportBarChart` through `React.lazy` in `ReportChartCard` (`src/components/reports/charts`), so the library is the separate `vendor-charts` chunk loaded only when a chart is drawn: one series, one colour, no legend, right to left, the report's table as its table view. The dashboard «فروش و مطالبات» widget (`SalesSummaryWidget`, plain SVG trend) reads `GET /reports/dashboard-summary` (`DASHBOARD_SALES_WIDGET_PERMISSIONS`: either key opens it, and each part comes only to its own key: month-to-date sales against the same days of last month with six months of trend for `reports.sales`, the aged receivables and their part over 90 days for `accounting.reports`). The purchases tab is `GET /reports/purchases` with the new key `reports.purchases` (`PURCHASE_REPORT_PERMISSION`, granted to no role; contract `src/lib/reports/purchaseReport.ts`, SQL `src/services/reports/purchaseReportQuery.ts`): final, non-voided receipts and purchases at their own date (no draft or purchase proforma), the supplier by `party_id` else the single live party of the exact name, by supplier, item, category or Jalali period and a second dimension, with quantity and average price when the item is in a dimension or filter, the currency rule of the sales report, comparison, the ten largest suppliers and items, Excel «گزارش-خرید-<period>.xlsx» and print.

## 19. Concurrency, OCC & Database Stability Rules
- **PostgreSQL Atomic Sequences (DB-001, DB-003, DB-011):** Sequence IDs generated via `nextval('...')` or the `document_ref_counters` / `item_code_counters` counter tables.
- **Idempotency OCC Locks (DB-010):** `IdempotencyService` uses `.onConflictDoNothing()` and OCC (`status` and `locked_until` in UPDATE WHERE clause).
- **Idempotency Key Contract (v8.0.79, TD-329):** The `idempotency` middleware stores only 2xx responses and releases the key on any other status (`releaseKey`), both before the response is sent; a key recorded for another method, path or body gets 422 `IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_REUSED` and is not executed; the key's lock is extended every third of its window while the request runs (`extendLock`). The browser derives the key from the submission's content (`submissionKeyFor` in `src/lib/submissionKey.ts`), keeps it until a definitive response (network errors, aborts and 502/503/504 keep it), never mutates the caller's options, and waits on 409 `IDEMPOTENCY_IN_FLIGHT` with the same key. After a network error only a GET is resent automatically; a POST / PUT / PATCH / DELETE is sent once and reports `UNCONFIRMED_MUTATION_MESSAGE` (check the result, then resend with the same key) (v9.0.307, TD-670, product-owner decision ت۳ of package 16). When the waits on `IDEMPOTENCY_IN_FLIGHT` run out the key is kept too and the error is `IN_FLIGHT_STILL_RUNNING_MESSAGE` (v9.0.308, TD-679). Every writer route (POST / PUT / PATCH / DELETE) behind the login in the money and stock sections (accounting, documents, inventory, items, raw material requests, piecework, procurement, projects, transfers, warehouses, WooCommerce, workshop) runs `idempotency()`, so a double click or a resend of one submission records once and the second send gets the first answer; only routes that write nothing are exempt, listed with their reason in `RESUBMISSION_EXEMPT_ROUTES` (`src/tests/security/resubmissionPolicy.ts`, the written resubmission rule; the route table carries `idempotent`, test `sec_money_stock_routes_idempotent_obs_r2_14`), and a new writer route there takes the middleware in the same change (v10.1.64, OBS-R2-14, decision ت۴۰).
- **Customer Optimistic Lock (v8.0.122, TD-403):** `PUT /customers/:id` requires the record's `version` (400 without it) and writes with the version in its WHERE (409 `OCC_CONFLICT` when stale or changed meanwhile); the customer Excel export carries «نسخه» and an imported row with an `id` is applied only at the current version, otherwise listed in the import errors. A sales lead never changes an existing customer (v9.0.5, TD-418, product-owner decision): creating, editing or converting a lead only links a customer (id, then exact phone, then exact name) or creates a new one (`linkCustomerForLead` in `src/services/crm/crmCustomerLink.ts`), and a differing phone or company name goes only into the lead's notes. Two active customers never share a name (v9.0.8, TD-420, product-owner decision): the key is `lower(btrim(name))`, checked by `assertCustomerNameAvailable` (`src/services/customers/customerIdentity.ts`) and enforced by the partial unique index `uq_customers_name_active` (migration 0052, created only on data without duplicates; the financial health check lists duplicates as `customer_name_uniqueness`); an index violation is the same Persian duplicate-name error. In the customer Excel import an empty party-type cell leaves an existing party's type unchanged and makes a new party a customer (v9.0.9, TD-421); the cell is read only through `parsePartyTypeCell` (`src/lib/customers/partyTypeCell.ts`, shared by the server and the preview). A party is never deleted while it has open business (v9.0.10, TD-431, product-owner decision): `deleteCustomer` locks the customer row and `assertCustomerDeletable` (`src/services/customers/customerDeleteGuard.ts`) refuses with 409 and Persian reasons a nonzero approved/permanent balance in any currency, draft voucher rows (both by `partyDetailedRowsCondition`), a draft or proforma document of the party (`documentPartyCondition`: its `party_id`, or a legacy document without one whose trimmed buyer name equals the party name), an active lead, a project not completed or cancelled, or a cheque in a status that still has a next step in `CHEQUE_TRANSITIONS`. A party's website, e-mail and Instagram username (`customers.website` / `email` / `instagram`, migration 0112) and each contact person's e-mail are checked and normalized by one rule shared by the server, the form and the Excel preview (`src/lib/customers/contactChannels.ts`: a website gets `https://`, Instagram is stored as the bare username): a wrong value is 422 `CUSTOMER_CONTACT_CHANNEL_INVALID` before any write (`resolveContactChannels` in `src/services/customer.service.ts`) and an import row error, a field not sent keeps its value and an empty import cell leaves it unchanged; the list, dossier and contacts show call and WhatsApp links (`whatsappLink`, the TD-296 phone key) and mail and page links (`ContactLinks.tsx`) (v10.1.15, N-02 part 1).
- **Stock Soft Delete & Reversal (DB-009):** `is_deleted = 1` + reversal transaction + balance recalculation.
- **Negative Stock Guard (DB-006, TD-180):** Inventory deductions below zero are rejected (policy fixed to `forbidden`, enforced in `ItemWarehouseStockService.applyMovement` and by the DB CHECK constraint).
- **No Second Pool Connection Inside a Transaction (v8.0.77, TD-324):** Code running inside a transaction uses that `tx` only — never `orm` / `pool` (with a full pool it waits for the connection timeout). Pass `tx` to `logActivity` (an insert error then propagates instead of turning COMMIT into a silent ROLLBACK), `FiscalPeriodService`, `startInstance` and `maybeStartWorkflow` (both run inside a savepoint of that `tx`; default workflows are seeded only at startup, only when their code is missing, and never overwritten — v9.0.46, TD-453, product-owner decision ت۸). `maybeStartWorkflow` returns null only when the entity type has no active definition; any start error propagates and rejects the caller's operation, never read as «no workflow» (v9.0.36, TD-451). `getDisplayTimezone` serves a stale cache without waiting and is warmed outside transactions (`warmDisplayTimezone`).
- **Stock Item Locks (v8.0.67, TD-320):** Every stock path (document create / finalize / void, sales return, transfer, BOM allocation, project delivery) locks ALL its items at once, in ascending id order, with `FOR NO KEY UPDATE` (`lockStockItems` in `src/services/inventory/stockItemLocks.ts`, or `withOrderedLocks`, which uses the same mode for items) BEFORE the document counter, the document row or any Kardex / line insert; a void locks items → project → document. Never lock an item row `FOR UPDATE` in these paths: inserting a Kardex row or document line takes a `FOR KEY SHARE` foreign-key lock on the item, which conflicts with `FOR UPDATE` and deadlocks concurrent voids. `withOrderedLocks` sorts by `LOCK_ORDER_MAP` in every environment and refuses a table without a lock level (v9.0.189, TD-618).

## 20. Observability, Error Handling & Lifecycle Rules
- **AppError Hierarchy (OBS-003):** Typed subclasses (`ValidationError`, `NotFoundError`, `UnauthorizedError`, `ConflictError`, `DatabaseError`). In production `errorHandler` keeps the `details` of a 4xx answer and hides only 5xx messages and details; body-parser errors get a Persian message and their own code (`BODY_PARSER_ERRORS` in `src/errors/customErrors.ts`, e.g. `INVALID_JSON_BODY` 400; the 413 keeps its TD-641 message) (v9.0.312, TD-594). A database error that no rule names answers the code `DATABASE_ERROR` with no stack in every environment, with «خطای پایگاه‌داده در انجام درخواست رخ داد.» (`DATABASE_ERROR_MESSAGE`, `internal`) outside production and «خطای داخلی سرور» in production like every 5xx; the SQL and its parameters stay in the log files, and the log entry (`database`) and the terminal line carry the database's own reason (SQLSTATE, table, constraint and message, `logDetails`), never the answer (v10.2.10, TD-1320).
- **AsyncHandler & Global Error Handler (OBS-002):** Routes wrapped in `asyncHandler` return uniform error schema with `traceId`. Every async route handler and every async middleware factory (`authorizePermission`, `validate`, `idempotency`) MUST go through `asyncHandler` (v7.0.66, TD-229); Express 5 also passes a rejected handler to the error handler, so `express-async-errors` was removed (v10.1.67). Enforced by unit test `unit_route_async_handler_td_229`. Express 5 keeps the Express 4 request shapes through `src/lib/express5Compat.ts`: Express 4's qs query parser (`arrayLimit` 1000, `parseExpress4QueryString`), `req.body` `{}` when no parser ran, and the mount paths the route table reads (`recordMountPaths`, the app's own `use` only); `validate` sets the parsed query as the request's own property, a catch-all path is `/{*splat}`, the app page is sent relative to its root (`sendFile('index.html', { root })`), and a listen error such as a busy port exits the process with 1 (`listenCallback` in `src/lib/serverListen.ts`) (Vitest `express5RequestShapes.test.ts`).
- **Structured Logging (OBS-001, OBS-006):** Winston logger with daily rotation into `logs/application-%DATE%.log` and `logs/error-%DATE%.log`.
- **Recursive Sanitization (OBS-009):** Sensitive fields scrubbed with `[REDACTED]`.
- **Lifecycle Probes (OBS-007):** Kubernetes probes at `/health/live`, `/health/ready`, `/health/startup`. The system health page (`GET /system/health`, system admin) measures the event queue, voucher balance and workflow each on its own (`collectSubsystemMetrics`, contract `src/lib/system/subsystemHealth.ts`): a failed query is `status: 'unknown'` with null counts and a Persian message, never zeros and `ok`, and the page shows it as «نامعلوم» (v9.0.388, TD-593). Its storage card checks the directories the app writes to, the attachment root (`getAttachmentsRoot`) and the image uploads (`getImageUploadsDir`), each with its path, by permission only: the directory, or its nearest existing parent, must be a writable directory, and the check creates and writes nothing (v9.0.389, TD-619). The event queue buttons replay the failed events or the events stuck in processing only after a confirmation («… دوباره اجرا شوند؟», «اجرای دوباره / انصراف») and are shown only when `outbox.dlqCount` / `outbox.stuckCount` is above zero; the stuck count uses the reset's own condition (`stuckOutboxCondition`; v9.0.391, TD-623). `/health` gives everyone only `version`; its `buildInfo` (commit and build time from `dist/build-info.json`, written by `npm run build`) goes only to the `METRICS_TOKEN` or a live system-admin session (`metricsReaderStatus`; v9.0.168, TD-601).
- **Prometheus Metrics (OBS-008):** System metrics exposed at `/metrics` and `/api/metrics`.
- **Graceful Shutdown (OBS-004, OBS-005):** Handles `SIGTERM`/`SIGINT` with a 10s force-exit timeout.

## 21. Frontend Optimization & Component Architecture
- **Code Splitting (FE-001):** Lazy-load heavy views with `React.lazy` and `Suspense`. Vite vendor chunks isolated.
- **Modular Components (FE-003, FE-004):** Keep components <300 lines; extract subcomponents and hooks.
- **React Query Cache (FE-005):** Use `@tanstack/react-query` with `staleTime: 5 * 60 * 1000` and invalidate on mutations. Saving, editing or voiding any document (invoice, proforma, stock document) invalidates the `documentChange` preset and a settlement the `settlementChange` preset (`INVALIDATION_PRESETS` in `src/lib/queryInvalidation.ts`; v9.0.341, TD-796). The accounting page reads only the lists of the tab on screen and, while the voucher window is open, its lists (`useAccounting({ tab, allowed })`, `ACCOUNTING_TAB_LISTS` in `src/hooks/useAccounting.ts`); a tab the viewer may not open reads none, and «به‌روزرسانی» reads again only the enabled lists (v10.2.17, TD-1322).
- **Auth Security (FE-006):** Centralized in `AuthContext.tsx`. NEVER store user objects or tokens in `localStorage`.
- **DatePicker Protection (FE-010):** Pass dates through `extractDateString()` before assigning to string state.
- **AbortController (FE-008):** Asynchronous fetch loops in `useEffect` must pass abort signal and abort on cleanup.
- **Type Safety (FE-007):** Replace loose `any` types with explicit TypeScript interfaces.
- **Production Console Drop (FE-002):** `esbuild: { drop: ['console', 'debugger'] }` in production Vite build.
- **Architecture Ratchet (v10.0.26, phase-2 decisions ت۲ to ت۵, series 10 roadmap §4):** `npm run ratchet:architecture` (`scripts/ratchets/architectureRatchet.ts`, baseline `scripts/ratchets/architecture-baseline.json`, run by Vitest `architectureRatchet.test.ts`) counts per production file, with the TypeScript parser: `insert` / `update` / `delete` on a Drizzle table in `src/routes/**` (`routeWrites`, TD-960), an optional transaction replaced by the pool (`x || orm`, `x ?? orm`, `= orm`; `ormFallback`), a hand-written error answer `res.status(4xx|5xx)` in a route (`routeErrorResponses`), `throw new Error(` in `src/services/**` (`serviceGenericErrors`) and server imports against the package direction of `scripts/ratchets/PACKAGE_MAP.txt` (`packageBoundary`, `scripts/ratchets/packageBoundary.ts`: transactional domains → workflow → the cores 3–6 → infrastructure 1, 2, 16; the shared kernel and the composition roots `app.ts`, `server.ts`, `workflowDomainActions.ts` are left out). It also counts physical deletes in production code (`hardDeletes`: `x.delete(<table>)` on a schema table or on a table held in a variable, and SQL text with `DELETE FROM` or `TRUNCATE`; the variable and `TRUNCATE` kinds since v10.1.66); the only files that may hold one are listed with their reason in `scripts/ratchets/HARD_DELETES.md` (queue and key housekeeping, the audit purge, the factory reset, role delete, a list's link rows, workflow design rows and pending approvals), which Vitest `hardDeleteExceptions.test.ts` keeps equal to the baseline, and a new physical delete needs its own line there and a product-owner decision (v10.1.65, OBS-R2-15, decision ت۴۰). No file may gain one, a lower count fails until the baseline is lowered with `-- --update`, and a server file no rule of the map covers is refused, so a new folder gets its rule in the same change. Never raise the baseline.
- **ESLint Ratchet (v7.0.65, audit P3-4):** `npm run lint:eslint` (CI gate) runs ESLint (`eslint.config.js`: `no-floating-promises`, `no-misused-promises`, `no-explicit-any`, `react-hooks/rules-of-hooks`, `react-hooks/exhaustive-deps`, `max-lines` 400 / 300 for `.tsx`; not applied to the changelog data files `src/data/changelogs/*.ts` since v8.0.31) and fails when any rule count exceeds `eslint-baseline.json`. New code must not add violations; when a change removes violations, run `npm run lint:eslint -- --update` and commit the lower baseline. Never raise the baseline. `no-explicit-any` is counted per file in `eslint-any-baseline.json` (v7.0.106, TD-106, product-owner decision): no file may gain an `any`, a new file has none, an `any` silenced with `eslint-disable` still counts, and moving an `any` to another file fails even if the total stays the same. In the server money/stock paths (`MONEY_STOCK_PATHS` in `eslint.config.js`: accounting, treasury, documents, inventory, items, procurement, piecework, transfers, WooCommerce services and routes, `money` / `financialDecimal` / `voucherBalance` / `stockAvailability`) `no-explicit-any` is an `error` and the gate rejects it whatever the baseline, also when silenced with `eslint-disable` (v7.0.109). `no-floating-promises` is zero and an `error` everywhere since v8.0.46: mark an intentional fire-and-forget call with `void` (the callee handles its own errors) or handle the rejection; copy to the clipboard only through `copyToClipboard` (`src/utils/clipboard.ts`), which resolves to `false` on failure.

## 22. Database Migrations, Base Data at Boot & Pool Timeouts
- **Atomic Migrations (DB-013):** `src/db/migrator.ts` runs the official Drizzle migrator over `drizzle/*.sql` (journal: `drizzle/meta/_journal.json`) in an atomic transaction; applied migrations are tracked in `drizzle.__drizzle_migrations`. Every new migration MUST be registered in the journal. `runMigrations()` never throws: every caller MUST check `result.success` and stop on failure (v7.0.50, TD-217 — `setupTestSchema`, `ensureTestDatabaseReady`/`assertTestDatabaseReady`, `runSeed`, `server.ts`).
- **Migrations Name Their Schema (v9.0.425, TD-590):** A migration never drops a schema object by a bare name with `IF EXISTS`; it names the current schema (`EXECUTE format('DROP INDEX IF EXISTS %I.<name>', current_schema())`), because an isolated test schema runs the migrations with the search path `"<test schema>", public` and a bare name missing there dropped the object of the same name in `public`. An existence check restricts its own schema too (v9.0.426, TD-610): a catalog lookup carries its schema column or table (`connamespace = current_schema()::regnamespace`, `schemaname = current_schema()`, `table_schema = current_schema()`, `conrelid` / `tgrelid = '<table>'::regclass`) and `to_regclass` takes `format('%I.%I', current_schema(), '<name>')`, because the catalogs hold every schema and a bare check found the object in `public` or another test schema and skipped it. Vitest `migrationSchemaQualified.test.ts` (scanner `src/tests/setup/migrationSchemaQualification.ts`) fails on such a statement.
- **Conditional Constraints Are Reported and Built by Hand (v9.0.427, TD-589, product-owner decision ت۵):** A migration that leaves a constraint or index out on unclean data says so with `RAISE WARNING` (or a notice saying `SKIPPED`, `not created` or `left NOT VALID`); `runMigrations` listens to the notices of its connection and returns them in `warnings` and the log (`migrationNoticeWarning` in `src/db/migrationNotices.ts`). The FKs and unique indexes of 0001, 0012 and 0015 that are missing from the current schema are listed by the financial health check with their cause (`conditional_constraints_missing`, `CONDITIONAL_CONSTRAINT_RULES` in `src/services/system/conditionalConstraints.ts`), and the system admin builds them once the data is clean from «عملیات سامانه» (`GET /system/conditional-constraints`, `POST /system/conditional-constraints/build`: a preview unless `apply: true`, advisory lock `CONDITIONAL_CONSTRAINTS` 91040, one savepoint per constraint, audited with `tx`); data is never fixed automatically. A new conditional constraint without a health check of its own goes into that list.
- **Migration Journal and Session (v8.0.81 / v8.0.82, TD-364 / TD-366):** A new migration's journal `when` is greater than every earlier entry — the Drizzle migrator applies only entries newer than the database's last applied one and skips an older `when` silently. `runMigrations` compares the journal with `__drizzle_migrations` first (`planMigrations` in `src/db/migrationPlan.ts`) and refuses a skipped migration or a database newer than the build; Vitest `migrationPlan.test.ts` lints the journal (strictly increasing `when`, tag = idx, every `.sql` registered). Migrations run on one dedicated connection without the request statement timeout (`MIGRATION_STATEMENT_TIMEOUT`, default 0) under advisory lock 91008, so two runs queue instead of failing; a run waits at most `MIGRATION_LOCK_TIMEOUT` (default `10min`) for that lock and then stops with a clear error (v10.1.52, OBS-PR-10).
- **Released Migrations Stay as Released (v8.0.89, TD-368):** A migration file changes after release only to remove an upgrade blocker for databases that have not run it, or to name the current schema where it named an object without one (v9.0.425 / v9.0.426, TD-590 / TD-610); its previous sha256 goes into `AMENDED_MIGRATIONS` (`src/db/migrationAmendments.ts`) so databases that ran the old text get no hash warning. A data `UPDATE` in a migration runs through `erp_update_with_unvalidated_checks(table, sql)` (0047) when the table can hold NOT VALID CHECK constraints: they are dropped for the statement and re-added exactly as they were, because a NOT VALID CHECK still applies to every updated row and one refused legacy row would roll back the whole upgrade.
- **Backup, Restore and Upgrade Rehearsal (v8.0.83–v8.0.88, TD-360..TD-363, TD-365, TD-367):** `scripts/backup.sh` locates the app directory itself and reads `DATABASE_URL` / `ATTACHMENTS_DIR` from its `.env` (cron from any directory), writes the dump and a content manifest (`scripts/sql/backup-manifest.sql`: rows and content hash of every table, every constraint) from one exported snapshot, and archives `public/uploads` — attachment records without the attachment directory fail the backup. A failed run removes the partial files it wrote and a database before its first migration is backed up (v9.0.184, TD-606). Backups are private: the backup directory is `0700` and every file `0600` (`umask 077`), and `scripts/go-live-verify.sh` fails readable ones (v9.0.122, TD-585). `scripts/restore.sh` drill compares the restored database with that manifest; creating databases needs `RESTORE_ADMIN_URL` or `sudo -u postgres` (the install.sh role has no CREATEDB); `apply` never restores over the live database: it builds and verifies a new one, refuses while sessions are connected, swaps names and keeps `<db>_before_restore_<ts>` and the previous uploads directory. `update.sh` reports success only when `/health/startup` answers on the `PORT` of `.env` and the running version is the built one (`scripts/verify-startup.sh`); a failed build puts the previous `dist/` back without restarting, and any failure after the source changed prints the rollback steps once, warning that restoring the backup erases later writes (v9.0.123 / v9.0.124, TD-586 / TD-587). The rollback step resets the branch to the previous commit (`git reset --hard <commit>`), never a detached checkout (v9.0.181, TD-603) `update.sh` and `scripts/go-live-verify.sh` read `.env` literally, like the service (`node --env-file`), and never run it as shell code (v9.0.183, TD-605). The daily backup and the five-minute monitor run from one cron file written by `scripts/install-ops-cron.sh` (install.sh; v10.0.3, TD-1020); a daily backup is copied with the `.env` (and so `ERP_SECRETS_KEY`) only to an rclone `crypt` remote (`scripts/backup-offsite.sh`, `BACKUP_RCLONE_REMOTE`; a failed copy exits 3; v10.0.4, TD-957), and `scripts/monitor.sh` alerts on readiness, disk, backup age and the `erp_*` queue gauges of `/metrics` through a bot or SMTP, its Persian texts in `scripts/ops/monitor-messages.fa.txt` (v10.0.5 / v10.0.6, OBS-R2-11, TD-1021; `docs/OFFSITE_BACKUP.md`). The same cron file runs `scripts/nightly-verify.sh` each night at least 60 minutes after the backup (v10.2.16, O-04): it restores the dump `.last_daily_ok` names into an `erp_restore_drill_*` copy after a free-space check, runs the financial health check and the business invariants on it (`scripts/nightly-verify.ts`, read-only, refused on any other database) and compares them with the last healthy night (`src/lib/system/nightlyVerify.ts`: only a count that got worse fails; a check the baseline lacks is listed as new; the same worse counts for `NIGHTLY_VERIFY_ACCEPT_AFTER_NIGHTS` (3) nights, or `--accept-baseline`, become the new normal); the same dump checked again after 12 hours is `no_new_backup`; it writes `logs/nightly-verify.json`, read by the «بررسی شبانهٔ پشتیبان» card, sends no alert, never touches the live database, keeps the password only in a private pgpass file and the environment, and a re-run of `install-ops-cron.sh` keeps the times the cron file holds. `update.sh --rehearse` / `scripts/upgrade-rehearsal.sh` run the new migrations on a restored copy first (`src/db/upgradeRehearsal.ts`, only on `erp_restore_drill_*` databases) and stop before the restart when they fail or change ledger totals per account, stock per item and warehouse, bank balances or the financial health check.
- **Data Export (v9.0.386, TD-592, product-owner decision ت۷ of package 1):** `GET /api/export-backup` (system admin) streams a zip (`ZipStreamWriter` in `src/lib/zipStream.ts`, no extra dependency): one NDJSON file per table, read in primary-key batches of `DATA_EXPORT_BATCH_SIZE` rows with back-pressure (`DataExportService.writeExport`), and `manifest.json` with each table's row count; `activity_logs` only with `activityLogs=1` and a business-day range (`from` / `to`, both required, 400 otherwise; `dataExportQuerySchema`). The tables come from one list shared with the settings card (`DATA_EXPORT_TABLES` in `src/lib/system/dataExportTables.ts`, read by `DATA_EXPORT_TABLE_SOURCES` in `src/services/system/dataExportSources.ts`): every Drizzle table is exported, the optional `activity_logs`, or left out with a reason written to the manifest (`DATA_EXPORT_EXCLUDED_TABLES`), so a new table is classified in the same change (Vitest `dataExportTables.test.ts`), and the card lists the list's groups (v9.0.387, TD-624). It is never a restorable backup and carries no password hash, token state, Nobitex password, webhook signing key or secret setting. Every exported row passes `scrubExportRow` (`src/lib/system/exportSecretScrub.ts`): a secret-like key (password, secret, token, API key, authorization, cookie) holding text, at any depth of the row's JSON, and every header value under `headers` / `customHeaders` are written as «********», so a webhook rule's `actionConfigJson.secretToken` never leaves in clear (v10.1.5, TD-1251).
- **Foreign Keys of the Schema (v9.0.444, TD-612, product-owner decision ت۶ of package 1):** every reference declared with `references()` in the Drizzle schema has a database foreign key, except the columns listed with their reason in `FOREIGN_KEY_EXCEPTIONS` (`src/db/foreignKeyPolicy.ts`: step and action ids of an instance's own snapshot, the workflow engine's user ids, the TD-085 repair record). The regression test `reg_foreign_key_inventory_td_612` compares the schema with the migrated database; its list of references still without a constraint only shrinks, and a new reference gets its constraint in its own migration or an exception with its reason in the same change. A reader of a workflow step title uses the instance's snapshot (`snapshotStateTitles`), never the live step table, whose ids change on every design save. A new foreign key carries the `onDelete` its Drizzle declaration names, is added `NOT VALID`, is validated in its migration only when no row breaks it, and is registered with `addedNotValid` in `CONDITIONAL_CONSTRAINT_RULES` (`src/services/system/conditionalConstraints.ts`), so the health check lists it until the system admin validates it after fixing the data; old rows are never rewritten (v9.0.445, TD-611: `fk_webhook_deliveries_subscription` CASCADE, `fk_event_action_logs_rule` SET NULL). Deleting a webhook subscription or a rule (`src/services/events/integrationParentDelete.ts`) runs in one transaction under the parent row lock (404 when missing), cancels its queued delivery jobs and writes one audit row with `tx`. The user columns declared as references to users have their foreign keys (v9.0.446, TD-902, migration 0091: `form_drafts.user_id` CASCADE, the other fifteen NO ACTION); users are only soft-deleted, and code that hard-deletes a user (the factory reset, test cleanup) removes or clears the referencing rows first. The thirty references between business tables have their foreign keys too, NO ACTION as declared (v9.0.447, TD-903, migration 0092); their parents are only soft-deleted, and a new declared reference gets its key in its own migration (`reg_foreign_key_inventory_td_612` refuses a declared reference without one). The Drizzle schema says what the migrations build (v9.0.448, TD-613): each foreign key's `onDelete`, each column's nullability, each index by name (a unique index or NOT NULL a migration builds only on clean data included) and each unique constraint; `reg_schema_drift_td_613` refuses any difference, so a migration that adds or changes one of them changes the Drizzle declaration in the same change. A NOT NULL left out over NULL rows is the conditional kind `not_null` (migration 0093: four `project_product_stage_progress` columns). Every foreign key column has an index that leads with it (v9.0.449, TD-614, migration 0094), partial on `<column> IS NOT NULL` for a nullable column; `reg_foreign_key_indexes_td_614` refuses a new foreign key or declared reference whose column has none.
- **Base Data at Boot (DB-014):** The seed runs at every boot in every environment, production included (v9.0.134, TD-526, permission model decision ت۶; the old `ALLOW_SEED_IN_PRODUCTION` flag is retired and ignored), under advisory lock (`pg_try_advisory_lock(89345)`). It only inserts what is missing (v9.0.133, TD-591, package 1 decision ت۴): each kind of base data (the categories of `DEFAULT_CATEGORIES` in `src/data/defaultCategories.ts`, default settings, piecework tasks, the standard chart of accounts) is inserted only into a database that holds none of that kind, and no existing row (role permissions, category, account, setting) is ever edited, so nothing an admin removed or changed comes back. It creates no role: a fresh install has only the system admin role (migration 0066), and every other role is made by the admin with «ساخت نقش از الگو» from `ROLE_TEMPLATES` (`src/lib/permissions/roleTemplates.ts`; a template only fills the role form, and its permission list is closed under `requires`); test schemas get the template roles from the test bootstrap. `GET /categories` only reads. The boot data steps live in `prepareDatabaseAtBoot` (`src/services/system/bootData.ts`); its default workflows, event rules and webhook subscriptions (`seedDefaultEngines`, each only when missing) also run after a factory reset, which wipes them and keeps the roles, and the reset card says so (`src/lib/system/factoryReset.ts`; v9.0.390, TD-620).
- **Database Mode Decided Once, Never a Silent Demo (v9.0.428, TD-616, product-owner decision ت۴):** `resolveDatabaseMode` (`src/db/databaseMode.ts`) decides the process's database once: `SQL_HOST` with `SQL_USER` / `SQL_PASSWORD` / `SQL_DB_NAME`, else a real `DATABASE_URL` (not empty, not the `.env.example` placeholder); the in-memory demo database (`mockPool`, admin / admin) runs only with `ERP_DEMO_MODE=1` outside production. Anything else is refused: `server.ts` logs the reason and exits 1 before listening, every query rejects with it, and a pool error never falls back to the demo database. `/health/ready` says which database answers (`database`), and the test runner accepts only PostgreSQL.
- **The Boot Never Touches Passwords (v9.0.429, TD-617, product-owner decision ت۴):** a stored password that is not a bcrypt hash never logs in and no boot step rewrites it. The one-off `npm run users:lock-plain-passwords` (`lockNonBcryptPasswords` in `src/services/users/plainPasswordLock.ts`) lists such users by the kind of value (a preview by default) and with `--apply` replaces each value, deleted users included, with `PLAIN_PASSWORD_LOCK_MARKER`, sets `must_reset_password = 1`, advances `token_version` and writes one audit row per user without the value, in one transaction under the user row locks; the system admin then gives the user a temporary password.
- **Session-Level Timeouts (DB-012):** `statement_timeout = 60000` ms and `idle_in_transaction_session_timeout = 60000` ms (defaults of `DB_STATEMENT_TIMEOUT` / `DB_IDLE_IN_TX_TIMEOUT`), sent in the pool's connection startup `options` (v7.0.39). A bulk task extends the statement timeout of its own transaction with `extendStatementTimeout(tx)` (`src/db/drizzle.ts`, `SET LOCAL`, 5 minutes; v9.0.188, TD-615), never on another pool connection. The same `options` pin the session `TimeZone=UTC`, and `src/lib/processTimezone.ts` (the first import of `server.ts` and the test runner) sets the process `TZ=UTC`, so server timestamps (`defaultNow()`, `toISOString()`, stored without a zone) are UTC on every host; business «today» still comes only from `businessClock` (v8.0.52, TD-314).
- **Single-Instance Deployment (v7.0.44, product-owner decision):** The system runs as ONE server process (one workshop, at most 20 concurrent users). In-memory caches, rate limiters and process-level locks assume a single process; do not add multi-replica infrastructure (Redis, object storage, cross-instance cache invalidation). The Kubernetes manifest (`deploy/k8s/erp-deployment.yaml`) is an unsupported example, not tested or kept in step with the app (no `ERP_SECRETS_KEY`, backup path or `securityContext`); the supported install is `install.sh` on one Linux server. It keeps `replicas: 1` with `strategy: Recreate` and no autoscaler, and its image tag and `APP_VERSION` follow the release (§23) (v10.1.65, OBS-R1-15, decision ت۴۰).

## 23. V10 Governance — Active Series, Series 9 Debt, Observations & Improvement Proposals
- **Active Series (v10.x.y):** The active changelog file is `src/data/changelogs/10.ts` (`v10.x.y`, from v10.0.0). Historical changelogs reside in `0.ts` (`v1.0.0`), `archive_1_6.ts` (condensed `v1.x.y`–`v6.x.y`, finalized at `v6.0.28`), `7.ts` (`v7.x.y`, closed and frozen at `v7.0.140`), `8.ts` (`v8.x.y`, closed and frozen at `v8.0.128`) and `9.ts` (`v9.x.y`, closed and frozen at `v9.0.461`). Every change MUST append one unique, short `AIUpdateLog` entry (§7) to `src/data/changelogs/10.ts` and bump `package.json` `"version"`.
- **Core Mission of Version 10 (`V10_MASTER_ROADMAP.md`, product-owner decision of the phase 5 review):** Start from what series 9 left open: the open debt rows of `TECH_DEBT.md` (84 at v10.0.0: 41 open phase 5 findings between TD-909 and TD-956, 26 P2 observations TD-957..TD-982 and 17 observation ledger rows TD-983..TD-999), the observation ledger `docs/audit/V9_OBSERVATIONS.md`, the two unmet V9 targets (writes from routes, `max-lines`), ratchets for the four phase 2 architecture decisions, and the 27 improvement and development items the product owner chose on 16 Mehr 1405 (22 of the 31 phase 5 proposals and 5 new needs, in phase order: section «پیشنهادهای انتخاب‌شده» of the roadmap; on 19 Mehr 1405 the product owner deferred D-04, D-07, O-04 and O-06 to the next plan, listed in its section «موکول به برنامه بهبود و توسعه بعدی»; on the same day (decision ت۲۳) the product owner chose to close 10.0 early and moved D-03, D-06, D-15, D-16, N-01, N-02, N-03, N-05 part 4, I-04, I-07 and most P3 work to 10.1, listed in its section «منتقل به نسخه ۱۰.۱»; decision ت۲۴, left unanswered, took its recommended option and kept TD-1244 (the eidi and prepayment payslip voucher dated on its issue day) in 10.0; electronic tax invoicing is not needed and is not planned). On 22 Mehr 1405 (decision ت۲۵) the product owner started 10.1 (v10.1.0): every later change is a `v10.1.x` release, and the plan is the roadmap section «برنامه آغاز نسخه ۱۰.۱», which adds N-06 (a production receipt without a project, shipped in v10.1.6, TD-1262; §3). On 27 Mehr 1405 (decision ت۴۱) the product owner approved the 10.2 plan and started 10.2 (v10.2.0): every later change is a `v10.2.x` release, and the plan is the roadmap section «برنامه آغاز نسخه ۱۰.۲» (production project control first, then payroll payments through the shared treasury path, the online shop and the other packages). Stability over rewrites, with the V9 method unchanged: every finding proven by a test that fails first (or a ratchet that only accepts decreases, run by a Vitest test), its own `TD-###` row (new rows from TD-1000) and its own `v10.0.x` release, package by package, severity P0–P3; observations without proof get no row (the one exception is the series 9 ledger below); a recommended option written in a row is not a decision until the product owner confirms it (open decisions ت۴..ت۱۱ in `V10_MASTER_ROADMAP.md`).
- **Version 9 Observation Ledger (v10.0.0, product-owner decision ت۱۲ ب of the phase 5 review):** The only written exception to «observations without proof get no row», and it covers exactly the 43 rows TD-957..TD-999. The 26 P2 observations of the phase 5 review have one row each, TD-957..TD-982 (one not yet proven says «مشاهده؛ پیش از رفع اثبات شود»); the 204 P3 observations live in the ledger `docs/audit/V9_OBSERVATIONS.md` (each with its id, package, text and evidence), with one row per package naming its ledger ids: TD-983 (package 1) … TD-998 (package 16) and TD-999 for tests and tooling (T). An observation found later still gets no row until it is proven. Each one is proven by a test that fails on the code before its fix (or by a ratchet that only accepts decreases) and is fixed in its own release, which marks its ledger entry «✅ v10.0.x»; a package row is closed only when every id it names is fixed or closed by a product-owner decision.
- **Version 9 Mission (closed at v9.0.461 — complete, and its rules remain binding; closing report in `V9_MASTER_ROADMAP.md`):** Complete V8, not repeat it: audit and stabilize the system package by package (16 packages) on architecture and layering (route → service → db), data integrity, concurrency, security, error handling, performance, code quality, tests and UI — first the modules V8 had no area for (CRM and customers, daily logs and attachments, items and pricing, dashboard and shell, personnel). Stability over rewrites. Every proven finding gets a failing test first (or a ratchet that only accepts decreases, run by a Vitest test), a `TD-###` row (from TD-414) and its own `v9.0.x` release; observations without proof get no row; policy changes wait for a product-owner decision. Severity P0–P3, finding ids, package score and exit targets are defined in `V9_MASTER_ROADMAP.md`; the phase 5 review of the sales, purchase, project production and payroll flows is section 18 of `docs/audit/STABILITY_AUDIT_V9.md`.
- **Version 8 Mission (closed at v8.0.128 — complete, and its rules remain binding; closing report in `V8_MASTER_ROADMAP.md`):** Prove or refute the business-logic correctness of inventory, costing, accounting, treasury, payroll and the other domains with executable invariants (balanced vouchers, three-way stock, stock value = inventory ledger accounts, one voucher per final document, exact round-trip on void, fiscal-year closing) and a seeded "one business year" simulation on real PostgreSQL 16. Every finding is reproduced by a failing test first, recorded as a `TD-###` row, and fixed in its own `v8.0.x` release; policy changes wait for a product-owner decision. The invariants suite (`business_invariants`) and `knownFindings.ts` stay binding in the 10.x series.
- **Version 7 Missions (closed at v7.0.140 — complete, and their rules remain binding; closing report in `V7_MASTER_ROADMAP.md`):**
  1. **Strict TypeScript & Type Safety (فعال‌سازی Strict Mode):** `strict: true` is on (v7.0.79, audit P3-3 — catch variables are `unknown`: read them with `errorMessageOf(err)` / `getErrorMessage(err)` from `src/utils`, never `err.message`); untyped `: any` escape hatches are removed incrementally (ESLint ratchet).
  2. **100% Zod Validation Coverage (اعتبارسنجی کامل روت‌ها):** Complete Zod middleware protection on all remaining unvalidated endpoints (Procurement, Dashboard, Transactions) preventing NaN/malformed inputs.
  3. **N+1 Performance Elimination (حذف کوری‌های متوالی در لوپ‌ها):** Batch fetching of warehouse locations and item base prices in document finalization and audit loops.
  4. **Database Migrator Hardening (استحکام مایگریتور):** Formalization of all ad-hoc DDL into numbered Drizzle migrations and fail-fast startup behavior in production.
  5. **Data Model Normalization (نرمال‌سازی انبار):** Migration of per-warehouse inventory quantities from JSONB blob into a normalized `item_warehouse_stocks` table with database-level constraints.
- **CHANGELOG.md Archive:** Condensed markdown archive of major series milestones lives in root `CHANGELOG.md`.
- **Technical Debt Registry (TECH_DEBT.md):** ANY shortcut, workaround, or known issue must be registered with unique `TD-###` in `TECH_DEBT.md`. Resolving a debt requires updating its status in the same change-set.
- **Data-Safety Invariant:** Test cleanup gated on `NODE_ENV ∈ {test,development}` AND `ERP_ALLOW_TEST_CLEANUP=1`, scoped to synthetic test IDs only. `npm run db:cleanup-test` checks both before connecting, previews by default (rolled back) and deletes only with `--force`, and then only `ERP-TEST-MARKER` rows nothing else refers to; it never touches Kardex, treasury, users, audit logs, counters or sequences (v9.0.121, TD-581). `db:push` is BANNED; migrations come exclusively from the atomic migrator. `drizzle.config.ts` refuses every drizzle-kit command except `studio`, `check` and `export`, which only prints the schema's SQL (`drizzleKitCommandRefusal` in `scripts/drizzleKitGuard.ts`; v10.1.56, OBS-R1-26; `export` since v10.1.66).
- **Business Clock Invariant:** Server-side dates must use `src/lib/businessClock.ts`.
- **Local Dev & MCP References:**
  - Windows local PostgreSQL & portable `.pgdata` lifecycle: see `docs/LOCAL_DEV_WINDOWS.md`.
  - DBHub read-only MCP configuration: see `docs/LOCAL_MCP_TOOLING.md`.
- **Release Paperwork (v8.0.45, generic since v9.0.0):** `npm run release -- <spec.json>` (`scripts/release.ts`, spec fields documented at its top; series and file come from `ACTIVE_CHANGELOG`) writes one release's paperwork: the active changelog entry, the CHANGELOG.md line, the four version locations, the closed TD rows (`closes[]`) moved to the active series section of `TECH_DEBT_ARCHIVE.md` with the counts and «آخرین بازبینی», new open rows of a documentation release (`openRows[]`), the fixed known-findings classes replaced by a «رفع شد» comment, and exact-text edits of the roadmap, audit report and AGENTS.md; nothing is written when any step fails; then run `npm run check:version`. Covered by Vitest `releaseTool.test.ts`. Parallel lanes take versions, migration numbers and audit report section numbers at merge time: `npm run release:renumber -- origin/master` (`scripts/release-renumber.ts`) merges the base without committing, resolves the conflicts of the release files (changelogs, TECH_DEBT, migration journal, the four version locations; any other conflict is resolved by hand and the command run again), moves the branch's own ones after the base's, rewriting only lines the branch added (comment lines outside Markdown), stages the result and runs `check:version` and the migration plan test (v9.0.109, TD-473; Vitest `releaseRenumber.test.ts`).
- **Release Version Bump = 4 Synced Locations (enforced by `npm run check:version`):**
  1. `"version"` in `package.json` (single source of truth; dynamically resolved by `src/lib/version.ts` and `/health`).
  2. Top entry in active changelog `src/data/changelogs/10.ts` (`ACTIVE_CHANGELOG` in `src/data/changelogs/index.ts`).
  3. `deploy/k8s/erp-deployment.yaml` — both the image tag (`erp:vX.Y.Z`) and the `APP_VERSION` env value (it overrides `package.json` at runtime).
  4. Header line of `README.md` (`نسخه مستقر: \`vX.Y.Z\``).

## 24. GitHub Sync Policy
- **Commit & Push (v9.0.0, product-owner decision; branch prefix `v10/` since v10.0.0):** Commit with descriptive messages on a separate branch (`v10/<phase or package>-<topic>`) and open a draft pull request; never push directly to `master` — merging is the product owner's step. In local development the same applies whenever Git credentials allow a push.
- **Cloud & Sandbox Environments:** In environments without Git credentials, avoid executing failing push commands that interrupt workflow.
- **Exclusions:** Never commit `.env`, local database directories (`/pgdata`, `/logs`), or source archives.
- **Vendored SheetJS (v7.0.58, audit P2-13 / TD-173, product-owner decision):** `xlsx` (a devDependency since v7.0.84) is installed from `vendor/xlsx-0.20.3.tgz` (the official SheetJS build, published only on cdn.sheetjs.com — the npm `xlsx@0.18.5` is vulnerable). This tarball is the one allowed third-party archive in the repository. Upgrade only by replacing it with a newer official tarball and updating the `package.json` reference and lockfile; never switch back to the npm package.
- **Runtime vs Dev Dependencies (v7.0.84, TD-177):** `dependencies` holds exactly the packages the server bundle requires (the Docker image installs only these with `npm ci --omit=dev`); frontend-only libraries (React, icons, fonts, xlsx …) go in `devDependencies`. Enforced by `npm run check:runtime-deps` and Vitest `runtimeDependencies.test.ts`. The audit gate (`npm run audit:gate`) reads `npm audit` over all dependencies so browser libraries stay covered, and fails when `npm audit` itself fails (v9.0.185, TD-607).
- **Conflict Handling:** Always use `git pull --rebase` if remote has progressed; never force-push.
